### [1] [Spectral-Target Physical Latent Structuring for JEPA-Style World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04264)
*Penghao Zhu,Salvatore Penachio,Kaustav Mukherjee,Aneesh Jonelagadda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对潜在世界模型（Latent World Models）在动态环境中出现的"物理表示懒惰"（physical representation laziness）新失效模式，提出了一种轻量级"傅里叶辅助头"（Fourier auxiliary head）训练时辅助监督方法。该方法通过对潜在空间施加物理信息结构化约束，在不增加推理成本的前提下显著提升动态环境中的规划成功率，同时在低数据条件下改善数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型（如LeWorldModel）使用正则化技术（如SIGReg）防止表示坍缩，但即使表示没有坍缩，在高度动态环境中仍可能出现新失效模式——物理表示懒惰，即潜在状态无法表征关键的物理属性，导致后续规划频繁失败。现有方法无法解决这一问题。

Method: 提出在训练阶段加入一个轻量级"傅里叶辅助头"（Fourier auxiliary head），通过辅助监督对潜在空间施加物理信息结构化约束，使潜在状态携带关键物理属性信息。该辅助头在推理阶段可完全去除，不增加推理成本，且可泛化到任意环境。

Result: 实验表明：1）在基线LeWM表现出物理表示懒惰的动态环境中，辅助头显著提升规划成功率；2）在基线未表现出懒惰的环境中也有适度改进；3）规划性能的提升伴随着潜在空间与关键物理属性之间相关性的提高，验证了方法对潜在空间的物理结构化能力；4）在低数据场景下辅助监督对成功率提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 傅里叶辅助头方法能有效避免潜在世界模型中的物理表示懒惰问题，同时提升整体成功率和数据效率，为潜在世界模型在动态环境中的物理可解释性和规划可靠性提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Latent world models have become increasingly popular as a method to predict and plan in latent space rather than pixel space. Recent architectures, such as LeWorldModel (LeWM), jointly train the encoder and predictor using regularization techniques like SIGReg to prevent representation collapse. Even with such regularization preventing representation collapse, we identify a new world model failure mode of \textit{physical representation laziness}, particularly noted in highly dynamic environments. For these lazy cases, the learned latent states do not collapse but nonetheless fail to represent key physical properties, causing ubiquitous downstream planning failure. To resolve this issue, we propose training-time auxiliary supervision with a lightweight "Fourier auxiliary head", which enforces physically-informed structuring of the latent space with no additional inference-time cost and can be generalized to any environment. Experimentally, we show that the auxiliary head substantially improves planning success rates in dynamic environments where the baseline LeWM exhibits physical representation laziness. It also leads to modest improvements in other environments, even when the baseline does not exhibit physical representation laziness. We further observe superior planning performance being accompanied by higher latent space correlations with key physical properties, indicating both the ability of our method to physically structure latent states and the potential planning-side benefit to the learned representation being physically structured. We also see in low-data regimes, auxiliary supervision is particularly impactful in increasing success rate. These findings support the use of our Fourier auxiliary head method to improve both overall success rate and data efficiency, while avoiding representation laziness in latent world models.

</details>


### [2] [An Energy-Based Conservative-Dissipative Latent Neural Evolution Operator for Magnetization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04530)
*Sebastian Schaffer,Lukas Exl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于能量的磁化动力学降阶模型，将卷积自编码器与结构化的潜在神经ODE耦合。潜在向量场由学习到的标量势的梯度通过反对称算子与对称半正定耗散算子生成，严格遵从LLG方程的进动-耗散结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微磁求解器对轨迹预测的计算成本过高，而现有降阶模型往往忽略或未充分利用Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程的内在几何结构（预先-耗散结构），导致预测精度和泛化能力受限。

Method: 将卷积自编码器与结构化潜在神经普通微分方程耦合。潜在向量场由学习的标量势的梯度经反对称算子（进动项）与对称半正定耗散算子生成。编码器、解码器、潜在能量及算子通过在短轨迹窗口上的潜在损失和解码回放损失联合训练，无需时间导数监督、物理能量标签或耗散惩罚。推理时编码一次，潜在空间演化，仅在请求时刻解码。

Result: 在NIST μMAG标准问题4的两个外加场方向数据集上，比较了二次、深度和加性深度-二次潜在能量。耗散-only与反对称-耗散模型在短训练风格窗口上精度相当，但在不间断rollout中差异显著——反对称-耗散模型的轨迹预测明显更准确。深度-二次能量在两个场方向上都取得最佳精度，且在扩展至两倍训练时域时误差增长更慢。

Conclusion: 融入LLG方程进动-耗散结构（反对称+耗散算子分解）能够显著提升长时程轨迹预测的准确性和泛化能力，且深度-二次潜在能量形式在本任务中表现最优，验证了物理启发式结构归纳偏置在磁化动力学降阶建模中的价值。

Abstract: We develop an energy-based reduced-order model for micromagnetic magnetization dynamics that couples a convolutional autoencoder to a structured latent neural ordinary differential equation. Motivated by the precessional-dissipative structure of the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation, the latent vector field is generated from the gradient of a learned scalar potential through an antisymmetric operator and a symmetric positive-semidefinite dissipative operator. This potential is learned in nonunique latent coordinates and is not identified with the Gibbs free energy, but decreases monotonically along autonomous continuous-time solutions, while the antisymmetric component permits motion along its level sets. The encoder, decoder, latent energy, and operators are trained jointly on short trajectory windows using latent and decoded-rollout losses alone, without time-derivative supervision, physical-energy labels, or dissipation penalties. At inference, an initial state is encoded once, evolved in latent space, and decoded only at the requested output times, enabling substantially cheaper trajectory prediction than the micromagnetic solver used to generate the training data. We compare quadratic, deep, and additive deep-quadratic latent energies on two datasets parameterized by field amplitude and generated for the two applied-field directions of the NIST $μ$MAG Standard Problem 4. Dissipative-only and antisymmetric-dissipative models achieve comparable accuracy on short training-style windows but differ substantially on uninterrupted rollouts, for which the antisymmetric-dissipative models provide markedly more accurate trajectory predictions. The deep-quadratic energy gives the best overall accuracy for both field directions and exhibits slower error growth when rollouts are extended to twice the training horizon.

</details>


### [3] [ProToMEx: Rapid, Interpretable Explanations via Structured Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04265)
*Athina Georgara,Adarsh Valoor,Sarvapali D. Ramchurn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProToMEx，一种基于概率主题模型（PTMs）的新型可解释性框架，超越了传统的特征归因方法，通过学习隐藏的"主题"来揭示模型决策背后的高层语义结构。该框架同时支持全局和局部解释，且相比SHAP和LIME，在保持可比较保真度的同时，将局部解释的生成成本降低约30-40倍，适合实时应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的事后可解释性方法（如SHAP、LIME）主要关注特征归因，即对单个特征分配重要性分数。然而，模型决策往往由复杂的组合模式驱动，传统方法难以表达这类组合性和语义性的决策逻辑，因此需要一种能超越简单特征重要性、揭示潜在语义结构的新解释范式。

Method: ProToMEx利用概率主题模型（PTMs）学习潜在"主题"，每个主题代表一个分类的独立高层原因。该框架是模型无关的（model-agnostic），能够自然地提供全局解释（模型整体行为）和局部解释（分解特定预测中的多个共存原因）。

Result: 实验表明，ProToMEx产生的解释在保真度上与SHAP和LIME相当，但在标准表格数据集和合成数据集上，生成局部解释的平均计算成本比SHAP和LIME快约30-40倍，适合实时应用场景。

Conclusion: ProToMEx代表了一种超越特征归因的可解释性新范式，通过主题建模捕捉决策的语义结构，在保持解释质量的同时大幅提升效率，为实时可解释AI系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing post-hoc explainers for machine learning classifiers primarily focus on feature attribution, assigning importance scores to individual features. While valuable, this approach struggles to articulate the complex, combinatorial patterns that often drive a model's decision-making process. To overcome this limitation, we introduce ProToMEx, a new paradigm for explainability that leverages Probabilistic Topic Models (PTMs). Our model-agnostic framework learns latent ''topics'' that represent distinct, high-level reasons for a classification, moving beyond simple feature importance to reveal underlying semantic structures. ProToMEx naturally provides both global explanations of a model's overall behaviour and local explanations that can disentangle multiple co-existing reasons for a specific prediction. We demonstrate empirically that ProToMEx not only produces explanations of comparable fidelity to popular methods like SHAP and LIME but also drastically reduces the amortised computational cost of generating local explanations, making it highly suitable for real-time applications. Specifically, we show that ProToMEx is ~30-40x faster than SHAP and LIME over standardised tabular datasets and synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [4] [A Data Fusion Framework for Grounding Aerospace Surrogate Model via Experimental Wind-Tunnel Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04267)
*Nitin Nagesh Kulkarni,Dheeraj Vemula,Yin Yu,Peter Lyu,Juan J. Alonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实验校正框架，利用风洞PSP（压敏涂料）测量数据对基于CFD训练的深度学习气动代理模型进行校准。Geotransolver代理模型在2,300个高保真CFD仿真（NASA CRM翼身组合体，覆盖几何变化、马赫数0.70-0.85、攻角0-4度）上训练，R2>0.99，但与实验数据存在系统偏差。通过训练一个校正网络学习代理预测与实验测得的表面压力分布之间的差异，在未重训原代理模型的情况下，显著改善了代理模型与实验的一致性，尤其在马赫0.85时对吸力峰、激波位置及压力恢复有大幅改进。在保留角度上，校正后代理与测量的误差在测量Cp范围的2.3%-2.7%以内，优于直接插值方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真CFD训练的深度学习气动代理虽能准确复现CFD数值预测（包括标量输出和全场数据），但其预测保真度受限于CFD与实验观测之间的系统偏差。仅依赖CFD数据的代理模型无法直接与实验一致，需要一种在不大幅增加成本的前提下将实验信息融入代理模型的机制，以弥合仿真与真实物理之间的差距。

Method: 1) 使用Geotransolver深度学习代理模型，在2,300个NASA CRM翼身组合体的高保真CFD仿真数据上训练，覆盖几何变化、Mach 0.70-0.85和攻角0-4度，复现CFD气动力与俯仰力矩R2>0.99。2) 不重训原代理模型，而是额外训练一个校正网络，其在两个自由流马赫数（0.70和0.85）的同攻角范围内，利用空间配准的PSP测量数据学习代理预测与实验测得的表面压力分布之间的系统差异。3) 校正网络在有限实验数据集上学习偏差模式，保留预训练代理参数不变。

Result: 在马赫0.85时，校正显著改善了与PSP数据的一致性，特别是翼面吸力峰、激波位置及后续压力恢复，同时减小了预测误差的幅值和Cp误差超过0.05的润湿表面比例。在保留的攻角上，校正后代理与测量的误差在测量Cp范围的2.3%-2.7%以内，并在所有测试状态下优于在测量条件间直接插值的方法。

Conclusion: 实验测量可以通过学习系统的CFD与实验差异来"落地"大规模仿真训练的代理模型，同时保留其泛化能力和计算效率。该方法在无需重训庞大CFD代理模型的前提下，用少量实验数据实现从"仿真准确"到"实测准确"的跨越，为多保真度数据的有效融合提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Aerodynamic surrogate models trained on high-fidelity CFD data reproduce numerical predictions of both scalar outputs and entire fields accurately, yet their predictive fidelity is limited by systematic discrepancies between CFD and experimental observations. We present an experimentally grounded correction framework that adapts a CFD-trained deep learning surrogate using wind-tunnel PSP measurements. A Geotransolver surrogate trained on 2,300 high-fidelity CFD simulations of the NASA CRM wing-body configuration, spanning geometric variation, Mach 0.70-0.85, and angles of attack 0 to 4 degrees, reproduces the CFD integrated aerodynamic forces and pitching moment to R2 > 0.99 but does not match the experimental data. To incorporate experimental information without retraining the surrogate, a correction network is trained on spatially registered PSP measurements at two freestream Mach numbers (0.70 and 0.85) across the same angle-of-attack range, learning the discrepancy between the surrogate-predicted and experimentally measured surface-pressure distributions. At Mach 0.85 the correction substantially improves agreement with PSP, particularly at the wing suction peak, shock location, and subsequent pressure recovery, reducing both the magnitude of the prediction error and the fraction of wetted surface on which it exceeds 0.05 in Cp, and it does so from a limited experimental dataset without modifying the pretrained surrogate parameters. On held-out angles of attack the grounded surrogate agrees with measurement to within 2.3-2.7% of the measured Cp range, and outperforms direct interpolation between the measured conditions at every state tested. Experimental measurements can therefore ground a large-scale simulation-trained surrogate by learning systematic CFD-to-experiment discrepancies while preserving its generalization capability and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [5] [Quantum-Assisted Memory-Efficient Training for Parameter-Intensive Wi-Fi-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04271)
*To Truong An,Jie Zhang,Guolin Yin,Junqing Zhang,Yanjiao Li,Trung Q. Duong,Simon L. Cotton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-MET框架，利用混合量子-经典神经网络间接生成Wi-Fi人体活动识别（HAR）模型参数，结合训练阶段的结构化剪枝，显著降低训练和推理的计算与内存开销，实现了90%-95%的可训练参数缩减与75%-85%的模型稀疏度，且分类精度几乎不损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Wi-Fi的HAR系统大多依赖深度学习模型，这些模型在训练和推理阶段计算密集、内存开销巨大，难以在资源受限的现实场景中部署。传统训练需要同时更新数百万参数，导致内存消耗难以接受，亟需一种更高效的训练与推理方案。

Method: Q-MET采用混合量子-经典神经网络，通过间接生成HAR模型参数替代直接优化，大幅减少可训练参数数量；同时，在训练阶段引入结构化剪枝，进一步支持轻量化推理，使模型适用于资源受限设备。

Result: 实验表明，与传统基于反向传播的深度学习训练相比，Q-MET可减少90%-95%的可训练参数，同时保持甚至超越经典分类精度；通过结构化剪枝实现75%-85%的模型稀疏度，分类精度损失小于2%。

Conclusion: Q-MET是首个同时解决HAR系统训练与推理阶段内存效率问题的量子辅助方法，为智能通信与感知融合场景下的高效HAR部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Wi-Fi-based human activity recognition (HAR) has become an important part of integrated sensing and communications, paving the way for a range of context-aware services. However, most existing Wi-Fi-based HAR systems rely on deep learning (DL) models that are computationally and memory intensive in both training and inference, which poses significant challenges for real-world deployment. Conventional training requires simultaneous updates of millions of parameters, leading to prohibitive memory consumption. In this paper, we propose a novel quantum-assisted memory-efficient training framework (Q-MET) designed to improve efficiency in both training and inference. Q-MET utilizes a hybrid quantum classical neural network to indirectly generate parameters for HAR models, significantly reducing the trainable parameter count compared to direct optimization. To further support the deployment on resource-constrained devices, we integrate structured pruning during the training phase. Experimental results demonstrate that Q-MET achieves a 90% to 95% reduction in trainable parameters compared with conventional backpropagation-based DL training while maintaining or even exceeding classical classification accuracy. Additionally, Q-MET supports lightweight inference through structured pruning, achieving 75% to 85% model sparsity with less than 2% loss in classification accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this work represents the first quantum-assisted approach to simultaneously tackle memory inefficiencies in both the training and inference stages of HAR systems.

</details>


### [6] [Interpretability for Turing Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04661)
*Billy Snikkers,Rumi Salazar,Daniel Murfet,Will Troiani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明可通过敏感性（susceptibilities）这一神经网络可解释性技术，通过探测嘈杂图灵机学习问题的局部损失地形，来识别图灵机中的算法结构。作者证明算法中的对称性和路径分离会诱发敏感矩阵中的置换对称和低秩块，并在确定性有限自动机（DFA）上实证验证了可通过PCA和聚类在敏感空间中恢复算法特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的可解释性技术通常主要用于理解网络内部表征，但能否通过可解释性工具揭示底层算法或计算结构仍是开放问题。作者希望利用敏感性分析来探测学习问题的损失地形，从而识别算法结构中内在的对称性等组织化特征。

Method: 理论层面：作者证明了图灵机实现算法中的置换对称性会诱导敏感性矩阵中的置换对称性，路径分离则导致低秩块结构。实证层面：在Murfet和Troiani提出的嘈杂图灵机学习问题框架下，对一组确定性有限自动机（DFAs）进行敏感性计算，并使用主成分分析（PCA）和聚类方法在敏感性空间中对算法特征进行恢复和分析。

Result: 在DFA数据集上的实验表明，算法特征（如对称性和路径分离）可以通过敏感性矩阵结合PCA和聚类方法被有效地恢复和识别，验证了理论预测的可行性。

Conclusion: 敏感性作为可解释性技术能够从神经网络的局部损失地形中揭示底层图灵机的算法结构，为理解神经网络的算法性质提供了新的分析工具和理论保证。

Abstract: We show that susceptibilities, an interpretability technique developed for neural networks, can identify the presence of algorithmic structure in Turing machines by probing the local loss landscape of a learning problem for noisy Turing machines introduced by Murfet and Troiani (arXiv:2504.08075). We prove that symmetries and path separation in the algorithm implemented by a Turing machine induce permutation symmetries and low-rank blocks in its susceptibility matrix. We study this empirically on a set of deterministic finite automata (DFAs) and demonstrate that algorithmic features can be recovered by principal component analysis and clustering methods in susceptibility space.

</details>


### [7] [Variational Continuation for Double Pendulum Periodic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05337)
*Leo Yao,Ziming Liu,Max Tegmark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于Hessian矩阵（黑塞矩阵）的数值延拓方法，用于在动力系统中连续追踪周期轨道。方法将周期轨道参数化为傅里叶级数，利用自动微分替代手工推导的雅可比矩阵，通过损失函数景观的平坦方向（零特征值方向）确定延拓方向，实现无积分器、高效且引导式的周期轨道搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往数值延拓周期轨道的方法依赖于手工推导的雅可比矩阵，过程繁琐且难以自动化。作者希望利用机器学习中成熟的自动微分技术来简化该过程，并通过损失函数景观的几何结构（零特征值对应的平坦方向）来引导延拓搜索，使周期轨道的探索更加高效、自动化且无需积分器。

Method: 周期轨道候选以傅里叶级数参数化；基于环路偏离物理微分方程的程度定义损失函数。利用自动微分自动计算梯度与Hessian矩阵，绕过了传统手工推导Jacobian的繁琐步骤。延拓方向由损失函数景观中的零特征值（平坦）方向确定，从而实现引导式的轨道族搜索。该方法无需积分器，可精确定初始化不稳定固定点附近的振动，并有效检测轨道族交叉与次谐波分岔。

Result: 以双摆系统为演示，完整实现了从固定点出发的周期振动延拓，展示了轨道族沿程分岔现象并对周期轨道分支进行了分类。尤其发现了两摆质量从不同时处于静止状态的周期轨道，该发现据作者所述在现有文献中尚属缺失。

Conclusion: 基于Hessian与自动微分的延拓方法能够高效、自动化地搜索和延拓动力系统中的周期轨道族，无需积分器，并能探测分岔与轨道族间的交叉关系。该方法在双摆系统中发现了新的周期轨道类型，展示了其发现新物理现象的潜力。

Abstract: We present a Hessian-based approach to numerically continue periodic orbits in dynamical systems. A loop (periodic orbit candidate) is parametrized as a Fourier series; a loss function is defined based on the deviation of the loop from the physical differential equations. Unlike previous work relying on hand-derived Jacobians, our method automates the process by leveraging automatic differentiation, a common machine learning technique. The continuation direction can be determined by the flat directions of the loss landscapes (directions with zero eigenvalues), making the search of periodic orbits efficient and guided. Our method is integrator-free, precisely initializes oscillations around unstable fixed points, and efficiently detects orbit family intersections and subharmonic bifurcations. As a demonstration, we present full continuations of periodic double pendulum oscillations from fixed points, showing bifurcations along orbit families and categorizing branches of periodic orbits. In particular, we find periodic orbits where both pendulum masses are never simultaneously at rest, which to our knowledge has been missing in the literature.

</details>


### [8] [Evaluating Large Language Models for Forced Outage Risk Prediction: Benefits and Comparison to Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04272)
*Christos Petridis,Zoran Obradovic,Mladen Kezunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在零样本框架下（无需标注训练数据）预测配电网天气相关强迫停电风险的能力。实验基于德克萨斯州中部某公用事业服务区域六年停电记录和高分辨率天气数据，将问题构建为三个预测时域（3h、6h、12h）的二元严重度分类任务，对四个零样本LLM和两个监督分类器在两种输入配置（当前天气观测vs天气预报数据）下进行基准测试。结果表明监督模型在macro-F1和精确率上优于LLM，但较新世代的LLM能达到接近的分数。LLM在可操作推理和地理可扩展性方面具有互补优势，与监督模型结合使用可能是最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网天气相关强迫停电的风险预测传统上依赖标注数据训练监督模型，但标注数据的获取成本高且地理可迁移性差。本研究旨在探索LLM能否在无需标注训练数据的零样本框架下直接利用天气和停电记录信息进行停电风险预测，以克服数据标注瓶颈并提升模型的泛化能力。

Method: 将停电风险预测形式化为跨三个预测时域（3/6/12小时）的二元严重度分类任务。基于中央德克萨斯州某公用事业区域六年的停电记录和高分辨率天气数据，在两种输入配置（当前天气观测和天气预报数据）下，对四个零样本LLM与两个监督分类器进行系统基准比较，采用macro-F1和精确率等指标评估。

Result: 监督模型在macro-F1和精确率指标上优于零样本LLM；较新世代的LLM取得了与监督模型具有竞争力的分数；LLM在可操作推理能力和地理可扩展性方面呈现互补优势。

Conclusion: 零样本LLM虽然目前在纯分类精度上不及监督模型，但其在可操作推理和地理可扩展性上的独特优势表明，将LLM与监督模型进行组合/互补使用可能是最优实践，为配电网停电风险管理提供了一种新的混合范式。

Abstract: This study examines the ability of large language models (LLMs) to predict the risk of weather-related forced outages in the distribution grid in a zero-shot framework, without labeled training data. The problem is formulated as a binary severity classification task across three forecast horizons (3h, 6h, 12h), using six years of outage records and high-resolution weather data for a utility service area in central Texas. Four zero-shot LLMs are benchmarked against two supervised classifiers across two input configurations: one using current weather observations and the other using weather forecast data. Results show that supervised models outperform LLMs on macro-F1 and precision, while newer LLM generations achieve competitive scores. Beyond accuracy, LLMs offer complementary strengths in actionable reasoning and geographic scalability, suggesting that combining them with supervised models may be the best practice.

</details>


### [9] [Confounding-Valid Conformal Inference for Counterfactual KPIs in Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05073)
*Abdessamed Qchohi,Jessica Moysen Cortes,Matteo Zecchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CV-CCI框架，结合丰富的观测遥测数据和有限的随机化遥测数据（通过GESPI原理），在存在隐藏混杂因素的情况下实现有限样本覆盖保证的反事实推断，同时提升预测集效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络运营商需要利用记录遥测数据进行反事实"what-if"分析，但观测遥测可能遗漏控制器使用的变量导致隐藏混杂，使统计保证失效。虽然随机化遥测可解决混杂问题，但因其可能干扰正常运营而稀缺，仅基于随机数据产生的预测集信息量不足。

Method: 提出Confounding-Valid Counterfactual Conformal Inference (CV-CCI)，通过General Synthetic-Powered Inference (GESPI)原则将丰富的、可能存在混杂的观测遥测数据与有限的随机化数据相结合，利用观测数据提升效率，同时用随机化数据在任意隐藏混杂下维持有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: 在两个代表性无线接入网（RAN）控制任务上的实验表明，CV-CCI在隐藏混杂下保持有效性，同时产生比最先进的混杂有效基线更高效的预测集。

Conclusion: CV-CCI成功解决了网络反事实推断中观测数据丰富的效率优势与随机化数据覆盖保证之间的权衡，证明可以在保持统计有效性的同时提升预测集的实用性。

Abstract: Conformal counterfactual inference enables network operators to use logged telemetry to reliably answer 'what-if' questions about network operation. These answers typically take the form of prediction sets that contain, with a user-defined probability, the key performance indicators (KPIs) that would have been observed under alternative control actions. A key challenge is that logged telemetry may omit variables used by the controller, resulting in hidden confounding and invalidating the statistical guarantees of counterfactual analysis. In principle, this issue can be addressed using randomized telemetry, collected by assigning control actions independently of the network state. However, because such randomization may disrupt normal operation, randomized telemetry is typically scarce, causing counterfactual analysis based solely on it to produce uninformative prediction sets. To address these challenges, we propose Confounding-Valid Counterfactual Conformal Inference (CV-CCI), which combines abundant, potentially confounded observational telemetry with limited randomized data through the General Synthetic-Powered Inference (GESPI) principle. CV-CCI leverages observational data to improve efficiency while using randomized data to retain finite-sample coverage guarantees under arbitrary hidden confounding. Experiments on two representative radio access network (RAN) control tasks show that CV-CCI remains valid under hidden confounding while producing more efficient prediction sets than state-of-the-art confounding-valid baselines.

</details>


### [10] [BER-PEF: Unified Human Mobility Predictability Evaluation via Bayes Error Rate Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04292)
*En Xu,Jingtao Ding,Zhiwen Yu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BER-PEF框架，将贝叶斯错误率估计转化为移动性可预测性估计，在无真实标签的情况下为异构移动数据的可预测性估计器提供统一评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动可预测性的真实值在真实移动数据上不可直接观测，现有估计器缺乏统一的比较协议，难以在不同类型数据（符号序列、数值轨迹、上下文特征、学习表示）上公平评估。

Method: BER-PEF框架将各类移动数据映射到统一的特征-标签空间，并沿受控扰动曲线评估估计器输出，通过与共享可预测性参考区间的偏差（低于区间、高于区间、全区间）来衡量估计器质量。

Result: 在Foursquare NYC/TKY、GeoLife和T-Drive数据集上，多种基于BER的估计器在符号序列和数值轨迹上的参考差异低于现有可预测性方法，且其估计能跟踪经验预测性能随扰动的变化；聚合多扰动水平的证据比单次未扰动观察更可靠。

Conclusion: BER-PEF为在地面真实可预测性不可用时对异构移动数据进行可预测性估计器评估提供了统一且可验证的路径。

Abstract: Human mobility predictability concerns the best prediction performance attainable from a given target and input information, but its ground truth is not directly observable on real mobility data. We present BER-PEF, a Bayes-error-rate-based framework that converts BER estimation into mobility predictability estimation and provides a unified protocol for comparing estimators without observable ground truth. The framework maps symbolic sequences, numeric trajectories, contextual features, and learned representations into a common feature--label space, then evaluates estimator outputs along controlled perturbation curves against a shared predictability reference interval by measuring deviations below the interval, above the interval, and across the full interval. Experiments on Foursquare NYC and TKY, GeoLife, and T-Drive show that several BER-based estimators achieve lower reference discrepancy than existing predictability methods on symbolic sequences and numeric trajectories, while their estimates track changes in empirical prediction performance under perturbation. Additional analyses show that contextual inputs and multiple structured representations can be evaluated under the same protocol, and that aggregating evidence across multiple perturbation levels provides a more reliable basis for estimator selection than relying on a single unperturbed observation. BER-PEF therefore offers a unified and verifiable path for evaluating predictability estimators on heterogeneous mobility data when ground-truth predictability is unavailable.

</details>


### [11] [Learning from VAE Errors to support ECG-based Differential Diagnosis of Myocardial Scar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05294)
*Shayan Sharifi,Riccardo Treu,Ilaria Gandin,Federico Garoia,Marco Merlo,Giulia Cisotto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于β-变分自编码器（β-VAE）的ECG表征能否区分心脏磁共振延迟钆增强（LGE）阳性和阴性的心肌病患者。在300名受试者的本地队列中，对比了来自ECGx.AI基础模型的32维特征与在正常PTB-XL心电图训练的较浅β-VAE特征，用于下游分类和基于DTW的重构误差分析。结果显示ECGx.AI的ROC曲线下面积（AUC）为0.686（随机森林），而β-VAE达到0.577（梯度提升，灵敏度0.775）；值得注意的是，12个导联中有10个导联的DTW重构误差在两类间存在显著差异（Mann-Whitney U检验），辅助逻辑回归分类时AUC达0.643，提示其可作为疤痕相关ECG改变的潜在标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LGE心脏磁共振是心肌疤痕的关键标志物，但其可及性有限，因此需要基于常规ECG的筛查方法来更广泛地识别心肌病患者的疤痕状态。

Method: 在300名受试者的本地队列中，比较了来自基础模型ECGx.AI的32维特征与在正常PTB-XL ECG上训练的较浅β-VAE特征。采用随机森林和梯度提升进行下游分类，并通过动态时间规整（DTW）计算重构误差，使用Mann-Whitney U检验评估组间差异，并利用逻辑回归结合DTW误差进行分类。

Result: ECGx.AI结合随机森林达到AUC 0.686；β-VAE结合梯度提升达到AUC 0.577（灵敏度0.775）；DTW重构误差在12个导联中有10个导联的两类间存在显著差异，结合逻辑回归后AUC达0.643。

Conclusion: DTW重构误差有望作为疤痕相关ECG改变的标志物，支持其在ECG筛查LGE阳性心肌病患者中的潜在价值。

Abstract: Late Gadolinium Enhancement (LGE) on cardiac magnetic resonance is a key marker of myocardial scar, but its limited accessibility motivates routine ECG-based screening. We evaluated whether $β$-variational autoencoder (VAE)-derived ECG representations can discriminate LGE+ from LGE- cardiomyopathic patients in a local cohort of 300 subjects. We compared 32-dimensional features from the foundation ECGx.AI model with those from a shallower $β$-VAE trained on normal PTB-XL ECGs, evaluating downstream classification and Dynamic Time Warping (DTW)-based reconstruction errors. ECGx.AI reached an area under ROC of 0.686 with Random Forest, while the proposed $β$-VAE reached 0.577 with sensitivity of 0.775 with Gradient Boosting. Notably, DTW-reconstruction errors significantly differed between classes in 10 out of 12 leads according to Mann-Whitney U test and help in classification, leading to an area under ROC of 0.643 with Logistic Regression, supporting their potential as markers of scar-related ECG alterations.

</details>


### [12] [Data-Driven Learning of Unknown Nonlinear Differential Equations Using Functional Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04329)
*Seyyed Shaho Alaviani,Yongzhi Qu,Gregory W. Vogl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的可解释机器学习方法用于数据驱动的非线性常微分方程（ODE）发现。该方法无需系统物理先验知识，仅从单一状态轨迹数据中学习未知向量场。其核心创新在于基于泛函分析与算子理论构建函数空间中的积分形式损失函数（而非传统离散误差和），并采用增量学习算法以在线方式处理新训练样本。方法适用于受迫/非受迫的自治与非自治（时变）动力系统，可同时发现随时间变化的外部强迫函数和底层未知动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的ODE发现方法主要基于离散误差之和的损失函数构建，在处理连续动力系统时存在精度和可解释性方面的局限；同时现有方法对非自治系统、外部强迫项以及在线增量学习的支持不足。本文旨在通过重新审视ODE发现问题的数学框架，提出一种更具理论基础且能处理更广泛动力系统的可解释机器学习方法。

Method: 该方法的两个核心差异是：1) 基于泛函分析和算子理论重新推导问题公式化表达；2) 损失函数在函数空间中构建为两个函数之间距离的积分形式，而非传统ML方法中的离散误差和。此外提出了增量学习算法，使模型能够以在线方式处理新到达的训练样本。该方法能够在有外力和无外力、自治和非自治（时变）动力系统下发现未知向量场，并可同时识别随时间变化的外部力函数与底层未知动力学。

Result: 通过数值实验示例展示了方法的优势，涵盖了受迫与未受迫的自治及非自治动力系统场景。结果表明该方法能够有效发现未知向量场并同时识别外部强迫函数与内部动力学。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法为数据驱动的ODE发现提供了一种新的理论基础框架，通过将损失度量定义在函数空间而非离散点集上，提高了学习的精度和泛化能力；增量学习机制使其适合流式数据处理场景，对更广泛的实际应用具有潜在价值。

Abstract: In this paper, the problem of data-driven discovery of nonlinear ordinary differential equations (ODEs) is recast, and a new interpretable machine learning (ML) method is proposed. The proposed method aims to learn the unknown vector field of nonlinear dynamics without prior knowledge of the system's physics from only one single state trajectory's data. The proposed method has two fundamental differences with existing methods: 1) the formulation presented in this method is derived based on Functional Analysis and Operator Theory, and 2) the cost function is constructed in the function space as a distance between two functions as an integral, instead of the discrete-sum of errors used in existing ML approaches. An incremental learning algorithm is proposed to learn the unknown vector field to handle new training samples in an online manner. The proposed method can discover the unknown vector field from both forced and unforced autonomous and non-autonomous (or time-varying) dynamical systems. The proposed method is able to simultaneously discover unknown external forces as a function of time and unknown underlying dynamics. Finally, numerical examples are given to demonstrate the advantages of the proposed method.

</details>


### [13] [Modular Deep Recurrent Neural Network: Application to Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04339)
*Nima Mohajerin,Steven L. Waslander*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化深度循环神经网络（RNN）框架，便于部署不同RNN架构并自动计算梯度。其中一种新架构引入层间前馈连接，显著提升了多层RNN学习高阶动态和非线性的能力，并缓解了空间维度上的梯度消失/爆炸问题。在四旋翼飞行器高度动力学建模案例中，该网络结构相比现有方法具有更优的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RNN架构部署过程繁琐且难以灵活组合，多层RNN在空间维度上存在梯度消失/爆炸问题，限制了其学习高阶动态和非线性系统的能力。

Method: 提出模块化深度RNN框架，支持自动微分用于基于梯度的学习。引入一种包含层间前馈连接的新型多层RNN架构，通过前馈连接缓解空间维度上的梯度消失/爆炸问题，并提升模型对高阶动态和非线性的表达能力。

Result: 在四旋翼飞行器高度动力学建模案例中，所提出的带前馈层间连接的多层RNN能够有效学习该动力学模型，而现有方法无法快速泛化甚至完全无法泛化。

Conclusion: 模块化设计使RNN架构的部署和梯度计算更加便捷，前馈层间连接显著增强了多层RNN的学习能力与泛化性能，为复杂非线性动态系统建模提供了有效方案。

Abstract: A modular deep Recurrent Neural Network (RNN) is introduced to facilitate the process of deploying various architectures of RNNs, and to automatically compute derivatives for gradient-based learning methods. The modularity leads to a set of new architectures, one of which includes feedforward inter-layer connections. By adding feedforward inter-layer connections in a multi-layer RNN, it is observed that the capability of the RNN to learn and model high-order dynamics and nonlinearities is significantly improved. The problem of vanishing/exploding gradient in space for a multilayer RNN is also alleviated using feedforward connections. These results are demonstrated using a quadrotor case study, for which a model of the altitude dynamics is learned with our particular network structure, while existing methods are unable to generalize as quickly or at all.

</details>


### [14] [SharedSAE: One Feature Dictionary Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04344)
*Daniil Ognev,Célian Vasson,Lijie Hu,Kentaro Inui,Benjamin Heinzerling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SharedSAE方法，用单一共享稀疏自编码器替代每个模型专属的SAE，通过共享字典加模型专属编码器-解码器对，在四个1B规模不同模型家族上保留96.6%的专属SAE解释方差，并支持新模型高效适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器广泛用于解释语言模型激活，但传统上每个模型都需要单独训练SAE并重复标注潜在语义，成本高昂且难以跨模型复用。作者旨在证明一个共享SAE可以替代一组专属SAE，减少重复训练与标注开销。

Method: SharedSAE结合共享潜在字典与模型专属的编码器-解码器对。与先前方法不同，其仅对选择分数进行归一化而保留激活幅度，并采用模型dropout实现单模型推理，从而避免丢失幅度信息并支持推理时无需全部模型在场。

Result: 在四个1B规模、不同家族和分词器的基座语言模型上训练，SharedSAE保留专属SAE平均解释方差的96.6%；其潜在激活的跨模型相关性是事后对齐的独立SAE的1.8倍；潜在语义描述可跨模型迁移。字典冻结后，新模型可高效适配并达到接近专属SAE的重建质量。

Conclusion: 共享字典的SAE框架在保持解释性能的同时实现跨模型复用的潜在语义，显著降低多模型SAE训练与标注成本，为语言模型可解释性提供更高效的规模化路径。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret language model activations, but SAE training and latent labelling are typically repeated for every model. Here, we show that a single shared SAE can replace a collection of dedicated per-model SAEs. Our method, SharedSAE, combines a shared dictionary with model-specific encoder-decoder pairs. Unlike the closest prior method, which discards activation magnitudes and requires all models at inference, SharedSAE instead normalizes only selection scores, preserving magnitudes, and uses model dropout for single-model inference. We train SharedSAE on four 1B-scale base language models spanning distinct families and tokenizers. Despite sharing its latents across models, SharedSAE retains 96.6% of dedicated SAEs' mean explained variance; its latent activations exhibit cross-model correlations 1.8 times as high as separate SAEs aligned post-hoc, and its latent descriptions transfer across models. After the dictionary is frozen, new models can be efficiently adapted to it, achieving near-dedicated-SAE reconstruction quality while reusing the shared latent descriptions.

</details>


### [15] [Coarse-Graining Hidden Representations: Unsupervised Neuron Selection via Mapping Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05126)
*Margherita Mele,Andrea Castagna,Roberto Menichetti,Raffaello Potestio,Alessandro Ingrosso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用映射熵（Mapping Entropy, ME）作为无监督准则，从过参数化神经网络的隐藏层中选择神经元子集（粗粒化），以识别任务必需神经元，所学选择的子网络在多种任务上优于随机同规模子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化神经网络包含远超任务需求的隐藏单元，但哪些神经元是关键、这一区分是否能在无需标签或梯度的条件下从表示本身读出，仍不清楚。

Method: 将神经元选择建模为对隐藏层的粗粒化问题——保留神经元子集并对每个候选选择计算映射熵（ME），该量度量丢弃部分神经元时判别信息的损失；ME 最小化即视为最具信息性的选择。该准则完全无监督，仅依赖隐藏层激活统计量。

Result: 在教师-学生网络中，ME 优化恢复最小教师一致表示，并以与隐藏层残差变异性成比例的方式保留额外单元；在非线性高斯过程任务中，它选出随训练推进偏好类别发生切换的一致功能类映射。在翻译增强 MNIST 等任务上，ME 选择的子网络在同等规模下优于随机子集，且在强压缩下优势最明显，表明构型可区分性与预测性能之间存在关联。

Conclusion: 映射熵提供了一种标签无关、梯度无关的神经元选择准则，能够揭示网络表示中的关键神经元结构，并且所选子网络的预测性能印证了这种无监督选择的有效性。

Abstract: Overparameterized neural networks carry far more hidden units than a task nominally requires, raising the question of which neurons are essential and whether that distinction is legible in the representation itself, without labels or gradients. We cast neuron selection as the problem of coarse-graining the hidden layer by retaining a subset of its neurons, and score each putative selection by the mapping entropy (ME). This quantity measures the loss of discriminatory power inherent in discarding part of the network neurons, and the selection that minimises the ME is taken as particularly informative. This criterion is fully unsupervised, in that it depends only on hidden-activation statistics. In teacher-student networks, ME optimisation recovers the minimal teacher-consistent representation and retains extra units in proportion to the hidden layer's residual variability; in a non-linear Gaussian process task, it selects coherent functional-class mappings whose preferred class shifts across training. On this task and on translation-augmented MNIST, ME-selected subnetworks outperform random subsets of equal size, most clearly under strong compression - linking configurational distinguishability to predictive performance.

</details>
