Abstract: Personalized federated learning (PFL) is a promising paradigm for collaborative learning over distributed devices, where edge nodes collaboratively train personalized models without sharing raw data. Although PFL addresses data heterogeneity by learning client-specific models, it still suffers from substantial uplink and downlink communication costs when exchanging high-dimensional parameters in bandwidth-constrained systems. Recent one-bit methods achieve extreme compression, but they usually rely on a single thresholding rule applied to the whole model. This design has two limitations. First, it overlooks layer-wise differences in parameter distributions and quantization sensitivities. Second, a single threshold provides only coarse binary information and cannot capture fine-grained variations in parameter distributions. To address these issues, we propose a communication-efficient PFL framework via layer-wise multi-threshold random sketching. In the proposed method, each layer is assigned its own set of quantization thresholds, so that the compressed representation can adapt to layer-specific statistics while using multiple intervals to provide a finer low-bit description of sketched parameters. The proposed method supports bidirectional communication using compact low-bit sketches and improves the communication-accuracy tradeoff compared with existing one-bit compression approaches.

</details>


### [51] [PACE: Propagation-Aware Collaborative Correction for One-Shot Personalized Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04832)
*Ruizhe Huang,Chengran Li,Xiaochuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PACE框架，将个性化联邦图学习中的协作知识视为对完整本地预测器的紧凑修正而非替代品。每个客户端上传秩r更新载体和对角化消息矩草图，服务器构建传播感知、接收者锚定的修正，客户端通过凸负对数似然校准(CNLL)在本地与外部logits之间选择系数，模型参数固定且无反馈。实验表明修正仅在接收者证据支持时获得非零权重并提升性能，否则保持本地预测不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户端异质性在个性化联邦图学习中既是机遇也是风险——其他子图的知识可能补充接收者的本地模型，但不兼容的迁移会覆盖可靠预测。一次性通信加剧了这一矛盾，因为不合适的服务器返回无法事后纠正。现有方法将外部知识作为本地模型的替代品，缺乏能根据接收者证据自适应决定是否采纳外部知识的机制。

Method: PACE方法：(1)每个客户端上传秩r更新载体和传播消息矩的对角化草图；(2)服务器利用这些构建传播感知且接收者锚定的紧凑修正；(3)接收者保留完整本地模型；(4)使用凸负对数似然校准(CNLL)在本地与外部logits之间选择一个系数，利用验证节点决定修正权重，模型参数保持固定且不发送反馈。

Result: 在秩6条件下，个性化返回数据在六个数据集上仅占用稠密张量字节的9.6%-17.6%。修正获得非零权重并在五个数据集上同时提升Accuracy和加权F1；在ogbn-arxiv上，CNLL将修正的预测权重设为零，精确保持本地预测。将相同的CNLL规则应用于三个引文数据集的匹配基线，无法解释这些增益。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：当接收者证据支持时，小型传输修正可以增强完整本地模型；当接收者证据不支持时，本地预测保持不变。这一机制使代理加权成为上下文相关、接收者锚定的过程，而非全局固定的系数。

Abstract: Client heterogeneity creates both an opportunity and a risk in personalized federated graph learning. Knowledge held by other subgraphs may complement a receiver's Local model, but an incompatible transfer can override reliable predictions. One-shot communication sharpens this tension because an unsuitable server return cannot be corrected later. We introduce PACE, which treats collaborative knowledge as a compact correction to a complete Local predictor rather than as its replacement. Each client uploads a rank-r update carrier and a diagonal sketch of propagated message moments. The server uses them to construct a propagation-aware, receiver-anchored correction, while the receiver retains its full Local model. Convex negative-log-likelihood calibration (CNLL) then selects one coefficient between Local and External logits using validation nodes; model parameters remain fixed and no feedback is sent. At Rank-6, personalized returns occupy 9.6-17.6% of dense tensor bytes across the six evaluated datasets. The correction receives nonzero weight and improves both Accuracy and weighted-F1 over Local on five datasets; on ogbn-arxiv, CNLL assigns zero predictive weight to the correction and preserves Local predictions exactly. Applying the same CNLL rule to matched baselines on three citation datasets does not account for these gains. The central result is therefore that a small transported correction can augment a complete Local model when receiver evidence supports it while leaving the Local prediction unchanged otherwise.

</details>


### [52] [KVMem: Virtualizing Million-Token Agent Workspaces on a Consumer GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04852)
*Di Chai,Leye Wang,Zeshen Su,Zhiguo Xia,Zhihang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KVMem是一个KV上下文虚拟化系统，将溢出的智能体工作区历史以分页KV状态形式保存在GPU内存、主机内存和NVMe中，通过轻量级的注意力空间索引选择相关历史块，并物化出受模型原生上下文窗口限制的查询依赖执行视图，使智能体工作区可超越模型原生上下文窗口（如将256K扩展到1M tokens）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM智能体在持久工作区中运行，其累积历史可能超过GPU KV容量和模型原生上下文窗口。现有系统要么将旧上下文压缩为摘要（丢失细粒度执行证据），要么以文本形式检索（反复预填充模型已处理过的内容），两者均无法同时兼顾完整性和效率。

Method: KVMem采用三层设计：1) 将溢出工作区历史以分页KV状态形式跨GPU内存、主机内存和NVMe分层存储；2) 使用轻量级、模型原生的注意力空间索引选择相关历史块；3) 物化受模型原生上下文窗口限制的查询依赖执行视图。该设计将可寻址工作区大小与LLM原生上下文窗口解耦。

Result: 在长达百万token历史的LongMemEval、MemoryAgentBench和AgentLongBench等长上下文智能体基准上，KVMem通常比压缩方法（处理上下文溢出的事实标准）获得更高任务效用和推理效率。在DeepSWE长上下文测试中（Qwen3.8-27B），任务成功率从压缩方案的43.8%提升至48.4%。在本地部署中，KVMem可在搭载24GB RTX 5090 Laptop GPU的普通笔记本上运行Qwen3.6/3.8-27B NVFP4 with MTP，虚拟化高达1M tokens的工作区；单会话场景下约50 tokens/s的生成速度，提供了交互式响应能力。

Conclusion: 通过将可寻址工作区规模与LLM原生上下文窗口解耦，KVMem为长时运行且工作区可超出上下文窗口的智能体提供了一条实用路径，证明了KV虚拟化在保持任务效用和推理效率的同时支持大规模工作区的可行性。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents operate in persistent workspaces whose accumulated history can exceed both GPU KV capacity and the model's native context window. Existing systems typically compact older context into summaries or retrieve it later as text, either losing fine-grained execution evidence or repeatedly prefilling content that the model has already processed. We present KVMem, a KV-context virtualization system that preserves overflowed workspace history as paged KV state across GPU memory, host memory, and NVMe. KVMem uses lightweight, model-native attention-space indexes to select relevant historical blocks and materializes a query-dependent execution view bounded by the model's native context window. Extensive evaluations on long-context agent benchmarks spanning histories up to one million tokens, including LongMemEval, MemoryAgentBench, and AgentLongBench, show that KVMem generally achieves higher task utility and greater inference efficiency than compaction-based approaches, the de facto standard for handling context overflow. In the DeepSWE long-context test with Qwen3.8-27B, KVMem improves task success from 43.8% with compaction-only context management to 48.4%.
  In our local-deployment evaluation, KVMem runs Qwen3.6/3.8-27B NVFP4 with MTP on an off-the-shelf laptop equipped with a 24\,GB RTX 5090 Laptop GPU, virtualizing agent workspaces of up to 1M tokens-four times the model's native 256K-token context window. In a single-session setting, KVMem generates $\sim$50 tokens/s, providing interactive responsiveness for local agent execution. More broadly, by decoupling addressable workspace size from the LLM's native context window, KVMem provides a practical path toward long-running agents whose workspaces can grow beyond that window.

</details>


### [53] [When Genomic Masking Priors Fail to Transfer: Strong Variant Prediction, Weak Functional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04861)
*Susu Hu,Preetam Gattogi,Jens Lehmann,Sahar Vahdati,Stefanie Speidel,Julien Vibert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了GenDA（Genomic Density-optimized Absorbing Diffusion），一种双向离散扩散模型用于基因组建模，假设熵引导的跨度放置能聚焦于组合上复杂的区域。经监督微调后，202M参数的GenDA在ClinVar SNV AUROC上达0.774，超过类似规模的AR模型0.103；但随机跨度变体达到0.777，表明熵引导并非改进原因。更意外的是，GenDA在零样本功能修复压力测试中失败，未能一致优于保持3-mer组成的洗牌对照组。诊断识别出多项边界条件，表明强微调变异预测、合理的损坏先验和功能生成是需要分别验证的不同主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组建模中，双向离散扩散模型因能利用双侧侧翼序列重建缺失片段，在结构上天然适合基因组序列建模。作者提出熵引导的跨度放置假设，认为将重建压力集中在组合复杂的区域可以提升下游变异效应预测和功能性序列生成的表现。

Method: 开发了GenDA模型，采用双向吸收态离散扩散机制，并提出熵引导的缺失跨度放置策略以集中重建压力于序列复杂度高的区域。通过监督微调进行ClinVar变异效应预测评估，并与匹配的随机跨度变体及类似规模的AR模型对比；同时设计零样本功能修复压力测试，使用保留3-mer组成的洗牌对照进行验证。

Result: GenDA微调后在ClinVar SNV AUROC达到0.774，优于AR模型0.103；但熵引导与随机跨度变体（0.777）无显著差异，熵引导假说未被支持。在功能修复测试中，GenDA在启动子、增强子、外显子边界和内含子边界均未一致优于3-mer保真洗牌对照，50-500bp缺口即出现失败，增强子在长缺口下退化加剧。

Conclusion: 诊断表明熵度量的是局部序列复杂度而非功能重要性，1-mer分词限制物理上下文，训练跨度上限300bp限制泛化，且高AlphaGenome保真度可与负对照归一化的低恢复性能共存。研究证实强微调变异预测、合理损坏先验和功能生成是不同层面的声明，需独立验证，不能相互替代推定。

Abstract: Bidirectional discrete diffusion model appears naturally suited to genomic modeling because it can reconstruct missing sequence from both flanks. We developed GenDA (Genomic Density-optimized Absorbing Diffusion) under the additional hypothesis that entropy-guided span placement would concentrate reconstruction pressure on compositionally complex regions, improving both downstream variant-effect prediction and functional sequence generation. Our results only partially support this premise. After supervised fine-tuning, the 202M-parameter GenDA model reaches a pooled ClinVar SNV AUROC of 0.774, exceeding a similarly scaled autoregressive model by 0.103. However, a matched random-span variant reaches 0.777, providing no evidence that entropy guidance causes the ClinVar improvement. More unexpectedly, GenDA fails a zero-shot functional inpainting stress test: across promoters, enhancers, exon boundaries, and intron boundaries, it does not consistently outperform a control that shuffles the native gap while exactly preserving 3-mer composition. Failure is already present for 50--500-bp gaps, although enhancer degradation worsens at longer gaps. Diagnostics identify several boundary conditions: entropy measures local sequence complexity rather than functional importance; 1-mer tokenization limits physical context; training spans are capped at 300 bp; and high absolute AlphaGenome fidelity can coexist with negative control-normalized restoration. These results show that strong fine-tuned variant prediction, a plausible corruption prior, and functional generation are distinct claims that require separate validation.

</details>


### [54] [From Deep to Shallow: Unconstrained and Efficient Layer Merging Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04881)
*Petro Shulzhenko,Gabriele Spadaro,Enzo Tartaglione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的深度压缩策略，使得在无解析解的情况下也能合并带有padding的卷积层，且不增加合并后层的kernel size，从而在资源受限的嵌入式平台上获得推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络计算开销大，限制了在资源受限环境中的应用。现有的深度压缩方法通过线性化冗余激活函数并合并层来减少计算量，但面临两个关键挑战：(1) 无法直接应用于带padding的卷积层，因为合并这些层缺乏解析解；(2) 合并层通常会导致卷积核尺寸增大，从而限制加速效果。

Method: 提出了一种高效的层合并策略，能够在不依赖现有解析解的情况下实现层的合并，同时避免增大合并后层的卷积核尺寸。具体实现细节涉及对带padding卷积的处理以及保持kernel size不变的合并方式。

Result: 在多种架构和数据集上验证了方法的有效性，并在真实嵌入式平台上测量了推理加速收益。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了深度压缩中padding卷积无法合并以及kernel size膨胀导致加速受限的难题，为资源受限环境下的深度神经网络部署提供了更有效的压缩方案。

Abstract: Although Deep Neural Networks have become foundational in many areas of Machine Learning, high computational demands limit their application in resource-constrained environments. To address this issue, depth compression methods have been proposed to identify and linearize redundant activation functions, thereby allowing for the merging of layers without intermediate non-linearities. However, these methods face two key challenges: they cannot be directly applied to convolutions with padding due to the absence of an analytical solution for merging these layers, and they typically increase the kernel size of merged layers, thus limiting speed-up gains. To overcome these limitations, we propose an efficient strategy that enables merging of layers without an existing analytical solution, and also without increasing kernel size. We validate our approach across multiple architectures and datasets, and measure inference speed-up gains on real embedded platforms. We publicly released the code at https://github.com/ShulzhenkoPetr/deep-to-shallow.

</details>


### [55] [Adaptation Interfaces for In-Context Tabular Foundation Models in Time-to-Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04901)
*Minh-Khoi Pham,Luca Cotugno,Dan Cernei,Alina Sirbu,Stefano Masi,Giuseppe Prencipe,Alessandro Pingitore,Patrizia Landi,Working Group on Uric Acid,Cardiovascular Risk of the Italian Society of Hypertension,Tai Tan Mai,Martin Crane,Marija Bezbradica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将表格基础模型（TabFMs）扩展到带删失的生存分析领域。作者基于先前工作，将TabFMs与CoxPH和DeepHit等生存模型链接，并修正了上下文重采样训练流程。在74个单风险数据集和4个竞争风险数据集上系统评估了零样本推断、基于分类的微调及冻结主干上的生存头适配三种范式。结果显示：小数据集上零样本推断有效，大数据集下有监督适配更优；Cox是最稳健的接口（尤其对IBS指标）；DeepHit在时间依赖C指数上相对优于IBS；MTLR在竞争风险分析中表现最好。结论强调TabFM迁移效果取决于数据规模与所选适配接口的统计结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在标准分类与回归任务上表现出色，但将其扩展到删失时间-事件（生存）预测具有挑战性，因为需要正确处理删失结构和事件时间动态。现有研究尚缺乏对TabFM在不同适配范式下用于生存分析的系统性评估。

Method: 将TabFMs与CoxPH和DeepHit等生存模型耦合，修订了context-resampled训练流程。在74个单风险数据集和4个竞争风险数据集上，使用冻结TabFM主干系统评估了三种范式：时间零样本再表述、基于分类的微调、以及生存头适配。评估指标包括IBS和时间依赖C指数等。

Result: 零样本推断在较小的单风险数据集上有效；随着数据规模增大，有监督适配优势递增。Cox在较大数据集上对IBS表现最稳健；DeepHit在时间依赖C指数上相对强于IBS；在4个数据集的竞争风险分析中，MTLR生存头表现最优。分类微调随数据增长竞争性增强，但在概率预测上仍较弱。

Conclusion: 有效的TabFM迁移取决于数据体制（规模）以及所选适配接口所表征的统计结构。没有单一通用方案，需根据数据条件和预测目标选择合适的接口与方法。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TabFMs) achieve strong performance on structured data, particularly for standard classification and regression problems. Yet, extending them to censored time-to-event prediction is challenging because it requires properly handling censoring and event-time dynamics. Building on our prior work, we further link TabFMs with CoxPH and DeepHit and revise the context-resampled training procedure. We evaluate temporal zero-shot reformulation, classification-based fine-tuning, and survival-head adaptation using frozen TabFM backbones on 74 single-risk data sets, and we additionally study 4 competing-risk data sets. Zero-shot inference is effective on smaller single-risk data sets, whereas supervised adaptation becomes increasingly advantageous as data sets scale. Cox provides the most reliably strong interface, especially for Integrated Brier Score (IBS) on larger data sets. DeepHit is relatively stronger for the time-dependent Concordance Index than for IBS, while cause-specific MTLR ranks highest among the TabFM survival heads in the four-data-set competing-risk analysis. Classification fine-tuning becomes more competitive with zero-shot inference as data sets grow but remains weaker for probabilistic prediction. Overall, our results indicate that effective TabFM transfer depends on the data regime and on the statistical structure represented by the chosen adaptation interface. The implementation scripts used for this work are available at https://github.com/kaylode/survival-fm.

</details>


### [56] [Physics-Aware Random Walk Fingerprints for Scalable Power Grid Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04943)
*Adnan Anwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MC-PA-RWF（多通道物理感知随机游走指纹）框架，一种轻量级图级表示方法，用于电力系统级联故障分类。通过将物理边状态引入随机游走动力学，在三个PowerGraph基准系统上达到与强GNN基线（GCN、GAT、GINE、TransformerConv）相当的平衡准确率，且MC-PA-RWF+在最大评估规模下实现98.04%-99.32%的平衡准确率，在故障类F1上较最强GNN基线提升1.60-5.84个百分点，并具有统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GNN在电力网络级联故障分类上表现优异，但其需要端到端训练和特定模型调优，且潜在表示难以解释为物理上显著的传播模式。已有的随机游走指纹（RWF）虽可扩展且可解释，但主要强调拓扑和节点级信息，未能将电网相关的运行边状态融入游走动力学中，这限制了其对电力系统故障传播的刻画能力。

Method: MC-PA-RWF从领域相关属性构建多个边加权通道，为每个加权图提取通道特定的随机游走指纹，并将结果向量拼接为紧凑的图级表示。具体流程：（1）基于物理边状态（如电力属性）构造多个加权图通道；（2）在每个通道上运行加权随机游走并提取指纹；（3）拼接所有通道指纹形成最终表示。MC-PA-RWF+为该框架的节点-边扩展版本。

Result: 在三个PowerGraph基准系统上的实验表明：与仅依赖拓扑的RWF相比有显著改进；与GCN、GAT、GINE和TransformerConv等强GNN基线相比具有竞争力的平衡准确率。在最大评估规模下，MC-PA-RWF+达到约98.04%-99.32%的平衡准确率，故障类F1较最强GNN基线提升1.60-5.84个百分点，在所有三个系统上均具有统计显著性增益。

Conclusion: MC-PA-RWF提供了一条兼顾可解释性、轻量化与性能的替代路径：通过物理感知的随机游走将电网运行边状态编码进图级表示，在保持与深度GNN基线竞争性准确率的同时避免了端到端训练的复杂性与模型不可解释性，为电力级联故障分类提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent benchmarks such as PowerGraph provide large collections of power-grid graphs for cascading-failure classification. Graph neural networks (GNNs) achieve strong predictive performance on this task, but typically require end-to-end training and model-specific tuning, while their latent representations can be difficult to relate to physically meaningful propagation patterns. Random Walk Fingerprints (RWF) offer a scalable and interpretable alternative, but existing variants primarily emphasise topology and node-level information, leaving grid-relevant operational edge states in the walk dynamics. We propose Multi-Channel Physics-Aware Random Walk Fingerprints (MC-PA-RWF) for power systems, a lightweight graph-level representation framework that introduces physical edge states into random-walk propagation. The method constructs multiple edge-weighted channels from domain-relevant attributes, extracts a channel-specific fingerprint from each weighted graph, and concatenates the resulting vectors into a compact representation. Experiments on three \textit{PowerGraph} benchmark systems show substantial improvements over topology-only RWF and competitive balanced accuracy against strong GNN baselines, including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCN), Graph Attention Networks (GAT), Graph Isomorphism Networks with edge features (GINE), and Transformer-based Graph Convolutional Networks (TransformerConv). At the largest evaluated settings, the node-edge extension MC-PA-RWF+ achieves around 98.04% - 99.32% balanced accuracy and improves failure-class F1 over the strongest GNN baseline by 1.60 -- 5.84 percentage points, with statistically significant gains across all three systems.

</details>


### [57] [Fractal basins trap latent reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04963)
*Jeffrey Lai,Anthony Bao,John Quinn,William Gilpin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现推理模型在困难任务上表现出"瞬时混沌"现象——推理轨迹是分形动力系统，分形维数随任务难度增加。这种混沌源于推理过程被困在鞍点附近，而鞍点对应近乎正确的解题尝试。研究表明推理变慢是问题难度的必然物理后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在更困难任务上推理时间更长的现象已被广泛观察到，但其背后的通用机制未知。作者希望揭示推理减速的根本物理与计算原理，而非仅仅停留在经验观察层面。

Method: 作者将多种主流推理模型的推理过程视为动力系统进行分析，在Sudoku、迷宫求解、视觉谜题和数学逻辑等多样化任务上，通过测量分形盆地（fractal basins）特性及其随任务难度的变化来验证瞬时混沌的存在；并识别推理轨迹在鞍点附近的逗留来建立混沌与错误尝试之间的对应关系。

Result: 实证表明多个主流推理模型均为具有分形盆地的动力系统，分形程度随任务难度增加而上升；推理减速源于模型在对应"近乎正确解答"的鞍点附近长时间滞留，由此产生瞬时混沌。这表明减速是问题复杂性的必然结果。

Conclusion: 推理减速是问题固有难度在动力学层面的必然体现，而非可被单纯优化的"缺陷"；推理轨迹构成一类全新的复杂动力系统，为理解和分析AI推理提供了物理学的理论框架。

Abstract: Reasoning allows artificial intelligence models to revisit and correct their mistakes, enabling recent frontier advances in mathematical theorem solving, software engineering, and autonomous task planning. Reasoning models are widely observed to reason for longer on harder tasks, but the general mechanism responsible for these slowdowns is unknown. Here, we show that reasoning models exhibit transient chaos, a physical consequence of the computational complexity of difficult tasks. As a consequence, we show that diverse leading reasoning models are dynamical systems with fractal basins, with fractality increasing with task difficulty across diverse tasks like Sudoku and maze solving, visual puzzles, and mathematical logic. We show that transient chaos emerges due to reasoning becoming trapped for extended durations near saddle points, which we show correspond to nearly-correct attempted solutions of the underlying problem. Our results show that reasoning slowdowns are an inevitable consequence of problem hardness in modern artificial intelligence models, and establish reasoning traces as a rich new class of dynamical system.

</details>


### [58] [BeaconKV: Key-Value Cache Compression Guided by Beacon Queries for Efficient Large Reasoning Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04971)
*Janghyeon Kim,Minsoo Kim,Kyuhong Shim,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BeaconKV，一种无需训练的KV缓存压缩方法，通过维护全局查询聚类的紧凑代表来预测长程推理中被重新关注的KV对，在多种LRM上实现最高5.8倍内存缩减并保持接近完整缓存的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型(LRM)通过扩展思维链提高问题求解能力，但KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，造成严重的内存瓶颈。现有KV缓存压缩方法依赖近期查询作为未来token重要性的代理，但这一假设在长程推理中失效——某些解码步骤会产生"思维回溯令牌"(TRT)，重新关注遥远远处的上下文（如早期制定的解题计划），导致基于近邻假设的压缩方法性能下降。

Method: 通过系统性分析发现，TRT对应的query在嵌入空间中聚集为少量相似簇。基于此洞察，提出BeaconKV：维护beacon queries（每个全局查询簇的紧凑代表），用于预测哪些KV对会被重新关注，而无需存储整个查询历史。该方法无需训练，直接应用于现有LRM的推理过程。

Result: 在四个开源LRM和多样化推理基准上，BeaconKV普遍优于现有压缩方法，实现高达5.8倍的内存缩减，几乎保持完整缓存的精度，吞吐量提升超过4.3倍。

Conclusion: BeaconKV通过捕获长程推理中TRT的模式化注意行为，有效解决了KV缓存压缩在长推理场景中的代理失效问题，在不牺牲准确性的前提下显著降低内存开销并提升推理吞吐。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve superior problem-solving through extended Chain-of-Thought (CoT) generation, but the resulting key-value (KV) cache grows linearly with sequence length and creates severe memory bottlenecks, often exceeding GPU capacity for long reasoning traces. Existing KV cache compression methods rely on recent queries to estimate future token importance, implicitly assuming these serve as reliable proxies for future attention patterns. We demonstrate that this assumption fails in long-horizon reasoning: certain decoding steps generate Thought Revisiting Tokens (TRT) that re-attend to distant previous context, such as task-solving plans formulated early in the trace. Through systematic analysis, we discover that queries corresponding to the TRT cluster into a small number of similarity groups in the embedding space. Based on this insight, we propose BeaconKV, a training-free KV cache compression method that maintains beacon queries, compact representatives for each global query cluster, to anticipate which KV pairs will be revisited without storing the entire query history. Across four open-source LRMs and diverse reasoning benchmarks, BeaconKV generally outperforms existing compression methods, achieving up to $5.8\times$ memory reduction while nearly preserving full cache accuracy and improving throughput by over $4.3\times$.

</details>


### [59] [Beyond Homoscedasticity: Decoupled Uncertainty Optimization for Deep Imbalanced Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04995)
*Juncheng Zhou,Jiaxi Lu,Weijing Zeng,Zhong Li,Hao Qi,Jingsong Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DUO框架，通过条件高斯分布建模、解耦均值-方差优化和分布引导对比学习，解决深度不平衡回归中尾部样本难拟合和特征松散的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度不平衡回归（DIR）中大多数方法假设所有样本具有统一的不确定性水平，忽略了长尾数据中普遍存在的实例级异方差性；同时异方差负对数似然存在梯度耦合问题，在DIR场景下削弱硬尾部样本的学习信号，导致优化惰性和尾部欠拟合。

Method: DUO框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 将回归目标建模为条件高斯分布以显式刻画实例级预测不确定性；(2) 通过解耦均值-方差优化将不确定性转化为尾部样本的动态增强信号；(3) 设计分布引导的对比学习机制，基于样本分布重叠度自适应构建正负样本对，缓解特征松散与跨标签语义纠缠。

Result: 在视觉和生物领域的DIR基准上，DUO在IMDB-WIKI-DIR、AgeDB-DIR和AAV2-DIR数据集上取得了最优的few-shot bMAE和GM指标，在few-shot MAE上也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: DUO通过不确定性建模与分布引导对比学习的结合，有效解决了DIR中尾部样本欠拟合和特征表征松散的问题，在多模态不平衡回归任务中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Deep Imbalanced Regression (DIR) is pervasive in continuous prediction tasks across diverse modalities, such as age estimation, depth prediction, and protein mutation activity prediction, where label-scarce tail samples often carry higher practical value. However, most existing methods still learn deterministic point mappings under mean squared error or its simple variants, implicitly assuming a uniform uncertainty level across all samples and thereby overlooking the instance-wise heteroscedasticity that is widespread in long-tailed data. We further point out that even heteroscedastic negative log-likelihood suffers from a gradient coupling issue, which, under DIR scenarios, weakens the learning signal of hard tail samples and leads to optimization inertia as well as tail underfitting. To address this, we propose DUO, an uncertainty-aware long-tailed regression framework. Specifically, the proposed method models the regression target as a conditional Gaussian distribution to explicitly characterize instance-level predictive uncertainty, and transforms uncertainty into a dynamic enhancement signal for tail samples through decoupled mean-variance optimization. Furthermore, we design a distribution-guided contrastive learning mechanism that adaptively constructs positive and negative pairs based on the overlap between sample distributions, thereby alleviating feature looseness and cross-label semantic entanglement. Across visual and biological DIR benchmarks, DUO achieves the best few-shot bMAE and GM on IMDB-WIKI-DIR, AgeDB-DIR, and AAV2-DIR while remaining competitive on few-shot MAE.

</details>


### [60] [Solution-space heterogeneity shapes federated learning dynamics across partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05012)
*Ping Luo,Jiahuan Wang,Ziqing Wen,Tao Sun,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出solution-space PDE-Dirichlet协议，将连续监督响应转换为可复用的解箱（solution bins），并通过最优传输量化客户端间数据分布差异，为联邦偏微分方程（PDE）学习提供了一种可迁移的非独立同分布（non-IID）数据定义。通过理论推导与跨七个PDE任务、三个神经算子家族的系统实验，揭示响应异质性与优化异质性之间的几何机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦科学机器学习允许机构在不集中本地物理数据的情况下训练神经代理模型，但PDE研究中缺乏对non-IID数据的可迁移定义。现有协议根据方程特定规则划分坐标、系数、边界条件或几何，难以在不同PDE任务间通用。因此需要一种通用的、可量化的non-IID数据定义框架。

Method: 提出solution-space PDE-Dirichlet协议：(1) 将连续监督响应转换为可复用的解箱；(2) 通过解箱几何上的最优传输量化客户端间的实际分离程度；(3) 推导群体分配异质性与Dirichlet浓度之间的精确逆关系；(4) 建立响应异质性引发梯度分歧、局部更新离散和参数发散的条件。实验覆盖七个受控与公开PDE任务、三个神经算子家族和五个随机种子。

Result: 较低的Dirichlet浓度一致地增加实现的解距离和优化异质性。最终误差退化程度依任务而定：最大影响出现在低粘度Burgers方程，在最异质设置下最终误差差达4.157个百分点；额外的通信或更平滑的动力学可缩小最终差距，尽管参数分离仍然持续存在。

Conclusion: 该工作区分了可复现的几何机制与任务相关的泛化结果，为评估non-IID联邦PDE学习提供了统一基础。

Abstract: Federated scientific machine learning enables institutions to train neural surrogates without centralizing local physical data, yet studies of partial differential equations (PDEs) lack a transferable definition of non-independent and identically distributed data. Existing protocols partition coordinates, coefficients, boundary conditions, or geometries according to equation-specific rules. Here, we introduce solution-space PDE-Dirichlet, a protocol that converts continuous supervised responses into reusable solution bins and quantifies the realized separation between clients through optimal transport over the geometry of these bins. We derive an exact inverse relation between population allocation heterogeneity and the Dirichlet concentration, and we establish conditions under which response heterogeneity induces gradient disagreement, local-update dispersion, and parameter divergence. Across seven controlled and public PDE tasks, three neural-operator families, and five random seeds, a lower concentration consistently increases the realized solution distance and optimization heterogeneity. The degradation in final error is task dependent: the largest effect occurs for low-viscosity Burgers, reaching 4.157 percentage points under the most heterogeneous setting, whereas additional communication or smoother dynamics can reduce the final gap despite persistent parameter separation. These results distinguish a reproducible geometric mechanism from task-dependent generalization outcomes and provide a common basis for evaluating non-IID federated PDE learning.

</details>


### [61] [Amortizing Scaling Law Construction Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05016)
*Abhash Kumar Jha,Diana Alexandra Onuţu,Neeratyoy Mallik,Swagatam Haldar,Sam Laing,Niccolò Ajroldi,Shiwei Liu,Joaquin Vanschoren,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于贝叶斯优化的高效缩放定律构建框架，将数据收集建模为优化问题，通过渐进扩展计算预算及代理-幻想评估来减少大规模训练网格所需的计算开销，可在节省10-100倍计算量的情况下逼近全网格训练的缩放定律拟合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统缩放定律推导需要在超参数、token预算和参数数量上进行全面的网格搜索与训练，计算成本极高；然而最终拟合缩放定律仅需要最佳损失前沿数据，绝大多数训练配置被丢弃，造成了巨大的计算浪费。

Method: 将数据收集过程形式化为贝叶斯优化问题；在采集过程中渐进扩展计算预算（与实践中按计算量排序的配置评估方式一致）；使用代理模型对未观测配置进行"幻想"（surrogate-fantasized）评估，以恢复更广泛的实验网格信息。同时引入用于在有限计算预算下比较不同缩放定律拟合方法的评估指标。

Result: 渐进扩展计算预算可显著提高恢复效率；结合代理-幻想评估能恢复完整实验网格，使得无需训练所有配置即可准确拟合缩放定律；在完整稠密网格上可获得近乎一致的拟合效果，计算节省达10-100倍。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了高效、经济可行的缩放定律构建，大幅降低了大规模基础模型设计中的计算开销，为缩放定律研究提供了实用的方法论工具。

Abstract: Scaling laws guide the design choices for training large foundation models, but deriving them involves training an exhaustive grid over hyperparameters, token budgets, and parameter counts, which is computationally expensive. Fitting a scaling law, however, only requires the best-loss frontier across compute scales, discarding most of the trained configurations. We propose a framework for efficient scaling law construction that formulates data collection as a Bayesian optimization problem, and introduce metrics for comparing scaling law fitting methods under constrained compute budgets. We find that progressively expanding the compute budget during acquisition, mirroring the compute-ordered evaluation of configurations in practice, substantially improves recovery efficiency. Augmenting the observed configurations with surrogate-fantasized evaluations then recovers the broader experimental grid, allowing accurate scaling law fitting without training every configuration. Together, these can closely match scaling law fits over a full dense grid at computational savings of up to $10\text{--}100\times$.

</details>


### [62] [Deep Microcompression: Structured Pruning and Bit-packed Quantization for Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05081)
*Opegbemi Matthias Busoye,Tolulope Matthew Busoye,Eghonghon-aye Eigbe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Deep Microcompression (DMC)，一个面向裸机微控制器的硬件感知深度学习推理流水线，集成了结构化剪枝、量化感知训练和定长比特打包，在LeNet-5上实现55.8倍权重压缩（准确率98.77%），生成无依赖的C库并具有确定性延迟。在RP2040（Cortex-M0+）上相比TensorFlow Lite将二进制体积减小3倍且准确率相当，并首次在仅有2KB SRAM的ATmega328P上实现了标准CNN部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裸机微控制器（尤其是资源极度受限的设备）上进行深度学习推理面临着极端的存储和内存约束，此前在仅有2KB SRAM的设备上运行标准CNN被认为不可行。现有工作缺乏面向这类超低资源硬件的高效压缩与部署流水线。

Method: DMC采用硬件感知的三阶段流水线：1) 结构化剪枝（structured pruning）减少冗余连接；2) 量化感知训练（quantization-aware training）在训练过程中模拟量化误差；3) 定长比特打包（fixed-length bit-packing）优化存储布局。最终生成无第三方依赖的C代码，保证确定性延迟。

Result: 在LeNet-5上达到55.8倍权重压缩比，保持98.77%准确率；在RP2040（Cortex-M0+）上相比TensorFlow Lite，二进制体积减小3倍而准确率持平；首次在ATmega328P（仅2KB SRAM）上实现了标准CNN的成功部署。

Conclusion: DMC证明了通过硬件感知的协同设计（压缩算法与目标硬件约束紧密结合），标准CNN可以在此前被认为不可能的极端资源受限微控制器上运行，为边缘超低功耗AI推理开辟了新可能。

Abstract: This paper introduces Deep Microcompression (DMC), a hardware-aware pipeline for deep learning inference on bare-metal microcontrollers. DMC integrates structured pruning, quantization-aware training, and fixed-length bit-packing to achieve a 55.8$\times$ weight compression ratio on LeNet-5 (98.77\% accuracy), generating a dependency-free C library with deterministic latency. On the RP2040 (Cortex-M0+), DMC reduces binary size by 3$\times$ versus TensorFlow Lite while matching its accuracy. Critically, DMC enables the first documented deployment of a standard CNN on the ATmega328P, a device constrained to 2KB SRAM, previously considered infeasible for CNN inference.

</details>


### [63] [NEAT-POCKET: Pocket-Conditioned Autoregressive 3D Molecular Generation with a Neighborhood-Guided Set Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05097)
*Roxane Axel Jacob,Daniel Rose,Thierry Langer,Johannes Kirchmair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NEAT-POCKET是一个基于自回归NEAT模型扩展的pocket条件3D分子生成框架，能在蛋白质结合口袋内逐原子生成分子，保持原子置换不变性并显式建模氢原子，在CrossDocked和SPINDR数据集上实现具有竞争力的基于结构的生成性能，同时采样速度显著快于现有基线，并天然支持pocket条件下的片段补全任务，可用于先导化合物优化和骨架扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速早期药物发现，利用AI驱动的从头分子设计直接在靶点蛋白结合口袋内生成新颖配体。

Method: 将自回归NEAT模型扩展为NEAT-POCKET，使其能够感知蛋白质口袋环境，在口袋内逐原子生成分子，保持原子置换不变性并显式建模氢原子；同时利用该框架自然支持pocket条件下的片段补全。

Result: 在CrossDocked和SPINDR数据集上的基准测试表明，NEAT-POCKET实现了具有竞争力的基于结构的分子生成性能，同时比现有基线的采样速度快得多，且能有效完成pocket条件下的片段补全任务。

Conclusion: NEAT-POCKET是一个快速、灵活且实用的基于结构的药物设计框架，兼具全分子生成与片段补全能力，对先导优化和药物研发流程具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: AI-driven de novo molecular design offers a promising route to accelerate early-stage drug discovery by generating novel ligands directly within target protein binding pockets. We present NEAT-POCKET, a pocket-conditioned extension of the autoregressive NEAT model for 3D molecular generation. NEAT-POCKET generates molecules atom by atom in protein pocket environments while preserving atom permutation invariance and explicitly modeling hydrogen atoms. Benchmarks on the CrossDocked and SPINDR datasets show that NEAT-POCKET achieves competitive structure-based generation performance while sampling substantially faster than existing baselines. Beyond full-molecule generation, NEAT-POCKET naturally enables pocket-conditioned fragment completion, a task directly relevant to lead optimization and scaffold elaboration. These results position NEAT-POCKET as a fast, flexible, and practical framework for structure-based drug design.

</details>


### [64] [A Comparative Study of Counterfactual Explainers for Graph Neural Networks Enabling Multiple Types of Graph Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05113)
*Maria Myrto Villia,Filippos Gouidis,Theodore Patkos,Panos Trahanias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对六种最先进的图结构数据反事实解释模型进行了全面比较，涵盖二分类和多分类的图与节点分类任务，使用多样化数据集和多种定量及定性指标评估其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构数据的反事实解释旨在找到使模型预测改变的最小且现实的图修改方案。尽管已有支持增删边的方法出现，但缺乏通用且高效的方法，且现有方法在解释规模、覆盖率和质量之间存在权衡。因此需要系统比较各方法的优劣，以指导该领域未来研究。

Method: 作者对六种最先进（SOTA）的反事实解释模型在多个真实世界和合成数据集上进行对比实验，覆盖二分类和多分类的图分类及节点分类任务，并采用多种定量和定性指标进行评估。

Result: 研究揭示了六种不同方法的性能差异，指出了每种方法在不同任务和数据场景下的优势与不足，特别是在解释规模、覆盖率和解释质量之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为图反事实解释领域提供了系统的基准评估框架，明确了现有方法的适用边界和局限，为未来开发更通用、高效的图反事实解释方法指明了方向。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations for graph-structured data seek to determine minimal and realistic modifications required in an input graph to alter a model's prediction to a predefined output. Although counterfactual explainers that support modifying the graph by both adding and removing edges have recently emerged, there is still a lack of general and efficient methods, especially when considering the quality of the generated explanations. Moreover, the problem remains far from solved, as existing methods exhibit different strengths and weaknesses, often trading off between explanation size, coverage and quality. For this reason, it is important to identify where each method performs well and where it falls short, so as to guide future research in the field. Thus, our study compares six state-of-the-art (SOTA) models on a diverse set of real-world and synthetic datasets, covering both binary and multi-class graph and node classification tasks, and evaluates their performance using diverse quantitative and qualitative metrics.

</details>


### [65] [Single-Query Black-Box Calibration Auditing via Logit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05125)
*Roman Plaud,Antoine Saillenfest,Matthieu Labeau,Thomas Bonald,Willem Waegeman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在商业LLM API隐藏输出概率的情况下，利用logit_bias参数以每样本仅需一次查询即可精确评估概率阈值的方法，并据此引入了一种对二分类任务真实校准误差（True Calibration Error）的一致性估计器，为黑盒基础模型的审计提供了高效框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为零样本分类器的安全部署依赖于对其校准性能的评估，但商业API提供商越来越多地隐藏连续输出概率，导致标准校准指标无法使用。作者旨在绕过这种不透明性，在受限的API访问条件下仍能进行可靠的校准审计。

Method: 作者证明任何暴露logit_bias参数的LLM API都可以通过数学操作，使用每样本单次查询来精确评估概率阈值。基于这一机制，提出了一种新的、可证明一致性的True Calibration Error估计器。

Result: 该方法提供了对黑盒基础模型进行高效审计的框架，能够在仅有一查询访问每样本的约束下实现校准误差的一致估计。

Conclusion: 利用logit_bias参数进行数学操纵，可以在无法直接获取输出概率的商用LLM API上实现精确且高效的校准评估，为黑盒模型的可靠性审计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Evaluating the calibration of Large Language Models (LLMs) is critical for their safe deployment as zero-shot classifiers. Yet, commercial API providers increasingly hide the continuous output probabilities required by standard calibration metrics. To bypass this opacity, we demonstrate that any LLM API exposing a logit\_bias parameter can be mathematically manipulated to evaluate exact probability thresholds using strictly one query per sample. Leveraging this mechanism, we introduce a novel and provably consistent estimator of the True Calibration Error for binary tasks. Our approach therefore provides an efficient framework for auditing black-box foundation models.

</details>


### [66] [MomentQuant: an even more minimalist interval method with linear time complexity for time series classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05136)
*Johann Faouzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MomentQuant算法，通过优化实现和引入Cornish-Fisher展开近似分位数两种方式，在保持预测精度基本不变的前提下显著加速了区间型时间序列分类算法Quant，特别适合推理频繁的实际应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分类算法长期面临预测性能与计算成本之间的权衡。Quant算法虽在准确性和速度上表现优异，但其依赖精确分位数计算需要对时间序列进行排序，这一步骤的计算开销在推理频繁的真实应用中尤为突出。作者希望通过优化实现和使用近似分位数来进一步降低计算复杂度。

Method: 提出两项改进：(1) 对原始Quant算法进行更优化的工程实现，不改变算法逻辑；(2) 设计新算法MomentQuant，用Cornish-Fisher展开基于矩信息推导近似分位数，从而省去对时间序列排序的步骤，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 实验证据表明：优化的Quant实现比原始版本更快；MomentQuant比优化的Quant实现更快，仅在预测性能上有微小下降。这些速度优势在推理（inference）远多于训练的实际场景中尤为有价值和相关。

Conclusion: 通过算法实现的工程优化和基于Cornish-Fisher展开的近似分位数策略，可以在几乎不损失预测精度的前提下大幅提升Quant算法速度，使该算法更适合计算资源受限或推理高频的真实应用场景。

Abstract: Time series data is very common in many real-world applications and in numerous domains, with increasing interest for automated information extraction using machine learning. One of these subfields is time series classification, which consists in assigning a label to each new, unseen time series. Many algorithms have been developed over the past decades, with the trade-off between predictive performance and computational cost being consistently discussed. Quant, an interval-based algorithm extracting quantiles from recursive, fixed, dyadic intervals, was shown to achieve high accuracy, while being very fast. We propose two changes to make this algorithm even faster. The first one is a better optimized implementation of the exact same algorithm. The second one is to derive approximate quantiles, using the Cornish-Fisher expansion, instead of exact quantiles. This change removes the necessity to sort the time series, leading to a smaller computational complexity. We call this novel algorithm MomentQuant. We provide evidence that our implementation of Quant is faster than the original one, and that MomentQuant is even faster than our implementation of Quant, at the cost of a tiny decrease in predictive performance. These improvements are especially relevant for real-life applications, where inference is performed much more often than training.

</details>


### [67] [From 80x to 385x: A Best-Matching-Unit Search at the L2 Roof, Measured Against a Symmetrically Tuned Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05138)
*Andrew James Amos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了同时调优一个新型SOM算法（SparseBin）及其基线算法cuSPARSE的对比研究。通过四个优化杠杆（tile size、tile成员聚类、神经元轴分块、向量化加载），SparseBin每epoch速度提升5.6-10.1x（地图尺寸32x32到512x512），对CUDA实现的优势从约80x扩大到约385x；同时cuSPARSE也获得2-3x加速。调优后的kernel达到L2带宽屋顶的77%，其他单元处于40-65%，理论上剩余优化空间仅约1.3x，属于终局性结果而非中间节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出GPU实现对比通常存在不对称性——一方经过作者精心调优，另一方仅按原样运行，导致对比结果不公平。本文试图通过同时调优双方算法来提供更为公平的基准对比，并探索新型SOM算法SparseBin的真实性能边界。

Method: 通过四个优化杠杆对SOM训练中占主导的best-matching-unit（BMU）搜索进行调优：(1) tile size（瓦片尺寸）、(2) tile-membership clustering（瓦片成员聚类）、(3) neuron-axis chunking（神经元轴分块）、(4) vectorised loads（向量化加载）。同时将每个杠杆中能在cuSPARSE侧找到对应物的优化也施加于cuSPARSE，实现双方的公平调优。

Result: SparseBin在32x32到512x512地图尺寸下每epoch获得5.6-10.1x加速，对先前CUDA实现的优势从约80x提升至约385x。cuSPARSE经对应优化后加速2-3x。调优后的kernel达到L2带宽屋顶的77%，其余单元维持在40-65%，理论上限剩余最多约1.3x的优化空间。

Conclusion: 该结果是终端性结论而非中间节点：所有未测试的杠杆要么被带宽上界所约束，要么经测量为无效。研究强调了GPU对比中双向调优的重要性，并证明了即使在公平条件下SparseBin仍保持压倒性优势。

Abstract: Comparisons between GPU implementations are usually asymmetric: one side is tuned by its author, the other is run as found. I report a programme that tuned both a novel SOM algorithm (SparseBin) and the baseline algorithm it was being compared to (cuSPARSE). The best-matching-unit search that dominates self-organizing map training was tuned through four levers - tile size, tile-membership clustering, neuron-axis chunking and vectorised loads - reaching 5.6-10.1x per epoch over the previously published configuration at map sizes from 32x32 to 512x512, and lifting the margin over the CUDA implementation behind our earlier MEDLINE atlases from ~80x to ~385x. cuSPARSE, the implementation SparseBin is compared against, received every lever with an analogue on its side, and became 2-3x faster in the process. The tuned kernel pressed the L2 bandwidth roof at 77% of peak with every other unit at 40-65%, bounding any further lever at ~1.3x - a terminal result rather than a waypoint, and every untested lever was either capped by that bound by construction or measured null.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Stationarity in Time Series: Discovering Causal Structures and Latent Regimes via Markov Blankets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05150)
*Lei Zan,Charles K. Assaad,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RCBNB-MB，一种面向时间序列的因果发现算法，它打破单一、时间一致因果结构的假设，通过识别潜在因果机制（regimes）并分段发现因果图来应对非平稳时序中的机制变化。算法利用马尔可夫毯而非直接父节点以提高鲁棒性，并提供理论保证，实验验证其在模拟数据与真实IT监控数据上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的动态系统常出现机制切换（regime shifts），导致假设静态因果结构的传统时序因果发现方法失效。已有方法大多假定整个时间序列上存在一个稳定的因果关系，这与真实场景不符，因此需要一种能同时识别潜在因果机制并恢复各机制内因果图的方法。

Method: RCBNB-MB采用迭代策略：先对时间序列进行分段以识别潜在的因果机制（每个机制内假设存在稳定因果结构），再在每个机制内进行因果图发现。算法引入马尔可夫毯（Markov Blanket）而非直接父节点实现因果发现，从而提升对因果发现误差的鲁棒性并保留预测信息，同时给出了恢复机制转换与因果图的理论保证。

Result: 在含已知真值的模拟数据集和强调机制变化的真实IT监控数据上进行了广泛实验，结果表明RCBNB-MB在准确检测机制变化及其关联因果图方面系统地优于基线方法。

Conclusion: RCBNB-MB被定位为一个稳健且通用的非平稳时间序列分析框架，有效整合了基于约束与基于噪声的因果发现策略，并借助马尔可夫毯实现了对机制变化下因果结构的可靠恢复。

Abstract: This paper introduces Regime-aware Constraint-Based and Noise-Based causal discovery with Markov Blankets (RCBNB-MB), a novel causal discovery algorithm for time series that relaxes the common assumption of a single, time-consistent causal structure. Time series are typically observed at discrete time points and often exhibit regime changes that challenge the assumption of a static causal structure, a limitation in many real-world dynamic systems. To address this challenge, RCBNB-MB identifies latent causal regimes, defined as subsets of time points within which a stable causal structure holds. The algorithm follows an iterative strategy that segments the time series into regimes and discovers the causal graph within each regime. By leveraging the Markov blanket rather than direct parents, RCBNB-MB gains robustness to errors in causal discovery and preserves predictive information. We provide theoretical guarantees for RCBNB-MB's ability to recover both regime transitions and causal graphs under reasonable assumptions. Furthermore, we validate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on simulated datasets with known ground truth and real-world IT monitoring data, where taking into account regime shifts is critical. Empirical results show that RCBNB-MB systematically outperforms baseline approaches in accurately detecting regime changes and their associated causal graphs, positioning it as a robust and versatile framework for non-stationary time series analysis.

</details>


### [69] [Phase Transition Frequency as a Training Time Predictor of Test Accuracy in ResNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05194)
*Arunan J*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了ResNet微调过程中的离散类别可分性跳变次数是否可作为最终测试精度的预测指标。在75个实验、四个基准（CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, CIFAR-10-C）和三种架构（ResNet-18/50/101）上，发现标准i.i.d.分类基准中跳变次数与最终精度呈强负相关（CIFAR-10: r=-0.84, CIFAR-100: r=-0.87），但在分布偏移压力下相关性显著衰减（TinyImageNet: r=-0.45, CIFAR-10-C: r=-0.19）。跨架构的偏相关分析显示该指标在CIFAR-100上仍保留预测力，但与六种替代训练曲线信号及文献中更先进的测度（有效秩、Hessian尖锐度等）的比较仍不完整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一个廉价、可随标准训练循环实时记录的训练质量探针，用于预测微调后模型的最终测试精度。作者试图回答的核心问题是：类可分性的离散跳变次数——这一宏观训练动态特征——是否携带关于最终泛化能力的有效信息，尤其是在跨架构、跨基准和跨分布偏移场景下是否稳健。

Method: 该论文采用纯实证方法：在75个实验中，对ResNet微调过程监测类可分性的离散跳变次数，将其作为预测变量与最终测试准确率进行相关分析。方法包含：(1) 四个基准（含i.i.d.与分布偏移场景）、三种架构、每配置5-10个种子的系统实验；(2) Pearson相关性分析（含p值与样本量）；(3) 以架构深度为协变量的偏相关分析；(4) 与六种替代训练曲线信号的对比排序；(5) 提供便于嵌入标准训练循环的廉价检测流程。

Result: 主要结果：i.i.d.基准上跳变次数与最终精度呈强负相关（CIFAR-10 r=-0.84, p<10^-8, n=30；CIFAR-100 r=-0.87, p<10^-5, n=15）；受压基准上相关性衰减（TinyImageNet r=-0.45, CIFAR-10-C r=-0.19）。控制架构深度后CIFAR-100上偏相关r=-0.69 (p=0.007)保留统计显著性。与六种训练曲线信号对比中，跳变次数在CIFAR-100上最强、CIFAR-10上接近最强，但在两个受压基准上被其他信号超越。其声称适用范围被限定为"分布内训练质量探针"。

Conclusion: 论文的结论是审慎且自限定的：跳变次数是一个在分布内（in-distribution）设置下有价值的训练质量探针，但并非普适的泛化预测器——它在分布偏移下失效。该指标在i.i.d.基准上能提供低成本、可实时记录的预测信号，但其竞争地位相对文献中更先进的测度（如有效秩、Hessian尖锐度、神经坍缩等）尚未建立，这一比较被明确留作开放问题。整体来看，该工作是候选探针家族中的一项实证贡献，而非一个被验证为最优的通用方案。

Abstract: The number of discrete class-separability jumps observed during ResNet finetuning is examined empirically as a predictor of final test accuracy. Across 75 experiments spanning four benchmarks (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and CIFAR-10-C) and three architectures (ResNet-18, ResNet-50, and ResNet-101), with five to ten seeds per configuration, a strong within-dataset negative correlation is obtained on standard i.i.d. classification benchmarks: \(r = -0.84\) on CIFAR-10 (\(p < 10^{-8}\), \(n = 30\)) and \(r = -0.87\) on CIFAR-100 (\(p < 10^{-5}\), \(n = 15\)). Under distributional stress, the relationship attenuates: TinyImageNet yields \(r = -0.45\), and the CIFAR-10-C corruption benchmark yields \(r = -0.19\). Two additional analyses discipline the empirical claim. A partial correlation controlling for architecture depth, treated as a linear covariate, shows that on CIFAR-100 the transition count retains statistically significant predictive power (\(r_{\mathrm{partial}} = -0.69\), \(p = 0.007\)); the corresponding result under the stricter categorical conditioning is not established at \(n = 15\). A comparison against six alternative training-curve signals shows that transition count achieved the strongest correlation among the evaluated signals on CIFAR-100 and one of the strongest on CIFAR-10, but is dominated by other signals on the two stressed benchmarks. The comparison is restricted to training-curve-level signals; comparisons against effective rank, Hessian sharpness, Fisher information, margin, and neural-collapse measures, which are the strongest competitors in the current literature, are not part of the present study and remain open. The observation is presented as an in-distribution training-quality probe among a family of candidate probes, and an inexpensive detection procedure suitable for logging alongside a standard training loop is provided.

</details>


### [70] [Dimension-Adaptive Batched Lipschitz Narrowing Without Knowing the Zooming Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05214)
*Yasong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Count-Adaptive BLiN算法，通过移除ALiN（A-BLiN）中ACE序列对缩放维度d_z的依赖，实现了在d_z未知情况下的最优悔恨与批次复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始A-BLiN算法的ACE（Appropriately Combined Edge-length）序列依赖于缩放维度d_z，当d_z未知时算法无法直接应用。作者希望消除这一依赖。

Method: 新算法不再依赖d_z或缩放常数C_z，而是通过选择仍存活的立方体数量来确定下一个边长（edge length），即"计数自适应"（Count-Adaptive）策略。

Result: Count-Adaptive BLiN在不需要d_z和C_z的情况下，仍达到~O_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})的悔恨界，且批次复杂度为O_d(log log T)。结合原论文定理10的自适应网格下界，当d_z未知时最优批次复杂度仍为Θ_d(log log T)。

Conclusion: 该结果表明即使在缩放维度未知的情况下，BLiN算法仍能保持最优的批次复杂度，进一步增强了算法的自适应性。

Abstract: The Appropriately Combined Edge-length (ACE) sequence in A-BLiN depends on the zooming dimension $d_z$. This note removes that dependence. The next edge length is selected from the number of cubes that survive the preceding elimination. The resulting Count-Adaptive BLiN algorithm does not use $d_z$ or the zooming constant $C_z$, yet it attains $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})$ regret with $\mathcal O_d(\log\log T)$ batches. Together with the adaptive-grid lower bound in Theorem 10 of the original paper, the optimal batch complexity remains $Θ_d(\log\log T)$ when $d_z$ is unknown.

</details>


### [71] [FedDRAW: Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting for Heterogeneous Multi-Institutional Chest Radiograph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05223)
*Maryam Moradpour,Anne-Christin Hauschild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedDRAW（联邦双声誉退火加权法），一种服务器端的聚合方法，用于联邦学习中的医学影像诊断模型训练。它结合数据量先验与客户端-全局参数间的余弦相似度，通过内外双层退火调度来控制聚合权重，解决FedAvg中大型但信息量少的医院主导全局模型的问题。在12个模拟客户端分区场景、两个胸部X光数据集上，FedDRAW在AUC和灵敏度/特异度几何均值上均取得最高平均排名，并经弗里德曼检验与Nemenyi事后分析确认统计显著性优于七个联邦基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许医院在不共享患者数据的情况下协作训练诊断模型，但标准FedAvg按本地样本数比例设置聚合权重，导致数据量少但信息丰富的医院被永久赋予小权重，而数据量大但信息量少的客户端可能主导全局模型，造成诊断偏差，需要更公平、信息驱动的聚合权重方法。

Method: FedDRAW是纯服务器端聚合方法。它将数据规模先验与客户端参数和全局参数之间的余弦相似度结合，采用两个耦合的退火时间表：内部退火调度将客户端声誉从规模先验逐渐转向相似度；外部延迟的softmax逆温度退火调度使权重在训练早期和中后期保持选择性，收敛时放松至均匀。通过声誉重加权实现更均衡的模型聚合。

Result: 在12个模拟客户端分区场景、两个胸部X光数据集（CheXpert和ChestMNIST）上，对比七个联邦基线方法（相同本地训练设置下），FedDRAW在AUC和灵敏度/特异度几何均值（GM）两个指标上均取得八种方法中的最高平均排名。弗里德曼检验配合Nemenyi事后检验确认方法间存在统计显著差异。

Conclusion: 调度两种信号（数据规模先验与参数相似度）而非仅依赖样本计数固定权重，可能有助于减少偏倚的诊断模型，为联邦学习中的聚合权重设计提供了一种更适配不平衡、异构医疗数据分布的新思路。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence models are promising for medical diagnosis, but they require large numbers of unbiased data, which in medicine are distributed across hospitals and cannot be centralized to protect patient privacy. Federated Learning (FL) addresses this, since hospitals train one shared diagnostic model while patient data remain local. Training proceeds in communication rounds, in which each hospital trains the shared model locally and returns it to the server for merging by weighted average. This aggregation weight determines whose institutional knowledge shapes the result. Federated averaging (FedAvg) sets it in proportion to local sample count, so a small but informative hospital is permanently assigned a small influence, andl argest clients could dominate the global model even when they are less informative. We propose Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting (FedDRAW), a server-side aggregation method that combines a data-size prior with the cosine similarity between client and global parameters under two coupled annealing schedules. An inner schedule shifts client reputation from the size prior towards similarity. An outer, deferred annealing schedule on the softmax inverse temperature keeps the weighting selective in the early and middle rounds and relaxes it to uniformity at convergence. We evaluate FedDRAW on 12 simulated client-partition scenarios of two chest radiograph datasets (CheXpert and ChestMNIST), against seven federated baselines under identical local training settings. FedDRAW achieved the highest average rank among all eight methods under both AUC and the geometric mean (GM) of sensitivity and specificity, which a Friedman test with Nemenyi post-hoc analysis confirmed to be a statistically significant difference between the methods. Scheduling two signals, rather than fixing the weights by sample count alone, could enable less biased diagnostic models.

</details>


### [72] [Hessian-based molecular conformation augmentation for a scalable and efficient strategy of machine learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05233)
*Bumju Kwak,Jeonghee Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种基于Hessian的数据增强方案（各向同性高斯位移UniAug和简正模式加权位移ModeAug），通过简单的泰勒展开实现，无需修改训练目标或扩展自动微分图，即可无缝集成到现有MLIP架构中，提升非平衡和平衡数据集上的模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（MLIP）虽能学习势能面（PES）和原子力，但许多实际应用（如振动分析和过渡态搜索）严重依赖PES的Hessian。然而标准MLIP仅以能量和力训练，未充分利用Hessian信息；现有显式将Hessian纳入训练目标的方法需要架构修改且因高阶反向传播引入显著的计算和内存开销。

Method: 提出两种基于Hessian的数据增强方案：UniAug（各向同性高斯位移）和ModeAug（简正模式加权位移）。两种方法均利用简单的泰勒展开引入Hessian信息进行有效增强，保持训练目标不变且不扩展自动微分图，实现即插即用的无缝集成。

Result: 在非平衡和平衡数据集上的全面评估表明，所提方法能提升模型精度，并提供了实用的、面向具体任务的指导准则。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙的数据增强方式将Hessian信息引入MLIP训练，在不改变训练目标或架构的情况下利用二阶导数信息，是一种轻量且有效、可广泛兼容现有训练流程的方案。

Abstract: While machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have successfully learned potential energy surfaces (PES) and atomic forces, many practical applications, such as vibrational analysis and transition state search, rely heavily on the PES Hessian. Yet, standard MLIPs tend to be trained on energy and forces alone, leaving Hessian information largely unexploited. Meanwhile, existing methods that explicitly incorporate the Hessian into training objectives require architectural modifications and introduce significant computational and memory overheads due to higher-order backpropagation. To address these limitations, we propose two Hessian-derived data augmentation schemes: isotropic Gaussian displacement (\textbf{UniAug}) and normal mode-weighted displacement (\textbf{ModeAug}). Both methods utilize simple Taylor expansions, achieving effective augmentation without altering training objectives or extending the autograd graph. This allows seamless, plug-and-play integration with existing architectures and training pipelines. Comprehensive evaluations across non-equilibrium and equilibrium datasets demonstrate that our approach enhances model accuracy while providing practical, task-specific guidelines.

</details>


### [73] [GLASS: Graph-Language Alignment with Spherical Scoring for Transferable Graph-Level Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05253)
*Xudong Wang,Chris Ding,Tongxin Li,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLASS是一个用于图级异常检测（GLAD）的框架，通过在单位超球面上进行图-语言对齐实现稳健的跨域迁移。它通过多切片软余弦目标将结构感知图编码器与指令感知文本嵌入对齐，利用Graph Descriptor Prompt (GraphDP)序列化图的局部、全局和语义属性，并通过Matryoshka表示切片强制多尺度一致性。异常评分采用球面多模态评分（SMS），在球面上用von Mises-Fisher核密度估计建模异常检测，在十二个基准和三个元域上取得最优平均AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图级异常检测（GLAD）面临跨域迁移能力不足的挑战：现有方法通常针对特定领域设计，难以在不同领域的图数据间有效泛化。作者希望构建统一的表示空间，利用图与语言的对齐实现可迁移的异常检测，使模型无需目标域训练数据即可跨域工作。

Method: GLASS包含三个核心组件：(1) 图-语言对齐：在单位超球面上通过多切片软余弦目标对齐结构感知图编码器与指令感知文本编码器；(2) GraphDP桥接：将局部、全局和语义图属性序列化为紧凑的Graph Decriptor Prompt，并结合Matryoshka表示切片实现多尺度一致性；(3) 球面多模态评分（SMS）：将异常检测形式化为对齐超球面上的密度估计，在图文嵌入空间分别使用von Mises-Fisher核密度估计器，该概率公式可退化为高浓度极限下的角度kNN评分，并在零样本和少样本场景下通过参考集校准实现跨域迁移。

Result: 在十二个基准数据集和三个元域上，GLASS相比近期先进的GLAD基线方法取得了最佳平均AUROC和最优排名，并展示了有效的跨域迁移能力（包括零样本和少样本设置）。

Conclusion: GLASS验证了图-语言对齐框架在GLAD任务上的有效性和可迁移性。通过将结构信息与语义信息在统一的球面表示空间中融合，并以概率密度估计方式对异常进行评分，GLASS为跨域图级异常检测提供了强有力的通用范式。

Abstract: We introduce GLASS, a framework for graph-level anomaly detection (GLAD) that achieves robust cross-domain transferability through graph-language alignment on the unit hypersphere. GLASS builds a unified representation space by aligning a structure-aware graph encoder with an instruction-aware text embedding via a multi-slice soft cosine objective. Our framework serializes local, global, and semantic graph properties into a compact Graph Descriptor Prompt (GraphDP), creating a text bridge that enables domain-agnostic anomaly scoring. By enforcing multi-scale consistency through Matryoshka representation slices, the model captures anomalous deviations at multiple levels of granularity. For scoring, we formulate anomaly detection as density estimation on the aligned hypersphere and introduce Spherical Multi-Modal Scoring (SMS), which instantiates von Mises-Fisher kernel density estimators in both graph and text embedding spaces. This probabilistic formulation recovers angular k-nearest-neighbor scoring as a high-concentration limiting case and provides a principled fusion of structural and semantic anomaly signals. The shared text embedding space further serves as a cross-domain bridge: by encoding a target domain's GraphDP without target-domain training data, GLASS performs zero-shot anomaly detection, and with only a handful of normal examples, few-shot adaptation via reference-set calibration. Across twelve benchmarks and three meta-domains, GLASS obtains the best average AUROC and rank compared with recent advanced GLAD baselines and enables effective cross-domain transfer.

</details>


### [74] [How to Speculate about Uncertainty in Agentic Coding? A Draft-Model Gate Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05274)
*Konstantin Grotov,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Speculative Uncertainty (SU) 方法，通过一个小型开源草稿模型对黑盒 LLM agent 已生成的轨迹进行单次前向评分（逆转推测解码过程），从输出 token 中恢复预测性失败信号，无需访问 logits、权重、激活值或重复采样。该方法提取推理与行动 span 分离的相位感知特征并校准到可验证目标，产生可供下游策略直接消费的失败似然分数。实验在 Qwen3-Coder-480B 和 Claude 3.5 Sonnet 上实现预执行否决门控策略，将执行错误率降低 6-8 个百分点，token 成本降低 14-19%，且无需重训即可迁移到分布外基准并跨 agent 模型泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程场景中部署的 LLM agents 失败代价高昂：它们常常自信地犯错，坏动作只有在昂贵的执行和重试之后才能被识别。现有方法要么需要访问模型内部信息（如 logits 或激活值），要么需要重复采样，这在黑盒和成本受限的实际部署中不可行。因此需要一种仅从输出 token 即可恢复失败预测信号的方法。

Method: SU 方法的核心思路是"逆转推测解码"：利用一个小型开源草稿模型，对 agent 已生成的完整轨迹执行单次前向传播，计算推测性交叉似然（speculative cross-likelihoods）。随后从中提取相位感知特征，将推理（reasoning）与行动（action）span 分开处理，并将这些特征针对可验证的目标进行校准，最终输出一个失败似然分数。

Result: 在 Qwen3-Coder-480B 和 Claude 3.5 Sonnet 两个软件工程 agent 上部署"预执行否决门控"策略后，SU 将执行错误率降低 6-8 个百分点，token 成本降低 14-19%。该方法无需重新训练即可迁移到分布外基准，并能跨不同 agent 模型泛化。

Conclusion: SU 证明了一种仅基于输出 token 的黑盒失败预测信号的可行性，为下游策略（如路由、人工干预或额外测试时计算）提供了直接可消费的信号，同时保持了低成本、高可迁移性和跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: LLM agents deployed for software engineering fail expensively: they act confidently wrong, and bad actions are recognized only after costly execution and retry. We present Speculative Uncertainty (SU), a method that recovers a predictive failure signal for a black-box agent from its output tokens alone, with no access to logits, weights, activations, or repeated sampling. Inverting speculative decoding, a small open-weight draft model scores the agent's already-generated trajectory in a single forward pass. From these speculative cross-likelihoods we extract phase-aware features by separating the reasoning and action spans, and calibrate them against a verifiable objective. SU produces a failure-likelihood score that any downstream policy, such as routing, human intervention, or extra test-time compute, can consume directly. To show the signal is actionable, we instantiate one such policy, a pre-execution veto gate, on software engineering agents Qwen3-Coder-480B and closed-source Claude 3.5 Sonnet, cutting execution error rate by 6-8 percentage points and token cost by 14-19% in deployment, transferring to out-of-distribution benchmarks without retraining, and generalizing across agent models.

</details>


### [75] [How Does mHC Use Its Residual Streams? Selective Routing and Near-Identity Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05309)
*Pengxiang Zhao,Xing Li,Xianzhi Yu,Wei Guo,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了DeepSeek-V4-Flash中四流残差通路（采用Hyper-Connections及其流形约束变体mHC）的实际使用情况，发现读写路由集中但随层深变化，残差混合主要发生在浅层，深层各流基本独立传输。通过针对性干预实验证明晚期混合器几乎无益（置换身份仅增加1.9%困惑度），而早期混合器功能关键（置换身份增加41%困惑度），模型的站点特定结构比token级变化更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyper-Connections/mHC将残差通路从单流扩展到n流，理论上赋予模型更大的表达灵活性，但训练后的模型究竟如何使用这一容量尚不清楚：块如何读取和写入各流、残差通路混合流的程度以及各流是否承载不同表征，这些问题有待系统分析。

Method: 在DeepSeek-V4-Flash的四流残差通路中，采用有效流数（effective stream counts）、跨流残差权重（cross-stream residual weights）和流间余弦相似度（inter-stream cosine similarity）三种指标分析。随后进行针对性干预实验：用身份映射替换混合器、将混合器固定到诊断均值、仅保留每token前三大路由权重等，评估对C4困惑度和六任务平均分数的影响。

Result: 读写路由集中但随深度变化，注意力和FFN站点通常有效使用约两条流，主导流跨层变化且表征方向性区分明显。残差混合程度适中且主要发生在浅层（第22-42层各流基本独立传递）。用身份替换晚期混合器仅使C4困惑度增加1.9%且六任务均分不变；而替换早期混合器使困惑度增加41%。将早期混合器固定到诊断均值仅增加0.2%困惑度并降低0.25个百分点均分；每站点仅保留三大路由权重最多增加2.7%困惑度并改变至多0.4分均分。

Conclusion: 训练模型仅实现了四流mHC所提供的一部分灵活性：大多数模块并不真正需要全部四条流，深层残差混合带来的可测量收益很小。模型的结构冗余高于token级动态性的重要程度，这为更高效的架构设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections and their manifold-constrained variant mHC widen a residual pathway from one stream to n, yet how trained models use this capacity remains unclear: how broadly blocks read and write, how strongly the residual pathway mixes streams, and whether the streams carry distinct representations. We examine these properties in the four-stream residual pathway of DeepSeek-V4-Flash using effective stream counts, cross-stream residual weights, and inter-stream cosine similarity. Read/write routing is concentrated but varies across depth: a typical attention or FFN site effectively uses about two streams, while the dominant stream changes across layers and the representations remain directionally distinct. Residual mixing is modest and occurs primarily in early layers; in layers 22-42, the pathway mostly carries each stream forward separately. Targeted interventions establish the functional significance of these patterns. Replacing the late mixers by identity increases C4 perplexity by only 1.9% and preserves the six-task average score, whereas replacing the early mixers increases perplexity by 41%. Fixing each early mixer to its C4 diagnostic mean increases perplexity by only 0.2% and reduces the average score by 0.25 percentage points, showing that its site-specific structure matters more than its token-wise variation on the evaluated metrics. Likewise, retaining the three largest routing weights per token at every site increases perplexity by at most 2.7% and changes the average score by at most 0.4 points. Thus, the studied model realizes only part of the flexibility afforded by four-stream mHC: individual blocks rarely require all four streams, and late residual mixing provides little measured benefit.

</details>


### [76] [Optimal Rates for Agentic Networked Information Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05318)
*MohammadHossein Bateni,Zahra Hadizadeh,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Shayan Taherijam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了网络化学习中的信息聚合问题（基于Kearns, Roth和Ryu的开创性工作），并闭合了他们遗留的间隙：在M-覆盖路径中，多余均方误差的正确速率在深度达到M²前是常数，之后为Θ(M²/D)。作者改进了循环实例的下界至Ω(√(M/D))，构造了深度D≥M²的M-覆盖路径的下界实例Ω(M²/D)，并证明了对于任意固定分布，多余误差沿路径呈几何收缩（排除单一实例在所有深度见证多项式下界的可能）。同时将相同的最优速率推广到逻辑回归分类（logit传递模型）中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kearns等人提出的网络化学习模型捕捉了代理式AI（agentic AI）的核心模式：每个智能体只能看到部分数据并将其结论传递给下游智能体。他们给出了多余误差的上界O(M/√D)和下界Ω(M/D)（当D<M²时），但这两个界之间存在明显间隙。作者的目标是确定该问题的精确最优速率，并检验该结果是否适用于逻辑分类问题。

Method: 1) 对循环实例进行更精细的分析，将下界从Ω(M/D)改进为Ω(√(M/D))（D<M²时）。2) 为每个深度D≥M²构造M-覆盖路径的实例，证明多余误差下界为Ω(M²/D)，并统一给出D<M²时的常数量级下界。3) 证明对任意固定分布，多余误差沿路径几何收缩，从而排除单一实例在任意深度均给出多项式下界的可能性。4) 将回归结果迁移到Bateni等人的logit传递（BCE损失）逻辑分类模型中，证明协同的最优上界O(M²/D)，并展示在构造的实例上逻辑路径同最小二乘路径一致（至多相差一个重缩放）。

Result: 作者闭合了Kearns等人遗留的间隙：正确速率为深度小于M²时是常数（Ω(1)∩O(1)），深度D≥M²时是Θ(M²/D)。循环实例下界改进到Ω(√(M/D))；对所有D≥M²构造了达到Ω(M²/D)下界的实例；逻辑分类（BCE损失，logit传递）中也获得了相同的最优速率Θ(M²/D)。

Conclusion: 网络化学习中信息聚合的精确误差速率被完整刻画：前M²层内误差不会显著恶化（常数级），之后以M²/D的速率衰减。该结果同时适用于MSE损失的线性回归与BCE损失的逻辑回归。此外，固定分布下的几何收缩性质表明任何单个实例都无法在广泛的深度范围内同时达到下界，多实例分析是必要的。

Abstract: Building on the pioneering paper of Kearns, Roth, and Ryu (SODA'26), we study information aggregation in a networked learning model. The model captures a central pattern in agentic AI: each agent sees only part of the data and passes on only its own conclusion. Their model considers a linear regression problem with the mean squared error (MSE) loss. Agents sit in a DAG and each sees only a subset of the features and its parents' predictions, fits a linear predictor, and passes only its prediction forward. The benchmark is the full-feature learner that sees all raw features. A path of depth $D$ is $M$-covered if every block of $M$ consecutive agents collectively sees all raw features. Kearns, Roth, and Ryu proved that the excess mean squared error of the last agent on such a path is $O(M/\sqrt D)$, and gave a cyclic instance with excess error $Ω(M/D)$ for $D<M^2$.
  We close this gap: the correct rate is constant up to depth $M^2$, and $Θ(M^2/D)$ beyond it. We first give a sharper analysis of the cyclic instance and improve its lower bound to $Ω(\sqrt{M/D})$ for $D<M^2$. We then construct, for every depth $D\ge M^2$, an $M$-covered path of depth $D$ with excess error $Ω(M^2/D)$. The same instance gives the constant lower bound for all $D < M^2$. We also show that for any fixed distribution the excess error contracts geometrically along the path, ruling out any single instance that witnesses any polynomial lower bound at every depth.
  Finally, we prove the same optimal rate for logistic classification in the logit-passing model of Bateni et al., which considers the binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss. The same improved upper bound of $O(M^2/D)$ holds, and we transfer all the regression lower bounds by showing that on those examples the logistic path follows the least-squares path up to rescaling.

</details>


### [77] [Embedded Graph Flows for Categorical Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05328)
*Ethan Ma,Zihan Wang,Chris Siu Yeung Chow,Xinguo Feng,Qingqing Li,Rui Jiang,Naipeng Dong,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Embedded Graph Flows (EGF)，一种为图生成模型学习节点和边类别连续嵌入的生成式模型。通过置换等变图Transformer将高斯噪声逐渐传输到学习到的类别嵌入端点，并用终端读出层映射回离散图类别。在QM9基准上，EGF在所有四个指标上均优于DiGress和GruM，FCD达到0.150（对比DiGress 0.717和GruM 0.812）；在ZINC250k更大分子数据集上也取得了最低的NSPDK MMD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图生成器通常将类别编码为固定的one-hot向量，这会给类别施加人为的几何结构（各类别等距），无法捕捉类别间的语义关系，也缺乏对节点顺序的不变性。传统的基于流或扩散的类别图生成方法难以有效地在离散类别空间中建模连续的生成过程。

Method: 提出Embedded Graph Flows (EGF)：1) 为节点和边类别学习连续的嵌入表示作为生成端点；2) 利用置换等变的图Transformer将高斯噪声流式地传输/推送到这些学习到的嵌入端点（类似连续流模型）；3) 通过终端读出层将连续嵌入映射回离散图类别，从而完成图生成。

Result: 在QM9分子数据集上，EGF在所有四个报告指标上均取得最佳结果，FCD为0.150，优于类别扩散基线DiGress（0.717）和桥接基线GruM（0.812）。在更大的ZINC250k数据集上，EGF在NSPDK核的最大均值差异（MMD）上取得最低值，与参考分子的局部子结构高度一致。

Conclusion: 通过学习类别的连续嵌入而非使用固定one-hot编码，并结合置换等变的图流生成框架，EGF能有效生成具有连贯结构且与参考分布高度一致的类别化图（分子图），在多个分子生成基准上达到最优或竞争力强的性能。

Abstract: Generating categorical graphs requires choosing node and edge types that form a coherent structure without depending on node order. Many graph generators encode categories as fixed one-hot vectors, which can impose an artificial geometry in which categories are equidistant. We propose Embedded Graph Flows (EGF), a generative model that learns continuous embeddings for node and unordered-edge categories and transports Gaussian noise towards these learnt endpoints using a permutation-equivariant graph transformer. A terminal readout maps the embeddings back to discrete graph categories. Across molecular benchmarks, EGF achieved competitive performance. On QM9, EGF gives the best result on all four reported metrics among the three methods, including a Fréchet ChemNet Distance (FCD) of 0.150, compared with 0.717 for the categorical-diffusion baseline DiGress and 0.812 for the bridge-based baseline GruM. When applied to larger molecules in ZINC250k, EGF retains the lowest maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) using the neighbourhood subgraph pairwise distance kernel (NSPDK), indicating close agreement with the local substructures of the reference molecules. Our code is available at https://github.com/Trusted-System-Lab/EGF.

</details>


### [78] [Distill Globally, Adapt Locally: Reasoning Distillation and Product-Type Test-Time Training for Scalable Trade-Up Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05363)
*Siliang Liu,Mohammad Ghasemi,Sapan Patel,Amin Banitalebi-Dehkordi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两级别（two-level）框架，将大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力蒸馏为高效的非生成式学生模型，用于商品升级推荐（trade-up recommendation）。Level 1通过检索增强的少样本LLM教师生成结构化标签和自然语言理由，训练紧凑的嵌入对分类器；Level 2通过产品类型测试时训练（PT-TTT）微调轻量级类别适配器，进一步提升决策边界精度。最终方案无需LLM调用，仅依赖768维商品嵌入，实现约5,000倍加速和10,000倍成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能够推理商品升级关系中的细微差异，但若直接将LLM应用于数亿商品对（pair）的推理在操作上不可行，亟需一种既能保留LLM推理能力又能在超大规模数据上高效运行的方案。

Method: 提出两级别框架：(1) Level 1：采用检索增强的少样本LLM教师，生成结构化关系标签与自然语言理由；通过对齐与对比学习目标，将理由蒸馏到紧凑的嵌入对分类器（15.5M参数、四分类）中；推理时仅需两个预计算的768维商品嵌入，无需LLM调用或文本生成。(2) Level 2：引入产品类型测试时训练（PT-TTT），利用少样本演示在冻结学生模型上优化轻量级类别特定适配器。

Result: 在8,352对人类标注基准上，推理蒸馏的15.5M参数四分类学生模型达到AUC 0.924（95% CI [0.918, 0.929]），优于仅标签训练的学生的0.912。PT-TTT将AUC从0.924提升至0.941，平均精度从0.920提升至0.940。在100K对代理目录上，单台八GPU机器上蒸馏学生比直接LLM推理快约5,000倍，估算成本低约10,000倍。

Conclusion: 通过"教师理由蒸馏+产品类型级测试时适配"的两级架构，可以在不牺牲推理质量的前提下，将LLM的升级推荐能力规模化部署至海量商品对场景，兼顾精度、速度与成本。

Abstract: Trade-up recommendation identifies higher-quality alternatives that preserve a customer's purchase intent while offering upgraded benefits. Large language models (LLMs) can reason about such distinctions, but applying them directly to hundreds of millions of product pairs is operationally impractical. We introduce a two-level framework that distills LLM reasoning into an efficient non-generative student and adapts its decision boundary to product-type-specific trade-up criteria. At Level 1, a retrieval-augmented few-shot LLM teacher generates structured relation labels and natural-language rationales. These rationales supervise a compact embedding-pair classifier through alignment and contrastive objectives; at inference, the student uses only two precomputed 768-dimensional product embeddings, with no LLM calls or text generation. On a fixed human-annotated benchmark of 8,352 pairs, a 15.5M-parameter four-class reasoning-distilled student achieves AUC 0.924 (95% CI [0.918, 0.929]), compared with 0.912 for the four-class label-only student. At Level 2, product-type test-time training (PT-TTT) uses few-shot demonstrations to optimize lightweight category-specific adapters over the frozen student. PT-TTT improves AUC from 0.924 to 0.941 and average precision from 0.920 to 0.940. On a 100K-pair proxy catalog, the distilled student on a single eight-GPU machine is approximately 5,000x faster and 10,000x lower in estimated cost than direct LLM inference.

</details>


### [79] [RegionFed: Federated Learning for Personalized Query Understanding in Heterogeneous Retail Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05403)
*Quoc H. Nguyen,Ali Lafzi,Abhijeet Phatak,Siddharth Pratap Singh,Rohit Upadhyay,Yogananda Domlur Seetharama,Chittaranjan Tripathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RegionFed是一个梯度级别的鲁棒联邦学习框架，通过利用区域梯度与全局梯度之间的ℓ2冲突作为统一信号，同时进行异构性诊断、个性化策略路由和个性化强度自适应控制，在不修改代码的前提下适用于T5、RoBERTa和CNN等现代架构，解决了参数级联邦个性化方法在Transformer上崩溃（准确率低于10%）的问题。在三个公开数据集和四种架构上，RegionFed-Meta实现了92.27%的准确率，逼近有隐私风险的集中式上界（92.04%，差距仅0.23个百分点，在1σ内），同时提供约0.60的差分隐私保证和O(1/√T)收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售搜索系统服务多个地理区域但面临显著的数据异构性（不同查询模式、词汇和产品偏好），需要在保持隐私的同时实现模型个性化。标准联邦学习产生牺牲区域性能的全局模型，而现有参数级个性化联邦学习方法在基于Transformer的现代架构（如T5）上因参数共享嵌入和LayerNorm交互而灾难性崩溃，准确率甚至低于10%。因此亟需一种架构鲁棒的联邦个性化框架。

Method: RegionFed完全在梯度层面运作而非参数层面，将模型视为可微黑盒。其核心创新是利用区域梯度与全局梯度之间的ℓ2冲突作为统一信号，实现三个功能：(i)诊断数据异构性程度；(ii)为每个区域路由到最低成本的充分个性化策略；(iii)自适应控制个性化强度。由于不涉及参数级操作，该方法可在T5-Small、T5-3B、RoBERTa和CNN上零代码修改直接部署。

Result: 在三个公开数据集（Amazon ESCI、Amazon Reviews、LEAF-FEMNIST）和四种架构上，RegionFed-Meta达到92.27%准确率，与隐私违反的集中式上界（Centralized + Regional Weighting: 92.04%）仅差0.23个百分点（在1σ内），同时提供ε≈0.60的差分隐私保证和O(1/√T)收敛率。在Transformer上取得显著收益（参数级方法在此完全崩溃），并在CNN上保持一致改进。

Conclusion: RegionFed通过梯度级个人化策略克服了参数级个性化方法在Transformer上的结构性失效问题，构建了架构鲁棒的个性化联邦学习框架。其在接近集中式上界性能的同时满足差分隐私要求，为多区域零售搜索场景下的隐私保护个性化提供了一种通用、无需修改架构的解决方案。

Abstract: Retail search systems serve diverse geographic regions with distinct query patterns, vocabularies, and product preferences, creating significant data heterogeneity that challenges both privacy-preserving training and model personalization. Federated learning offers a natural solution for privacy, but standard FL methods produce global models that sacrifice regional performance, while existing personalized FL approaches operate at the parameter level and catastrophically collapse on modern transformers (below 10\% accuracy on T5) due to tied embeddings and LayerNorm interactions. We introduce RegionFed, an \textit{architecture-robust} federated learning framework that sidesteps this failure by operating entirely at the gradient level. RegionFed uses the $\ell_2$ conflict between regional and global gradients as a unified signal that (i) diagnoses heterogeneity, (ii) routes each region to the cheapest sufficient personalization strategy, and (iii) adaptively controls personalization strength. Because it treats models as differentiable black boxes, RegionFed deploys on T5-Small, T5-3B, RoBERTa, and CNN with zero code changes, providing large gains on transformers (where parameter-level methods collapse) and consistent improvements on CNNs. Across three public datasets (Amazon ESCI, Amazon Reviews, LEAF-FEMNIST) and four architectures, RegionFed-Meta achieves 92.27\%, closing the gap to the privacy-violating centralized upper bound (Centralized + Regional Weighting: 92.04\%, $Δ$=0.23pp, within 1$σ$) while providing $(ε{\approx}0.60)$-differential privacy and $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ convergence.

</details>

### [80] [On the torsion in the Chow motive of an Enriques surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04360)
*Jake Huryn,William C. Newman*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Enriques曲面S的整Chow motive由挠和项t(S)决定的问题，探讨了形如t(S)的motives之间的同构与自同态，这与两个Enriques曲面乘积的整Hodge猜想密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Enriques曲面的整Chow motive结构，尤其是其挠部分t(S)的贡献，以及这如何关联到两个Enriques曲面乘积上的整Hodge猜想。

Method: 通过研究形如t(S)的motives之间的同构与自同态来分析其代数结构。

Result: 揭示了t(S)的同构与自同态结构，并建立了与两个Enriques曲面乘积的整Hodge猜想之间的联系。

Conclusion: 对Enriques曲面motives中挠分量的结构分析为理解整Hodge猜想提供了重要工具。

Abstract: The integral Chow motive of an Enriques surface $S$ is determined by a torsion summand $\mathfrak{t}(S)$, which contributes the torsion in the cohomology of $S$. We study isomorphisms between and endomorphisms of motives of the form $\mathfrak{t}(S)$. This is closely related to the integral Hodge conjecture for products of two Enriques surfaces.

</details>


### [81] [Cohomology on Cotangent Bundles of Partial Flag Varieties in Type A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04670)
*Nikolay Grantcharov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究SL_n(C)的旗流形余切丛上向量丛的上同调。作者将T^*((G/P)拉回的向量丛的上同调表达为仿射Kac-Moody群相关Bott-Samelson簇上某些线丛上同调的直极限，由此证明高阶上同调消失，并表明全局截面由其零次部分V_G(λ)^*在C[g^*]上生成。结合与A. Slipper合作中构造的Braverman-Kazhdan交织算子，给出C[T^*(SL_n/[P,P])]的显式生成集；最后与Tom Gannon合作，证明该环的仿射化Spec(C[T^*(SL_n/[P,P])])具有终奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在理解G/P余切丛上自然出现的等变向量束的上同调结构，建立理论与仿射Kac-Moody群Bott-Samelson簇之间深层的联系，从而解决余切丛坐标环的显式刻画及其奇点性质的判定问题。

Method: 方法核心是将T^*(G/P)上拉回向量束的上同调转化为仿射Kac-Moody群上的Bott-Samelson簇上某类线束上同调的直极限，通过该比较建立上同调消失定理；再利用Braverman-Kazhdan交织算子构造C[T^*(SL_n/[P,P])]的显式生成集；与Tom Gannon合作，利用生成集结果证明仿射化对应的谱具有终奇点（terminal singularities）。

Result: 证明了该向量束的高阶上同调消失，并确定其全局截面由零次部分V_G(λ)^*在C[g^*]上生成；进而得到C[T^*(SL_n/[P,P])]的一个显式生成集；最终结合这些结果证明了仿射化Spec(C[T^*(SL_n/[P,P])])具有终奇点。

Conclusion: 文章建立了仿射Kac-Moody群几何与有限维旗簇余切丛上同调之间的桥梁，获得了上同调消失和生成截面结构等关键结论，并解决了SL_n/[P,P]余切丛坐标环的生成问题及其仿射化的终奇点性质，为相关表示论与奇点理论问题提供了系统方法。

Abstract: Let $G=\mathrm{SL}_n(\mathbf C)$ and let $P\subset G$ be a standard parabolic subgroup with Levi factor $L$. For a $G$-dominant weight $λ$, consider the vector bundle on $T^*(G/P)$ obtained by pulling back the vector bundle on $G/P$ associated to the irreducible $L$-module $V_L(λ)^*$. We express its cohomology as a direct limit of the cohomology of certain line bundles on a Bott--Samelson variety associated to an affine Kac-Moody group. This comparison yields vanishing of higher cohomology and shows that the global sections are generated over $\mathbf C[\mathfrak g^*]$ by their degree-zero part $V_G(λ)^*$. Using these results together with the Braverman--Kazhdan intertwiners constructed in earlier joint work with A. Slipper, we give an explicit generating set for $\mathbf C[T^*(\mathrm{SL}_n/[P,P])]$. Finally, in an appendix joint with Tom Gannon, we combine these results to show the affinization $\mathrm{Spec}(\mathbf{C}[T^*(SL_n/[P,P])])$ has terminal singularities.

</details>


### [82] [Heisenberg Equivariant Compactifications of Rational Homogeneous Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04685)
*Cong Ding,Baohua Fu,Zhijun Luo*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了复射影有理齐次流形 G/P（维度为 2m+1）何时能等变紧致化海森堡群（维度同为 2m+1）。证明了 G/P 成为海森堡群的等变紧致化当且仅当它与伴随簇、3 维光滑二次曲面 Q^3，或形如 P^{2m+1-d} × Y（其中 1≤d=dim Y≤m，Y 为 cominuscule 簇之积）的乘积空间同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海森堡群在几何表示论与代数群作用理论中具有重要地位。研究哪些复射影有理齐次流形可以作为海森堡群的等变紧致化，有助于理解代数群在有理齐次流形上的作用与紧致化之间的内在联系，并揭示这类几何对象的分类结构。

Method: 本文采用代数群与齐次空间理论的方法，通过对 G/P 的结构和维度条件（dim=2m+1）进行细致分析，逐一考察其是否承载一个与海森堡群作用相容的等变紧致化结构。分类通过排除不满足条件的 G/P 类型，并构造满足条件的显式例子（伴随簇、Q^3 及形如 P×Y 的乘积空间）来完成。

Result: 得到了一个完整的分类定理：G/P（dim=2m+1）是海森堡群（同维）的等变紧致化，当且仅当 G/P 同构于以下三类之一：(1) 伴随簇；(2) 三维光滑二次曲面 Q^3；(3) P^{2m+1-d} × Y，其中 1≤d=dim Y≤m 且 Y 是 cominuscule 簇的乘积。

Conclusion: 该定理给出了复射影有理齐次流形作为海森堡群等变紧致化的完整刻画，揭示了伴随簇、二次曲面 Q^3 以及 cominuscule 簇乘积与投射空间的乘积作为海森堡群紧致化之间的密切联系，为理解海森堡群在齐次流形上的等变嵌入提供了分类性结论。

Abstract: Let $G/P$ be a complex projective rational homogeneous variety of dimension $2m+1$. We prove that $G/P$ is an equivariant compactification of the Heisenberg group of dimension $2m+1$ if and only if it is isomorphic to either an adjoint variety, or the 3-dimensional smooth quadric $Q^3$, or a product $\mathbb{P}^{2m+1-d} \times Y$ with $1 \leq d=\dim Y \leq m$, where $Y$ is a product of cominuscule varieties.

</details>


### [83] [q-Opers and Quantum/Classical Duality Beyond Type A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04739)
*Peter Koroteev,Myungbo Shim,Rahul Singh*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用q-oper的语言，提出了量子XXZ自旋链的Bethe Ansatz方程与经典B/C/D型三角Ruijsenaars-Schneider/Macdonald多体系统之间对偶性的代数几何描述。该对偶性通过Z/2 Z折叠实现，将XXZ Bethe Ansatz方程的解与B/C/D型哈密顿量的能量等值集建立双射对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有已知结果：GL(N) XXZ自旋链（带扭曲周期边界条件）与N体Ruijsenaars-Schneider系统之间存在对偶性。本文旨在将这一对偶性推广至经典群B/C/D类型的XXZ自旋链（带开放边界条件）情形，从而拓展该对偶性框架的一般性。

Method: 作者以q-oper语言为基础，实施Z/2 Z折叠操作：一是在(GL(N),q)-oper数据层面进行折叠，二是在N体三角Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型层面进行折叠，从而建立经典B/C/D型系统与A型XXZ Bethe Ansatz方程（开放边界条件）之间的代数几何对应关系。BC型非约化系统也在同一框架内自然出现。

Result: 本文发现B/C/D型经典哈密顿量的能量等值集与A型XXZ Bethe Ansatz方程（开放边界条件）的解集之间存在双射关系，且该结果涵盖了非约化的BC型系统。

Conclusion: 该字典推广了以往GL(N) XXZ自旋链与N体Ruijsenaars-Schneider系统之间对偶性的已知结果，提供了一个统一的代数几何框架，将经典群类型的多体可积系统与XXZ型量子自旋链联系起来。

Abstract: Using the language of q-opers, we propose an algebro-geometric description of the duality between Bethe Ansatz equations for quantum XXZ spin chains and many-body trigonometric Ruijsenaars-Schneider/Macdonald systems for classical groups. Upon this duality, the energy level sets of the classical B/C/D-type Hamiltonians are found to be in bijection with the set of solutions of the XXZ Bethe Ansatz equations of type A, albeit with open boundary conditions. This dictionary generalizes previous results in which a GL(N) XXZ spin chain with twisted periodic boundary conditions was dual to an N-body Ruijsenaars-Schneider system. Our construction implements a $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ folding both at the level of the (GL(N),q)-oper data as well as at the level of the N-body trigonometric Ruijsenaars-Schneider model. The nonreduced BC-type systems appear in our analysis as well.

In expected dimension zero, equivariant localization gives $1225$ and $1176$ conics on general linear sections of $\operatorname{Gr}(2,7)$ and $\operatorname{Gr}(3,6)$, respectively.

In doing so, we eliminate the characteristic constraint previously found in the relative version of Kummer's exact sequence. Furthermore, given a finite subintegral extension of noetherian rings $A \hookrightarrow B$, we show that all the $fppf$ cohomology groups, $H^i_{fppf}(Spec(A),μ^f_n); \;\forall i\geq 0$ vanish whenever $n$ does not divide the characteristic of $A$.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 核心方法是混合局部约化：对混合de Rham–Dolbeault微分分次代数(∧•V∨, d_V)上的有限Maurer–Cartan对象，构造乘法同伦收缩“实方向”（叶方向），再利用Block的Dolbeault规范定理消去正次的横向形式次数，从而将一般对象化简为有限平坦V-超连接。对紧致X，证明了该构造诱导同伦范畴的精确等价；并通过Čech下降、横向单值群胚的等变凝聚解析层识别以及全纯悬架下的Morita等价等工具建立类别间的具体对应。

Conclusion: 本文建立的有限超连接模型为横截全纯叶状流形的凝聚层导出范畴提供了统一的、可计算的微分几何描述，是叶状流形上非交换几何与导出范畴理论之间的一次深度融合。S1与S2的反例表明，普通单值1-群胚不足以完整恢复导出范畴，需要更精细的高阶或超连接结构。

Abstract: Let $X$ carry a transversely holomorphic foliation, equivalently an elliptic involutive structure $V\subset T_{\mathbb C}X$, and let ${\mathcal O}_V$ be its sheaf of leafwise-constant, transversely holomorphic functions. We construct a finite superconnection model for the derived category of coherent ${\mathcal O}_V$-modules. The key input is a mixed local reduction for finite Maurer--Cartan objects over the mixed de Rham--Dolbeault dga $(\wedge^\bullet V^\vee,d_V)$: a multiplicative homotopy contracts the real directions, after which Block's Dolbeault gauge theorem removes the positive transverse form degrees. For compact $X$, this gives an exact equivalence between the homotopy category of bounded finite-rank flat $V$-superconnections and $D^b_{\mathrm{coh}}(X,{\mathcal O}_V)$, interpolating between the de Rham and Dolbeault realizations. We prove locally finite Čech descent under necessary uniform amplitude and rank bounds, identify the coherent heart with equivariant coherent analytic sheaves on a transverse monodromy groupoid, and characterize descent to ordinary holonomy. For a holomorphic suspension, we identify the full superconnection category, up to Morita equivalence, with the homotopy fixed points of the Dolbeault category of the transversal and derive an equivariant Ext spectral sequence. Examples on $S^1$ and $S^2$ delimit when ordinary monodromy $1$-groupoids can recover the derived category.

</details>
