Method: 首先证明F(Z,z)的每个不可约分量是接触超平面中的积分子簇（integral subvariety）；随后在两种情形下研究其Legendre分量：(i) Z存在Springer分解；(ii) Z为经典类型射影化简单李代数中的平方零轨道。利用这些结构结果推导出两个应用性结论。

Result: 给出F(Z,z)不可约分量均为接触超平面中积分子簇的证明；在Springer分解情形和经典类型平方零轨道情形下对Legendre分量进行刻画；得到两个推论：利用F(Z,z)在平方零轨道中刻画Richardson轨道；对不可约Hermitian对称空间的分层Mukai flop对应的Z给出F(Z,z)的完整描述。

Conclusion: 该研究将幂零轨道闭包上的直线切方向空间与接触几何、Legendre子簇联系起来，建立了F(Z,z)的结构定理，并将其应用于Richardson轨道的刻画及Mukai flop相关的几何描述，丰富了幂零轨道闭包几何的研究工具和结果。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak{g}$ be a complex semisimple Lie algebra, and $\overline{Z}$ a nilpotent orbit closure in the projectivization $\mathbb{P}(\mathfrak{g})$. We investigate the space of tangent directions of lines on $\overline{Z}$ passing through a general point $z$, denoted by $F(\overline{Z},\,z)$. We first prove that every irreducible component of $F(\overline{Z},\,z)$ is an integral subvariety of the contact hyperplane in the projectivized tangent space. Next, we study Legendrian components of $F(\overline{Z},\,z)$ in two cases: the case where $\overline{Z}$ admits a Springer resolution; the case where $\overline{Z}$ is square-zero in a projectivized simple Lie algebra of classical type. As an application, two corollaries are presented: a characterization of Richardson orbits among square-zero orbits in terms of $F(\overline{Z},\,z)$; a description of $F(\overline{Z},\,z)$ for $\overline{Z}$ arising from stratified Mukai flops associated to irreducible Hermitian symmetric spaces.

</details>

Abstract: We study constructible $\mathbb{Z}[\tfrac{1}{p}]$-linear étale motives on parahoric Hecke stacks associated with quasi-split tamely ramified reductive groups. We prove a canonical equivalence between their equal-characteristic and Witt-vector incarnations that is monoidal with respect to convolution. This extends Bando's comparison, which concerns split reductive groups and constructible étale sheaves. The proof combines the study of a family of affine Grassmannians for Pappas--Zhu group schemes with the use of the motivic nearby cycles functor.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 建立了Kähler空间中伪有效层在陈类消失条件下的平坦性判据，通过两个新颖的技术工具（稳定层的Hermitian平坦性定理及Simpson定理的奇异Kähler类比）完成了证明，显著拓展了原有定理的适用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that if $E$ is a pseudo-effective sheaf with vanishing first Chern class on a klt compact Kähler space $X$, then, after passing to a finite quasi-étale cover, the reflexive pullback of $E$ is locally free and flat. The second is a singular Kähler analogue of Simpson's flatness theorem for extensions of locally free Hermitian flat sheaves.

### [91] [Filtrations on D-modules and multiplicities of roots of Bernstein-Sato polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05215)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 给出了确定门限使得每个超过该门限的b_f(s)的根平移都是Archimedean zeta函数的极点；证明了这些极点的阶等于对数单值算子的幂零指数，后者又被表示为幂b-函数根重数的极限；对Gelfand问题给出有效渐近解；在f具有齐次孤立奇点、超平面排列或球面簇上半不变量情形下肯定地回答了若干开放问题；在超曲面具有log canonical奇点条件下给出了Torelli问题的肯定回答（1/f属于交复形当且仅当-1是b_f(s)的单根）。

Conclusion: 本文确立了Bernstein-Sato型多项式重数在一系列具体几何/代数情境下与奇点不变量（权重与Hodge过滤、对数单值算子幂零指数、极点阶等）的精确等价或逼近关系，解决了Gelfand问题的有效形式并在若干重要特殊情形与log canonical假设下回答了几何问题，深化了对D-模与奇点理论交叉领域的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we relate multiplicities of Bernstein--Sato-type polynomials with respect to a holomorphic function f to several singularity invariants. First, we introduce certain ``mod'' b-functions and show that they characterize the weight filtration on the localization of a simple regular holonomic D-module along f, and we provide an algorithm to compute them. Second, we show that they can be approximated by the multiplicities of roots of power b-functions (the b-functions with respect to powers of f). Further, we give a sharp upper bound for the Hodge level of elements given by a certain sum of such multiplicities. Next, we give an effective asymptotic solution to the Gelfand problem by determining an explicit threshold after which every integer shift of a root of b_f(s) is a pole of the Archimedean zeta function of f. We also show that the order of these poles is equal to the nilpotency index of the logarithmic monodromy operator, which we further express as the limit of the multiplicities of roots of power b-functions. We define several filtrations, relating them to the weight and Hodge filtrations, based upon which we leave some open questions that we address in the affirmative in the case when f has a homogeneous isolated singularity, or it is a hyperplane arrangement, or it is a semi-invariant on a spherical variety. We give several immediate applications to our results, including a positive answer to a question of Torelli assuming the hypersurface has log canonical singularities: 1/f lies in the intersection complex of the hypersurface of f if and only if -1 is a simple root of b_f(s).

</details>

Abstract: In this manuscript, scalar norm-type accelerators are used for first time in order to increase the order of convergence of known iterative methods for solving the matrix equation $X^{-1}-A=0$, while decreasing the number of matrix-matrix products. In the main results, not only the convergence and its order is proven, but also its efficiency and stability. This new technique provides good numerical results, even compared with existing fourth-, sixth- and eighth-order schemes in large matrices of up to $10^6$ entries, but also promises the opening of a new kind of procedures with good performance and scalability. Finally, an application to digital image restoration is made to show the behavior of the method, compared with existing ones.

Abstract: I present a matrix-free symmetric interior penalty discontinuous Galerkin method for adaptively refined Cartesian meshes on GPUs. At non-matching interfaces, auxiliary \emph{shadow cells} represent the adjacent coarse polynomial on the fine level. This approach converts non-matching interfaces into matching faces, permitting uniform face evaluation throughout the mesh. I prove that this construction is equivalent to the standard non-matching formulation. Furthermore, this representation yields a local geometric multigrid method in which frozen shadows provide inter-level boundary data and carry residual contributions to coarser levels. A primal--dual pairing eliminates shadow assembly from the Krylov iteration. Numerical experiments with cubic elements ($p=3$) on an NVIDIA A100 show stable multigrid convergence under increasing refinement depth and efficient GPU execution.

Finally, we prove optimal error estimates for smooth solutions to nonlinear scalar conservation laws, and prove strong convergence for discontinuous solutions to strictly convex conservation laws via compensated compactness.

### [96] [A Complete Resolution of Forsythe's Conjecture for Restarted Conjugate Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04659)
*Matthew J. Colbrook,George Stepaniants,Alex Townsend*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文完整解决了Forsythe猜想（1968年提出）：关于重启共轭梯度法（restarted conjugate-gradient）在实对称正定问题上的渐近收敛行为。作者按重启长度s给出了完整分类：当s=1,2,3时猜想成立（残差沿奇偶重启子序列分别收敛），当s≥4时猜想不成立（存在维度s+4的对角正定反例，其偶残差方向不收敛）。关键阈值在s=4处，通过区间算术与Sturm序列证明Hopf分岔点，并借助周期轨道与shading论证构造反例，再通过度提升推广到所有更大的重启长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forsythe于1968年提出的猜想在近六十年间除经典最速下降情形（s=1，由Akaike定理解决）外，一般情形的渐近问题始终悬而未决。该问题的解决对理解重启共轭梯度法的收敛性与行为分类具有根本性的理论意义，是数值代数中一个长期开放的经典猜想。

Method: 1）对于s=2,3的正面结果：利用与度无关的双正交恒等式（degree-independent double-orthogonality identity）及低度不动点集分析；2）对于s=4的反例：通过有理区间算术（rational interval arithmetic）和Sturm序列证明重标定平方权重映射的引导向量场存在横向Hopf点；3）利用解析周期轨道与shadowing论证构造重启长度4的收敛失败反例；4）通过度提升（degree elevation）将反例推广到所有s≥4的重启长度。

Result: 给出了重启长度的完整分类：s=1,2,3时Forsythe猜想的普适结论成立（每个问题要么终止，要么偶/奇残差方向收敛）；s≥4时猜想不成立，构造了维度s+4的对角正定反例，其迭代永不终止且偶残差方向不收敛。确立了s=3与s=4之间的尖锐阈值（sharp threshold）。

Conclusion: Forsythe猜想被完全解决：猜想所述的普适收敛结论仅在重启长度s∈{1,2,3}时成立，对所有s≥4均不成立。该分类提供了一个清晰的锐利阈值，终结了近六十年未解决的渐近性公开问题。

Abstract: Forsythe's conjecture, published in 1968, asserts that for each restart length $s$, every exact-arithmetic restarted conjugate-gradient iteration on a real symmetric positive definite problem either terminates or has normalised residuals that converge separately along the even and odd restart subsequences. Apart from the classical steepest-descent case, this asymptotic question remained unresolved in full generality for nearly six decades. We give a complete classification by restart length in the original finite-dimensional setting and identify a sharp threshold. For $s=2$ and $s=3$, every problem either terminates or has convergent even and odd residual directions. For every $s\ge4$, there is a diagonal positive definite counterexample of dimension $s+4$ which never terminates and whose even residual directions do not converge. Together with Akaike's theorem for $s=1$, this shows that the conjectured universal conclusion is true precisely for $s\in\{1,2,3\}$ and false for every $s\ge4$. The positive results follow from a degree-independent double-orthogonality identity and an analysis of the low-degree fixed-point sets. At restart length four, rational interval arithmetic and Sturm sequences certify a transverse Hopf point of the leading vector field of the rescaled squared-weight map. Analytic periodic-orbit and shadowing arguments yield the counterexample at restart length four, and degree elevation extends the construction to every larger restart length.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子分裂方法是求解耦合端口哈密顿系统的有效途径，但分解方式的选择不唯一，且会显著影响计算效率和系统能量行为的保持。目前缺乏系统的判据来评估不同分裂/分解策略对能量行为的影响，因此需要引入能量一致性准则并给出充分条件，以指导分解策略的选择。

Method: 1) 提出"能量一致性"（energy consistency）作为评估分裂方法的标准；2) 推导分裂方法基于给定分解继承连续系统能量行为的充分条件；3) 基于这些条件分析多种分解策略，包括保持端口哈密顿结构的分解、利用低维子系统动力学的分解以及分离时间尺度的分解；4) 使用Strang分裂及其多时间步长扩展进行数值实验验证。

Result: 数值实验表明：保持子流的端口哈密顿结构对于能量一致的分裂至关重要；利用子系统结构或时间尺度分离的分解能带来显著的计算优势；特别是时间尺度分解对具有明显多速率特征的系统可带来显著效率提升，而破坏结构的分解可能导致不良的能量行为。

Conclusion: 在耦合端口哈密顿系统的算子分裂中，结构保持的分解是保证能量一致性的关键；同时，利用子系统动力学或时间尺度分离的分解策略可以在不损害能量行为的前提下获得可观的加速效果，其中多速率分解在具有强多速率特性的系统中优势最为突出。

Abstract: Operator splitting provides an attractive approach for the numerical integration of (coupled) port-Hamiltonian systems, as it allows the underlying system structure to be exploited at the level of the individual subproblems. However, the choice of the decomposition is not unique and may strongly affect both the computational efficiency and the preservation of the energy behavior of the original system. In this work, we investigate this interplay systematically and introduce energy consistency as a criterion for assessing splitting methods for port-Hamiltonian ordinary differential equations. We derive sufficient conditions under which a splitting based on a given decomposition inherits the energy behavior of the continuous system and use these conditions to analyze several decomposition strategies for coupled port-Hamiltonian systems. In particular, we compare decompositions that preserve the structure with approaches that exploit lower-dimensional subsystem dynamics or separated time scales. The analysis is complemented by numerical experiments using Strang splitting and its multiple-time-stepping extension. A scalable electro-thermal benchmark with fast electrical and slow thermal dynamics is employed to assess accuracy, energy behavior, and computational efficiency. The results demonstrate that preserving the port-Hamiltonian structure of the subflows is essential for energy-consistent splitting, whereas decompositions that exploit subsystem structure or time-scale separation can provide substantial computational advantages. In particular, the time-scale decomposition yields significant efficiency gains for systems with pronounced multirate characteristics, while structure-destroying decompositions may lead to undesirable energy behavior.

</details>

Method: 作者建立了一个统一的构造性框架来设计局部Fortin算子。核心方法包括：1) 构造满足散度保持、局部稳定性和逼近性质的算子；2) 为实现迹保持性质，某些有限元配对需要对速度空间进行富集（enrichment）；3) 将该框架系统性地应用于P_d-P_0、Bernardi-Raugel、协调Crouzeix-Raviart、修正MINI和广义Taylor-Hood等代表性有限元单元，逐一展示具体构造。

Result: 成功为一系列经典混合有限元配对（P_d-P_0、Bernardi-Raugel、协调Crouzeix-Raviart、修正MINI、广义Taylor-Hood）构造了同时满足散度保持、局部稳定性、逼近性质和迹保持性质的局部Fortin算子。某些单元（如P_d-P_0）需要速度空间富集才能实现迹保持。此外，论文揭示了这类加强Fortin算子在多类应用中的价值，包括非牛顿流体、非齐次Dirichlet边界条件和移动域/域逼近中一致inf-sup稳定性的保证。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种统一且通用的局部Fortin算子构造方法，超越了传统上仅确保inf-sup稳定性的目标。这类具有附加性质（特别是迹保持和散度保持）的Fortin算子为处理更广泛的实际问题（含非牛顿流体、非齐次边界条件及移动域）奠定了坚实的理论基础，显著拓展了混合有限元方法的适用范围。

Abstract: We present a unifying framework to construct local Fortin operators for conforming mixed finite element pairs for the Stokes equations. The operators are constructed to satisfy the divergence-preservation property, local stability and approximation properties, and certain trace-preservation properties. For the latter, some of the finite element pairs require an enrichment of the velocity space. We present the construction for the $P_d-P_0$ element, the Bernardi-Raugel element, the conforming Crouzeix-Raviart element, a modified MINI element and generalised Taylor-Hood elements. Furthermore, we discuss implications of the existence of such a Fortin operator beyond inf-sup stability. These include applications to non-Newtonian fluid flow, problems with inhomogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions, as well as uniform inf-sup stability relevant for domain approximation and moving domains.

</details>


### [99] [Frequency-explicit convergence analysis of a multiscale finite element method for highly heterogeneous scattering problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04930)
*T. Chaumont-Frelet,Z. Kassali*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究高频时间谐波散射问题中高度异质可穿透障碍物的数值逼近，提出一种高阶多尺度有限元方法，并给出对波数k和特征尺度ε均显式的误差分析，证明高阶方法在高频下可降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频散射问题（kL≫1）数值求解极具挑战性，而当散射体包含不同材料且其特征尺度满足kε≪1时，问题更加复杂。传统方法在高频下计算成本高昂，需要发展新的数值方法与相应的误差分析来提升效率。

Method: 提出一种高阶多尺度有限元方法（high-order multiscale finite element method），用于处理含异质材料（特征尺度ε远小于波长）的可穿透障碍物的高频时间谐波散射问题。作者推导了同时显式依赖于波数k和异质尺度ε的误差估计。

Result: 误差估计表明，采用高阶方法可以在大频率下显著降低计算成本。这一理论结果得到了数值实验的验证。

Conclusion: 高阶多尺度有限元方法在高频异质散射问题中具有优势，其误差分析显式揭示了高阶方法在高频区域的计算效率优势，为相关数值算法的选择和优化提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We analyze the numerical approximation of time-harmonic scattering by highly heterogeneous penetrable obstacles. These problems are especially challenging in the high-frequency regime, where the size of the scatterer $L$ is much larger than the wavelength, i.e., the wavenumber $k$ is such that $kL \gg 1$. Here, we further consider the situation where the scatterer contains different materials, with a characteristic size $\varepsilon$ such that $k\varepsilon \ll 1$. We propose a high-order multiscale finite element method, and provide an error analysis that is explicit in both $k$ and $\varepsilon$. Crucially, our error estimates suggest that using a high-order method should reduce the computational cost for large frequencies, which is corroborated by numerical examples.

</details>


### [100] [A simple shallow neural network for emulating the solution to singularly perturbed problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05180)
*Christos Xenophontos,Aayushman Raina*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有两个小参数的奇异摄动二阶边值问题，提出了一种利用渐近展开分解（光滑部分+层部分）的浅层神经网络模拟方法，通过将合适的指数函数增强到逼近空间中，并结合tanh激活函数及残差或能量最小化训练目标，实现了对摄动参数的鲁棒模拟行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异摄动边值问题在工程和物理中广泛存在，其解具有边界层/内部层等急剧变化特征。传统的神经网络方法在处理这类参数依赖问题时往往缺乏参数鲁棒性，即网络性能会随摄动参数变化而产生严重退化。作者旨在利用已有的渐近展开分析，构建对参数具有鲁棒性的神经网络近似方案。

Method: 作者采用前馈浅层神经网络（shallow NN），其核心设计思想包括：(1) 利用解的加性分解（光滑分量与层分量），借助已有文献中显式依赖于微分阶数和摄动参数的导数估计；(2) 使用tanh激活函数；(3) 将合适的指数函数（模拟边界层结构）增强到神经网络的逼近空间中，类似于有限元方法中的富化空间（enriched spaces）技术；(4) 采用残差最小化或能量最小化作为不同的训练目标。

Result: 通过一维和二维（包括光滑非张量积域）的数值实验，展示了所提方法在所测试的摄动参数范围内呈现出参数鲁棒（parameter-robust）的行为特性，验证了增强指数函数空间与渐近展开信息结合的神经网络能有效模拟奇异摄动问题解。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将渐近分析理论（解的分解结构）与神经网络逼近相结合，是解决奇异摄动问题参数鲁棒模拟的有效途径，为深度学习在参数化偏微分方程中的应用提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: We consider (feed-forward) Neural Networks (NNs) for the emulation of the solution to singularly perturbed second order boundary value problems, with two small parameters. We describe a shallow NN which exploits available asymptotic expansions for the solution. These additive decompositions into smooth and layer components, allow for derivative estimates which are explicit in the order of differentiation as well as the singular perturbation parameter(s) \cite{melenk, Irene, SX}. Utilizing such decompositions, we propose a simple NN for emulating the solution to such problems using the $\tanh$ activation function together with different training objectives, such as residual or energy minimization. The key idea is to augment the approximation space with suitable exponential functions, similar to enriched spaces in finite element methods, e.g.~\cite{Kellogg}. Numerical examples in one and two dimensions, including a smooth non-tensor-product domain, illustrate the resulting parameter-robust behavior over the tested perturbation ranges.

</details>


### [101] [A Generalized Scalar Auxiliary Variable Method for Structure-Preserving and Efficient Integration of Nonlinear port-Hamiltonian DAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05246)
*Aashutosh Sharma,Andreas Bartel,Manuel Schaller*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向非线性指标1端口哈密顿微分代数方程(pH-DAEs)的能量最优广义标量辅助变量(EOP-GSAV)框架，利用端口哈密顿结构将非线性项与常数隐式核心分离，所得的BDF-1和BDF-2格式每步仅需一次矩阵分解可复用的线性求解，同时保持离散无源性和哈密顿量的精确追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端口哈密顿微分代数方程在数值模拟中需要高效且保持结构特性的时间离散方法，但现有方法（如隐式中点配合牛顿迭代）在计算成本与结构保持之间存在权衡。

Method: 构建EOP-GSAV框架，分离非线性force、互联(interconnection)与耗散(dissipation)项与常数隐式核心；推导BDF-1和BDF-2格式，每时间步仅需一次线性求解且分解可复用。

Result: 数值实验覆盖强状态依赖非线性应力测试到大规模基准，在匹配精度条件下相较隐式中点法（含完整、修正和冻结雅可比牛顿迭代）展现可观效率提升；与SUNDIALS IDA比较显示同阶时工作量-精度行为相当，高精度区间非专用自适应变阶IDA更快。

Conclusion: EOP-GSAV框架为pH-DAEs提供了兼具结构保持性与计算效率的时间积分方案，在中等精度应用中具有竞争力，尽管高精度极端区间仍以变阶自适应方法占优。

Abstract: We develop an energy-optimal generalized scalar auxiliary variable (EOP-GSAV) framework for nonlinear index-one port-Hamiltonian differential-algebraic equations (pH-DAEs). Exploiting the port-Hamiltonian structure, we separate the nonlinear effort, interconnection, and dissipation terms from a constant implicit core. The resulting BDF-1 and BDF-2 schemes require one linear solve per time step with a reusable factorization, while retaining discrete passivity and accurate tracking of the Hamiltonian. The schemes are compared with the implicit midpoint method equipped with full, modified, and frozen-Jacobian Newton iterations. Numerical experiments ranging from a strongly state-dependent nonlinear stress test to large-scale benchmarks demonstrate robust and competitive performance, with substantial efficiency gains in matched-accuracy regimes. A comparison with SUNDIALS IDA shows comparable work-precision behavior at equal order despite a non-specialized Python/SciPy implementation, while unrestricted variable-order adaptive IDA is faster in the high-accuracy regime.

</details>


### [102] [Shallow neural network approximation in mixed Sobolev spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05263)
*Yuwen Li,Guozhi Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究浅层神经网络对混合Sobolev空间的最佳L2逼近问题，提出与激活函数无关的Fourier-block原理，将全局逼近速率确定为min{α,ρ}（目标混合光滑度α，激活单变量逼近阶ρ），并对ReLU^k、B样条、soft-ReLU^k、ELU、cosine等激活函数给出精确的代数逼近指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解浅层神经网络（单一隐藏层）对高维混合光滑函数的逼近能力是深度学习理论的核心问题。现有工作缺乏对一般激活函数统一的理论框架，也不清楚混合光滑度与激活函数固有逼近能力如何共同决定全局逼近速率。

Method: 1) 建立激活无关的Fourier-block原则：若激活函数在Fourier-block意义上具有单变量逼近阶ρ，则n个神经元的全局L2逼近率为min{α,ρ}（混合光滑度α），含显式对数因子；2) 引入结构化单变量逼近条件，证明其蕴含Fourier-block性质并给出显式参数；3) 对ReLU^k给出匹配的代数下界，确证min{α,k+1}为最优代数逼近指数；4) 将框架推广到B样条、soft-ReLU^k、ELU和cosine激活。

Result: ReLU^k在任意维度下最优代数逼近指数为min{α,k+1}（上界含对数因子）；B样条和soft-ReLU^k同样获得指数min{α,k+1}；ELU和cosine激活获得完整混合光滑度指数α。所有结论均以显式对数因子为界。

Conclusion: 浅层神经网络的逼近速率由目标函数混合光滑度α与激活函数自身单变量逼近阶ρ共同决定，即min{α,ρ}。该Fourier-block框架为多种激活函数提供了统一且最优（在代数指数意义上）的偏差点刻画。

Abstract: We investigate the best $L_2$ approximation of mixed Sobolev spaces by shallow neural networks with $n$ neurons and general activation functions. We first establish an activation-independent Fourier-block principle: if an activation has univariate approximation order $ρ$ in the sense of the Fourier-block property, then the global approximation rate has algebraic order $\min\{α,ρ\}$ for target functions of mixed smoothness $α$, up to explicit logarithmic factors. To verify this property for concrete activations, we introduce a structured univariate approximation condition that implies the Fourier-block property with explicit parameters. For $\mathrm{ReLU}^k$, a matching algebraic lower bound identifies $\min\{α,k+1\}$ as the optimal algebraic approximation exponent in any dimension, up to logarithmic factors in the upper bound. The framework also yields the exponent $\min\{α,k+1\}$ for cardinal B-splines and soft-$\mathrm{ReLU}^k$, and the full mixed-smoothness exponent $α$ for ELU and cosine activations, again up to logarithmic~factors.

</details>


### [103] [A Neural-preconditioned Poisson Solver for Mixed Dirichlet and Neumann Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.00177)
*Kai Weixian Lan,Elias Gueidon,Ayano Kaneda,Julian Panetta,Joseph Teran*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向混合边界条件泊松方程的神经预条件迭代求解器。该神经预条件器经过训练，能高效近似离散拉普拉斯算子的逆，可直接泛化到训练集之外的域形状、边界条件和网格尺寸。关键在于一种采用空间变化卷积核的轻量级神经网络架构，支持快速推理。实验表明该方法优于代数多重网格等传统方法及近期提出的神经预条件器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松离散化通常产生大型病态线性系统，迭代求解器需要强力的预条件器才能高效收敛。然而多重网格等有效预条件器在域形状或边界条件变化时需要昂贵的重新设置阶段，这严重制约了具有演化边界的应用（如需反复求解的不可压缩流体模拟）。

Method: 训练一个神经网络作为预条件器，近似离散拉普拉斯算子的逆。网络采用新颖的空间变化卷积核架构，既保证对不同域形状、边界条件和网格尺寸的泛化能力，又具备快速的推理速度，适用于演化边界场景中的反复求解需求。

Result: 在来自不可压缩流体模拟的挑战性测试用例中，所提求解器在性能上超越了代数多重网格等最先进方法以及近期提出的神经预条件器。

Conclusion: 神经预条件器在域形状、边界条件和网格大小变化时无需重新设置，能够高效泛化，显著降低了演化边界问题的求解瓶颈，为复杂泊松系统求解提供了一种可行的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce a neural-preconditioned iterative solver for Poisson equations with mixed boundary conditions. Typical Poisson discretizations yield large, ill-conditioned linear systems. Iterative solvers can be effective for these problems, but only when equipped with powerful preconditioners. Unfortunately, effective preconditioners like multigrid require costly setup phases that must be re-executed every time domain shapes or boundary conditions change, forming a severe bottleneck for problems with evolving boundaries. In contrast, we present a neural preconditioner trained to efficiently approximate the inverse of the discrete Laplacian in the presence of such changes. Our approach generalizes to domain shapes, boundary conditions, and grid sizes outside the training set. The key to our preconditioner's success is a novel, lightweight neural network architecture featuring spatially varying convolution kernels and supporting fast inference. We demonstrate that our solver outperforms state-of-the-art methods like algebraic multigrid as well as recently proposed neural preconditioners on challenging test cases arising from incompressible fluid simulations.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Reduced Precision Diffusion Synthetic Acceleration for S$_N$ Neutron Transport in LLNL's ARDRA using Hypre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04451)
*Joanna Piper Morgan,Mario Ivan Ortega*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在离散纵标输运计算中，使用降精度（32位）扩散综合加速（DSA）来加速双精度（64位）输运迭代的算法可行性。通过在独立代码和LLNL的ARDRA代码中实现降精度DSA并与hypre库耦合，发现在大多数情况下降精度DSA对加速性能几乎没有影响，但在要求更严格公差时可能失效并增加求解时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散综合加速（DSA）在生产级离散纵标输运代码中至关重要，能显著减少迭代次数但需消耗额外内存与计算成本。近期研究表明降精度预处理具有潜力，但需要验证降精度DSA在减少内存和时间成本的同时，是否不会显著恶化高精度线性系统的收敛行为。

Method: 首先在独立代码中实现降精度DSA以研究平板几何中的理论限制，然后在LLNL的离散纵标中性粒子输运代码ARDRA中实现降精度DSA，并与支持混合精度线性求解的hypre库耦合，求解多种中子输运问题。

Result: 在多数情况下，降精度DSA与源迭代结合使用时，对DSA的加速特性几乎没有影响。然而，当需要更严格的容差时，降精度DSA可能无法成功加速输运求解，反而增加求解时间。

Conclusion: 降精度DSA在大多数输运问题中是可用的性能优化手段，但在需要高精度收敛的情况下需要谨慎使用，因为可能失去加速效果反而增加计算时间。

Abstract: Fast solution times in production discrete ordinates transport codes often require the use of diffusion synthetic acceleration (DSA). A good implementation of DSA will substantially reduce the number of iterations to converge the transport solution at the cost of solving a diffusion-like linear system at each iteration. Often fewer transport iterations will lead to smaller times to solution. Recent work has shown the promise of reduced precision preconditioning. Time and memory cost can be decreased as long as the reduced precision does not substantially degrade the convergence behavior of the higher precision linear system being solved. In this paper, we discuss the algorithmic implications of accelerating transport iterations in double precision (64 bit) while using a reduced precision (32 bit) DSA solve. We initially implement a stand alone code to investigate any theoretical limitations of reduced precision DSA in slabs. Then we implement reduced precision DSA in ARDRA, Lawrence Livermore National Laboratory's (LLNL) discrete ordinance neutral particle transport code, coupled with {\it hypre}, LLNL's scalable linear solver and multigrid methods library capable of mixed precision linear solves Then we solve various neutron transport problems of interest. We find that in most circumstances, DSA acceleration in reduced precision has little to no impact on the acceleration properties of DSA when used with source iteration in most circumstances. However, as tighter tolerances are required, reduced precision DSA may fail to successfully accelerate the transport solve and increase time to solution.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Minimax Lower Bound for Estimating Diffusion-based Local Intrinsic Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04822)
*Jaehee Seo,Wontae Jeong,Jisu Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究基于扩散方法估计高维数据内在几何结构的统计难度。作者聚焦于FLIPD（基于高斯平滑密度的局部内在维度量）所对应的有限尺度总体泛函，证明在规则流形模型下该有限尺度场与流形维度d的偏差至多为O(σ²)，并建立了以(nσ^d)^{-1}阶为下界的极小极大估计下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的方法近来已成为探测高维数据内在几何的有效工具，但其统计难度（statistical difficulty）在很大程度上仍未得到探索。理解这些方法在有限样本下的估计误差下界，对于判定其理论可靠性和指导实际应用具有重要意义。

Method: 研究FLIPD（Kamkari et al., 2024）这一基于扩散的局部内在维度（LID）量，其定义为高斯平滑密度对数值尺度导数的函数。在规则流形模型下：(1) 证明有限尺度场与流形维度d的偏差在模型类上一致地以O(σ²)为界；(2) 建立极小极大下界，估计误差阶为(nσ^d)^{-1}，适用的尺度范围为n^{-1/(2α+d)}≤σ≤σ_0；(3) 在下界构造覆盖的最小尺度处，该下界退化为非参数估计的经典速率n^{-2α/(2α+d)}。

Result: 有限尺度场与真实流形维度d之间的偏差一致地以O(σ²)为界。估计该有限尺度场的极小极大下界为(nσ^d)^{-1}量级，且在最小可覆盖尺度处退化为非参数速率n^{-2α/(2α+d)}。

Conclusion: 该工作首次揭示了基于扩散方法的局部内在维度估计的统计下限，表明其估计误差受到样本量与噪声尺度联合影响，并在特定尺度下与非参数估计的理论速率相吻合，为扩散几何方法的统计理论奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: While diffusion-based methods have recently emerged as effective tools for probing the intrinsic geometry of high-dimensional data, their statistical difficulty remains largely unexplored. We study estimation of the finite-scale population functional underlying FLIPD (Kamkari et al., 2024; arXiv:2406.03537), a diffusion-based local intrinsic dimension (LID) quantity defined through the logarithmic scale derivative of a Gaussian-smoothed density. Intuitively, Gaussian smoothing turns local dimension into a scale law: near a $d$-dimensional manifold, the kernel mass grows like $σ^d$, so differentiating with respect to the noise scale reveals the intrinsic exponent. Under a regular manifold model, we show uniformly over the model class that the finite-scale field differs from the manifold dimension $d$ by at most $O(σ^2)$. We then establish a minimax lower bound of order $(nσ^d)^{-1}$ for estimating this finite-scale field from $n$ observations, for $n^{-1/(2α+d)}\lesssimσ\leσ_0$. At the smallest scale covered by our lower-bound construction, the bound becomes the nonparametric rate $n^{-2α/(2α+d)}$.

</details>


### [106] [An Analysis of Self-supervised Pre-training with Dependent Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05031)
*Maximilian Fleissner,Debarghya Ghoshdastidar,Samory Kpotufe*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了自监督学习中数据增强的"池化"策略（将多个增强样本一起学习）相较于"划分"基线（将数据分成独立子集分别处理）在估计不变子空间方面的统计优势。理论分析表明，池化的估计误差界从不劣于划分基线，且在掩码、噪声注入等特定增强场景下，池化能实现更快的学习速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习实践中通常将同一数据点的多种增强（如掩码不同像素）池化在一起学习不变子空间，尽管这些增强之间存在明显的相互依赖。然而现有理论工作往往规避此类依赖，仅局限于处理较小规模的独立数据子集，理论与实际之间存在显著差距。

Method: 作者在估计不变子空间V的统计框架下，严格对比了两种策略的估计误差界：(1)池化策略——将所有增强样本(含相互依赖的样本)放在一起学习；(2)划分基线——将数据划分为相互独立的子集并分别处理。作者针对浅层神经网络下的掩码型和噪声注入型增强进行了具体分析。

Result: 证明池化策略的统计估计误差界从不差于划分基线；在特定情况下（如浅层神经网络的掩码或噪声注入增强），朴素池化在增强数量维度上可实现更快的收敛速率。当增强之间的相关性对估计影响温和或有助于降低估计方差时，池化的优势尤为显著。

Conclusion: 该分析为自监督预训练中池化增强样本的成功提供了新见解，并解释了实践中倾向于使用大量增强的直觉依据——池化带来的依赖关系不会损害估计性能，反而可能带来统计收益。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning relies on so-called data augmentations $φ(x)$ of unlabeled datapoints $x$ --- for example, masking random pixels in an image $x$ --- that should leave the label of $x$ invariant and are often used to learn a lower-complexity invariant subspace $\cal V$ for downstream tasks. In practice, such augmentations $\{ φ_l(x_i) \}$ are pooled together to learn $\cal V$, despite obvious inter-dependencies between different augmentations $φ_l(x), φ_k(x)$ of the same datapoint $x$. However, theoretical works on the subject typically consider procedures that avoid such dependencies, and are therefore limited to operate on smaller subsets of independent data.
  We show in this work that pooling augmentations together, despite inter-dependencies, is a better alternative than the baseline of partitioning the data into subsets of independent data. More precisely, in the context of estimating $\cal V$, the statistical estimation error bounds for pooling are never worse than the partitioning baseline, and in some cases --- such as masking or noise injection-based augmentations over a shallow neural network --- naive pooling leads to faster rates in terms of the number of augmentations. The benefits of pooling are particularly prominent when the correlations between different augmentations $φ_l(x), φ_k(x)$ have mild effects on estimation or help decrease the estimation variance. The analysis, therefore, yields new insights into the success of pooling augmented samples in self-supervised pre-training, and provides an intuition behind the practical preference towards using many augmentations.

</details>


### [107] [FluxDisco: Symbolic Regression for Stoichiometric Dynamical Systems via Monte Carlo Graph Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05207)
*Cassandra Durr,Alvaro Köhn-Luque,Chris Jewell,Lloyd A. C. Chapman*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出FluxDisco，一个面向通量基（flux-based）化学计量ODE系统的物理信息引导的动力学符号回归框架，利用已知的化学计量关系缩小表达式搜索空间并保证物理一致性，同时改进蒙特卡洛图搜索算法以应对多个通量联合发现的挑战。在多种物理和生物系统上验证了其准确恢复可解释控制方程的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准动力学符号回归方法虽然能在解释性和预测准确性间取得平衡，但往往会生成违反已知物理定律的表达式。这种物理不可靠性限制了符号回归方法在科学发现中的实际应用价值。

Method: FluxDisco利用已知的化学计量矩阵（stoichiometry）来约束和控制ODE系统的结构，从而缩小表达式搜索空间并确保物理约束的满足。框架将蒙特卡洛图搜索（MCGS）算法进行适配，以解决化学计量系统中联合通量发现（joint flux discovery）的特殊挑战，实现多个通量表达式的协同搜索。

Result: 在多种物理和生物系统的实验中，FluxDisco能够准确恢复控制系统的动力学行为，并以可解释的方程形式呈现，验证了该方法在保证物理一致性的同时兼具识别精度和可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过将物理知识（化学计量）显式嵌入符号回归框架并适配搜索算法，FluxDisco有效解决了标准符号回归方法违反物理定律的问题，为通量型化学计量ODE系统的动力学识别提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamical symbolic regression methods identify governing differential equations from noisy data, balancing interpretability and predictive accuracy. However, standard methods often produce expressions that violate known physical laws. To address this, we propose FluxDisco, a physics-informed framework tailored for flux-based, stoichiometric ODE systems. By leveraging a known stoichiometry, we reduce the expression search space and ensure physical adherence. Our framework adapts the Monte Carlo Graph Search algorithm for the unique challenges associated with joint flux discovery of stoichiometric systems. We evaluate our method across a range of physical and biological systems, demonstrating its ability to accurately recover governing dynamics through interpretable equations.

</details>


### [108] [PAC-Bayesian Reconstruction Guarantees for Time Series Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05212)
*Chloé Hashimoto-Cullen,Ghislain Agoua,Benjamin Guedj,Sylvain Le Corff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为时间序列上的潜变量模型（特别是马尔可夫潜结构生成模型）建立了PAC-Bayesian泛化界，该界不随轨迹长度增长，弥补了VAE等潜变量模型理论分析仅局限于i.i.d.设置的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式潜变量模型在时序预测中应用日益广泛，但现有的PAC-Bayesian理论分析主要针对i.i.d.设置，对现代潜变量模型在时间序列上的泛化保证十分有限，需要建立适用于时序结构的理论框架。

Method: 基于重建（reconstruction）损失界，将PAC-Bayesian保证扩展至马尔可夫潜结构，通过顺序生成过程（sequential generative process）捕获时序依赖，并利用马尔可夫假设使泛化界不依赖于轨迹长度。

Result: 提出了一套不随轨迹长度增长的PAC-Bayesian泛化界，该界依赖文献中常见的假设条件；进一步通过一个示例框架验证这些假设并非过于严格。

Conclusion: 该工作为时序潜变量模型的泛化理论奠定了PAC-Bayesian基础，表明即使时序生成模型具有马尔可夫潜结构，仍可获得不随序列长度退化的理论保证，且所依赖的假设在实践中是可满足的。

Abstract: Forecasting time series accurately is critical for applications with complex data ranging from energy systems to healthcare and finance. Among current state of the art models, generative latent variable models are increasingly implemented; yet principled generalisation guarantees for modern latent variable models remain limited. In particular, while Variational AutoEncoders are widely used for sequential data, their theoretical analysis is largely restricted to i.i.d. settings. In this work, we develop a PAC-Bayesian framework for latent variables models applied to time series. Building on reconstruction-based bounds, we extend PAC-Bayesian guarantees to Markovian latent structures, capturing temporal dependencies through a sequential generative process. These guarantees do not grow with the length of the trajectory. Our bounds depend on assumptions which are common in the literature; we provide an example framework where they would be verified to show that they are not as restrictive as they may seem.

</details>

### [109] [Characterizing periodic orbits in two-dimensional Rayleigh-Bénard flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04477)
*Joaquín Cullen,Melisa Y. Vinograd,Patricio Clark di Leoni*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文在Pr=1的二维Rayleigh-Bénard对流中，在通向混沌的转变附近计算并刻画了一个稳态和三个周期轨道族，利用Floquet分析研究其稳定性与分岔，揭示了流场在准周期化后在多组轨道间跳跃，并发现所有对称性在混沌开始前就已破缺，且混沌的起始不改变热输运行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不稳定周期轨道和稳态被认为构成时空混沌与湍流的骨架，然而在热驱动流动的过渡态中，对其计算仍然稀少。作者旨在填补这一空白，理解二维Rayleigh-Bénard对流从有序到混沌转变过程中的轨道动力学本质。

Method: 在Pr=1的二维Rayleigh-Bénard系统中，通过数值计算求解稳态与三族周期轨道；利用Floquet分析研究轨道稳定性并刻画其分岔特征；通过跟踪流场对轨道的阴影（shadowing）行为，确定各轨道在不同参数区间的动力学相关性。

Result: 揭示了流场在通向混沌的路途中先经历准周期化，随后在若干组轨道之间跳跃；鉴定了主频与次频的出现以及相位锁定机制均与轨道动力学相关；发现所有对称性在混沌开始前即已破缺，且混沌的起始并未改变热输运行为。

Conclusion: 不稳定周期轨道与稳态确实构成混沌转变动态的骨干结构。对称性破缺先于混沌开始，而热输运在混沌开始前后保持连续，表明混沌的出现并非由全局对称性破缺驱动，也不直接影响宏观传热特征。

Abstract: Unstable periodic orbits and steady states are believed to form the backbone of spatiotemporal chaos and turbulence, yet their computation in thermally driven flows remains scarce for transitional regimes. In this work we compute and characterize a steady state and three families of periodic orbits in two-dimensional Rayleigh-Bénard at $\mathrm{Pr}=1$, near the transition to chaos. We find that in its route to chaos, the flow hops between several sets of orbits after becoming quasiperiodic. We use Floquet analysis to study the stability of the orbits obtained and characterize their bifurcations, showing how the appearance of primary and secondary frequencies, as well as phase-locking mechanisms, are all related to the dynamics of the orbits. We study in detail how the flow shadows the orbits found and determine in which regimes each orbit is dynamically relevant or not. Our analysis also reveals two important insights: (1) all symmetries are broken before the onset of chaos, and (2) this onset does not alter the behavior of the heat transport.

</details>


### [110] [Two-phase Temperature Reconstruction in Ice-Water Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04479)
*Zhukun Wang,Daisuke Noto,Douglas J. Jerolmack,Hugo N. Ulloa*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理的数据同化方法，通过粒子追踪测速获取液体速度场，并结合热边界与运动学边界条件，在水-冰耦合体系（液态水中的对流与冰中热传导）中重建温度场并量化跨界面热输运，在实验室验证中成功应用于cabbeling诱导对流的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解水与冰相互作用时的热输运过程对研究经历相变的水体至关重要，但现有实验方法缺乏一种最小侵入式的手段来同时解析液态水和非等温冰系统中的温度场，限制了实验进展。

Method: 提出一种基于物理的数据同化方法：利用粒子追踪测速（PTV）提供液体速度场，以热边界条件和运动学边界条件约束反问题；将液态水中的对流-扩散方程与冰中的热传导方程耦合，重建同时刻的平均温度场，并量化水-冰界面的热输运，该方法具有最小侵入性且兼容自由表面系统。

Result: 在实验室实验中进行了演示验证，实验中与冰接触的液态水温度范围驱动了cabbeling诱导的对流，成功重建了温度场并定量表征了水-冰界面的热输运。

Conclusion: 该框架使冷冻圈水生系统中耦合热流体动力学的研究成为可能，包括冰-水界面热交换及液相能量学，并可推广至食品与能源工业中的相变过程。

Abstract: What controls heat transport when liquid water interacts with ice? Answering this question is essential for understanding water bodies undergoing phase change. Yet experimental progress remains limited by the lack of a minimally invasive methodology for simultaneously resolving temperature fields in coupled liquid water and non-isothermal ice systems. Here, we introduce a physics-based data-assimilation method for reconstructing temperature fields in buoyancy-driven flows interacting with non-isothermal ice. Particle tracking velocimetry provides the liquid velocity field, while thermal and kinematic boundary conditions constrain the inverse problem. The method couples advection--diffusion in liquid water with conduction in ice to reconstruct simultaneous mean temperature fields and quantify heat transport across the water--ice interface, while remaining minimally invasive and compatible with free-surface systems. We demonstrate the method in laboratory experiments in which the temperature range across the liquid water in contact with ice drives cabbeling-induced convection. This framework enables investigation of coupled thermo-fluid dynamics in cryospheric aquatic systems, including heat exchange at the ice-water interface and liquid-phase energetics, with broader applications to phase-change processes in food and energy industries.

</details>


### [111] [Coherent-structure dynamics in wall turbulence from state-space trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04515)
*Emma Lenz,Ahmed Elnahhas,H. Jane Bae*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过将相干结构能量表示为低维状态空间中的轨迹，并利用网络模体分析重复出现的轨迹模式，识别了湍流壁面边界层近壁区的动力学过程。研究涵盖三种构型（Re_τ≈180的MFU、同雷诺数的全尺度通道、Re_τ≈2200的MFU），发现多个普遍存在的模体表明近壁动力学在不同背景流下具有鲁棒性，且其倾向于沿特定动力学路径演化，该路径受外层活动调节但不会消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流壁面流的近壁区动力学过程复杂，传统分析方法难以系统识别其中的关键动力学路径。已有研究集中在经典的自我维持过程（SSP），但对是否存在超越经典再生循环的优选动力学路径、以及这些路径如何受外层流动活动影响等问题尚不清楚。本文旨在通过低维状态空间结合网络模体分析的方法，系统识别并比较不同流动条件下的近壁动力学过程。

Method: 采用直接数值模拟（DNS）计算三种构型：Re_τ≈180的最小流动单元（MFU）隔离SSP、同雷诺数的全尺度通道中风研究近壁结构间相互作用、Re_τ≈2200的MFU研究近壁/外层耦合。使用本征正交分解（POD）识别对应条纹（streaks）、滚流（rolls）和蜿蜒结构（meanders）的模态，将流动投影到这些模态上跟踪其随时间演化的能量，形成低维状态空间轨迹。随后通过网络模体分析识别这些轨迹中具有统计显著性的动力学路径。

Result: 多种模体在三种构型中普遍存在，表明近壁动力学在不同背景流下具有鲁棒性。除恢复经典SSP相关模体之外，还发现多个与SSP无直接对应的同等显著模体，表明优选近壁动力学超出经典再生循环。针对Re_τ≈180 MFU在特定外层能量条件下的分析显示：平静的外层状态产生类似低雷诺数全通道的动力学，而高能外层状态促进高能爆发事件。研究表明近壁湍流沿优选动力学路径演化，外层活动会改变这些路径的盛行程度但不会将其消除。

Conclusion: 近壁湍流存在超越经典SSP的多种优选动力学路径，这些路径在不同雷诺数和不同背景流条件下保持稳健。外层流体活动对近壁动力学路径的盛行程度起调节作用，但并不能消除或推翻近壁区固有的动力学偏好。研究为理解壁湍流近壁动力学的系统组织和干预控制提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We identify dynamical processes in the near-wall region of turbulent wall-bounded flow by representing coherent-structure energy as trajectories in a low-dimensional state space and analyzing recurring trajectory patterns using network motifs. Direct numerical simulations (DNS) are performed for three configurations: a minimal flow unit (MFU) at $Re_τ\approx 180$ to isolate the self-sustaining process (SSP), a full-scale channel at the same Reynolds number to study interactions between near-wall structures, and an MFU at $Re_τ\approx 2200$ to investigate near-wall/outer-layer coupling. Proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) is used to identify modes corresponding to streaks, rolls, and meandering structures of the SSP, and the flow is projected onto these modes to track their energy over time. Motif analysis then identifies statistically significant dynamical pathways in the resulting state-space trajectories. Several motifs are common to all three configurations, indicating robust near-wall dynamics across different background flows. While motifs associated with the classical SSP are recovered, equally prominent motifs with no direct SSP analogue are also identified, demonstrating that preferred near-wall dynamics extend beyond the canonical regeneration cycle. Conditioning the $Re_τ\approx 180$ MFU on outer-layer energy further shows that quiescent outer-layer states produce dynamics resembling those of the low-Reynolds-number full channel, whereas energetic outer-layer states promote high-energy bursting events. These results demonstrate that near-wall turbulence evolves along preferred dynamical pathways whose prevalence is modified, but not eliminated, by outer-layer activity.

</details>


### [112] [An Aeroelastic Solver Integrating Reformulated-Vortex-Particle and Finite-Element Methods across Non-Conforming Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05243)
*Neeraj Balachandar,A. Padmaprabhan,Vishnu R. Unni*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: VarFlExI 是一个模块化气动弹性求解器，用于柔性升力面的双向流固耦合（FSI）建模。它采用改进的涡粒子法（rVPM）求解不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程，无需昂贵的体积网格划分；结构侧使用Reissner-Mindlin板单元有限元离散并结合广义-α时间积分。流体与结构求解器通过显式交错分区方案耦合，并通过中间界面实现功守恒的力和几何传递。框架经水洞实验验证，能以显著降低的计算成本准确预测耦合气动弹性响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真流固耦合模拟通常需要昂贵的体积网格划分和大量计算资源。现有方法要么在气动精度上妥协，要么在计算效率上不足。作者旨在开发一个模块化、可变保真度的气动弹性求解器，能够在保持精度的同时显著降低计算成本，并支持灵活的结构与气动模型组合。

Method: 该方法包括：(1) 使用改进的涡粒子法（rVPM）求解不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程，避免体积网格的生成；(2) 集成FLOWUnsteady框架实现可变保真度气动建模；(3) 结构侧采用Reissner-Mindlin板理论，通过FEniCS框架中的有限元法离散，使用广义-α方法进行时间积分，非线性平衡方程由Gauss-Newton过程求解；(4) 通过显式交错分区方案耦合流体和结构求解器；(5) 为处理气动载荷和几何的多代表性特征，通过中间界面使用分离的功守恒力传递和逆向几何传递算子。

Result: 通过与水洞实验对比验证，框架能够准确预测耦合气动弹性响应。无网格气动求解器的计算效率使得模拟能以显著更低的计算成本进行而不损失精度。此外，通过敏感性分析和参数研究（涵盖不同流动条件、结构属性和耦合参数）对求解器进行了进一步评估。

Conclusion: VarFlExI 提供了一种高效且模块化的气动弹性FSI求解框架，通过无网格气动求解器大幅降低了计算成本，并通过功守恒的界面耦合确保了跨非匹配界面的物理一致性。实验验证表明其能够可靠预测柔性升力面的耦合气动弹性行为，为后续参数化研究和设计优化提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We present $\texttt{VarFlExI}$ (Variable Fidelity Unsteady Flow-FEniCS Exchange Interface), a modular aeroelastic solver for modeling two-way fluid-structure interaction (FSI) of flexible lifting surfaces. The framework employs a reformulated Vortex Particle Method (rVPM) to solve the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations without the need for computationally expensive volumetric meshing, while supporting variable-fidelity aerodynamic modeling using the $\texttt{FLOWUnsteady}$ framework. On the structural side, a Reissner-Mindlin plate formulation is discretized using the finite element method and integrated in time through the generalized-$α$ method, with the nonlinear equilibrium equations solved using a Gauss-Newton procedure within the $\texttt{FEniCS}$ framework. Fluid and structural solvers are coupled through an explicit staggered partitioned scheme, ensuring conservation of virtual work for load and displacement transfer across the non-matching interface. To accommodate the multi-representative nature of the aerodynamic loads and geometry, the interface coupling employs separate work-conservative force and reverse-geometry transfer operators via a common intermediate interface. The framework is validated against water-tunnel experiments, demonstrating accurate prediction of the coupled aeroelastic response rather than independent validation of the constituent solvers. The computational efficiency of the meshless aerodynamic solver enables simulations at significantly lower computational cost while maintaining accuracy. The solver is further evaluated through sensitivity analyses and parameter studies spanning different flow conditions, structural properties, and coupling parameters.

</details>


### [113] [Quasi-Two-Dimensional Simulation of a Rotating Detonation Engine Combustor and Injector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04519)
*S. She-Ming Lau-Chapdelaine,Matei I. Radulescu,Zekai Hong*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文对化学计量比氢氧环形旋转爆轰发动机进行了数值模拟，提出了一种通用的准二维方法来模拟喷注器和燃烧室中的面积变化，研究了爆轰与喷注器之间的相互作用。研究发现喷注器扩张段形成激波导致总压不可恢复损失，燃烧室总压增益不足以弥补该损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转爆轰发动机（RDE）中爆轰波与喷注器之间的相互作用对发动机性能有重要影响，理解喷注器内部激波形成及其对总压损失的影响，对于评估和优化RDE的总压增益性能至关重要。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法对环形旋转爆轰发动机进行模拟，使用准二维方法（Q2D）来建模喷注器和燃烧室中的面积变化，喷注器喉部面积与燃烧室面积之比为1:4，并通过Favre平均稳态分析流线和粒子路径来研究激波对总压的影响。

Result: 喷注器扩张段因燃烧室内爆轰产生的高背压而形成了激波；Favre平均稳态分析表明该激波导致总压产生不可恢复的损失；燃烧室内的总压增益不足以弥补这一损失，流体以低于集气腔总压的状态离开发动机。

Conclusion: 喷注器内激波引起的总压损失是RDE整体总压性能下降的关键因素，简单的面积比配置（如4:1）下无法实现净总压增益，需要进一步优化喷注器设计以减小激波损失。

Abstract: A numerical simulation of an annular rotating detonation engine with stoichiometric hydrogen-oxygen is performed. A generic, well posed, and easily implemented approach using a quasi-two-dimensional method to model the area variations through the rotating detonation engine's injector and combustor is presented. The detonation--injector interaction is studied for the case with a ratio of four between the combustor and injector's throat areas. A shock wave is formed in the divergent portion of the injector due the high back pressure created by the detonation in the combustor. A Favre-averaged steady-state analysis of stream lines and particle paths reveals that the shock causes an irrecoverable loss of stagnation pressure. Stagnation pressure gain in the combustor is insufficient to make up for the loss and the flow leaves the engine with lower stagnation pressure than in the plenum.

</details>


### [114] [Lagrangian dynamics unveil polymer conformation in viscoelastic flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04538)
*Louison Thorens,Gabriel Juarez,Jeffrey S. Guasto,Paulo E. Arratia*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过直接拉格朗日追踪单个DNA分子，揭示了微流控粘弹性流动中聚合物链的构象动力学，发现标准本构模型未能完全捕捉瞬态动力学，并证明聚合物弛豫与形状存在显著各向异性，通过流体动力阻力耦合旋转与拉伸，调节其拉格朗日力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性流体的复杂流动行为源于流体应力与聚合物链非平衡构象动力学之间的反馈。尽管本构模型能预测简单几何中的流动和应力场，但对混合运动学和非平凡聚合物动力学调控机制的机理理解仍然缺乏，量化流动中聚合物链的历史依赖构象对解决这些复杂系统至关重要。

Method: 采用直接拉格朗日追踪技术对微流控粘弹性流动中单个DNA分子进行实时追踪，考察不同聚合物轮廓长度和浓度下的分子构象动力学，并将测量的分子延伸与取向结果与典型本构模型预测进行对比。

Result: 测量得到的分子延伸与取向结果与典型本构模型存在偏差，说明现有模型未能完全捕捉瞬态动力学。测量显示聚合物弛豫与形状存在显著各向异性，这种各向异性通过流体动力阻力将聚合物的旋转与延伸耦合，从而调控其拉格朗日力学。

Conclusion: 研究强调拉格朗日聚合物历史的重要性，并指出现有本构描述需考虑非平凡的分子内力学机制，以改进粘弹性流动的描述精度。

Abstract: The complex flow behavior of viscoelastic fluids emerges from a feedback between fluid stress and the out-of-equilibrium conformational dynamics of their constituent polymer chains. While constitutive models can predict flow and stress fields in simple geometries, a fundamental understanding of the mechanisms regulating viscoelastic flows with mixed kinematics and non-trivial polymer dynamics remains elusive. Quantifying the history-dependent conformation of polymer chains in flow is essential to resolve these complex systems. To address this knowledge gap, we employ direct Lagrangian tracking of individual DNA molecules to reveal their conformational dynamics in microfluidic viscoelastic flows for different polymer contour lengths and concentrations. Comparing the measured molecular extension and orientation reveals discrepancies with canonical constitutive models, which do not fully capture the transient dynamics. Our measurements show a distinct anisotropy in polymer relaxation and shape, which couple the polymer's rotation and extension through hydrodynamic drag, regulating their Lagrangian mechanics. These findings emphasize the importance of Lagrangian polymer history and the need to account for non-trivial intra-molecular mechanics to improve constitutive descriptions of viscoelastic flows.

</details>


### [115] [Transport fidelity and domain of validity of compact Gaussian kinetic representations for rarefied flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04606)
*Ehsan Roohi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了用于离散速度法（DVM）动力学状态的局域高斯紧致表示策略，在两个基准（单原子正激波和顶盖驱动方腔流）中验证其传输保真度。结果表明，在固定系数预算下，该高斯方法在守恒量上达到亚百分比误差，在非平衡量上仅1-2%误差，相较于多线性网格（89-98%误差）和双线性/SVD基线分别降低约7-24倍的最大误差，并通过保持对应性的局域基将拒收马赫数外推误差从42.86%降至11.45%，提供了参数化使用的定量判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀薄流动的紧致表示需要在保留非平衡输运信息的同时界定其适用范围。现有DVM状态的高效表示往往在存储效率与信息保真度之间难以兼顾，缺乏对不同基准下表示策略统一性能的量化评估，亟需一种既能紧凑表示动力学状态又保持输运保真度的方法。

Method: 采用局域高斯策略分别针对两类动力学状态进行专门化处理：(1) 对于正激波分布，利用正相空间混合（positive phase-space mixture）表示激波分布，并通过求积（quadrature）再生其矩层级；(2) 对于方腔流动，采用具有共享支撑（shared support）的物理空间映射表示20个流场量。研究中对比了多线性网格、双线性基线和SVD基线，并在不同马赫数条件下测试了保持对应性的局域基，同时引入归一化坐标守卫以拒绝训练范围外的马赫数。

Result: 对单独拟合的Mach-3和Mach-5激波，守恒量误差低于1%，输运和高阶矩误差约为1-2%；在相同的4608系数预算下，多线性网格产生89-98%的非平衡量误差。在两种方腔基准中，高斯映射显著优于双线性和SVD基线，最大误差降低约7-24倍。在Mach条件化测试中，保持对应性的局域基将阻留的Mach-6分布误差从42.86±5.40%降至11.45±0.94%，但输运误差仍保持30-40%；归一化坐标守卫能拒绝训练范围外的Mach 12数据。独立网格研究确认这些趋势不受DVM离散化误差主导。

Conclusion: 研究确立了局域高斯表示作为拟合动力学状态的高存储效率、高输运保真度映射方法，并提供了一套用于参数化使用的定量接受准则。该方法局部支撑、连续求值、输运保真和系数计数核算的统一结构在两个基准中得到了验证。

Abstract: Compact representations of rarefied flows must retain nonequilibrium transport information while identifying their range of validity. We investigate a common localized-Gaussian strategy for discrete-velocity-method (DVM) states in monatomic normal shocks and lid-driven cavities. The strategy is specialized to the available kinetic state: a positive phase-space mixture represents shock distributions and regenerates their moment hierarchy by quadrature, whereas a shared-support physical-space map represents 20 cavity fields. Localized support, continuous evaluation, transport fidelity, and coefficient-count accounting therefore provide the common structure across the two benchmarks. For separately fitted Mach-3 and Mach-5 shocks, the method gives sub-percent errors in conserved quantities and approximately 1--2\% errors in transport and higher-order moments. At the same 4608-coefficient budget, the tested multilinear grids produce 89--98\% errors in these nonequilibrium quantities. For both cavity cases, the Gaussian map also outperforms matched bilinear and singular-value-decomposition baselines, reducing maximum errors by factors of approximately 7--24. In the Mach-conditioned tests, a correspondence-preserving local basis reduces the withheld Mach-6 distribution error from $42.86\pm5.40\%$ to $11.45\pm0.94\%$ at fixed storage, while its transport errors remain 30--40\%; a normalized-coordinate guard rejects Mach 12 outside the training range. Independent grid studies confirm that these trends are not dominated by DVM discretization error. The results establish localized Gaussian representations as storage-efficient transport-fidelity maps for fitted kinetic states and provide quantitative acceptance criteria for parametric use.

</details>


### [116] [Elastic turbulence in straight confined geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04617)
*Giulio Foggi Rota,Daulet Izbassarov,Aditya Tekriwal,Marco Edoardo Rosti*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文报道了在直管和矩形管道两种几何构型中均观察到持续的弹性湍流（ET）混沌动力学，并证明它们与理想化配置（如周期剪切和平板通道流）中识别的ET状态是同一状态，从而确立ET为与几何无关的吸引子，并指出管道中存在不同的转变机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性湍流在直线几何中已被广泛研究，但实验通常局限于受限系统（如管道），且管道中混沌弹性波动的起始存在争议。作者旨在厘清管道与管道系统中ET状态的统一性，解决几何约束导致的争议。

Method: 在直管（pipe）和矩形管道（duct）两种几何构型中开展实验研究，观测并对比混沌弹性动力学行为，与理想化配置（周期剪切、平板通道流）中已识别的ET状态进行关联和比对。

Result: 在两种几何构型中均观察到持续的混沌动力学，且证明其与理想化装置中的ET状态完全一致，表明ET是一种与几何无关的吸引子；同时发现管道中ET的转变机制与先前理解不同。

Conclusion: 弹性湍流是一类不受几何构型限制的普适吸引子状态，管道中的ET起始遵循一种独特的转变机制，这完善了对弹性湍流触发条件的认识。

Abstract: Elastic turbulence (ET) in straight geometries has been extensively studied in idealised configurations such as periodic shear and planar channel flow, yet experiments are typically restricted to confined systems like ducts and pipes. While chaotic elastic fluctuations are observed in ducts, their onset in pipes is debated due to geometric constraints. Here we report sustained chaotic dynamics in both geometries and show they correspond to the same ET state identified in idealised setups. Our results establish ET as a geometry-independent attractor and point to a distinct transition mechanism in pipes.

</details>


### [117] [Homoclinic-shedding in the steady forced water-wave problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04888)
*Jack Keeler,Mark Blyth*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究无粘无旋流体流过局部地形凹陷时的稳态孤立波解结构，通过fKdV模型的精确稳态解和全非线性Euler系统的数值解，探究弗劳德数从临界流延伸到超临界流时解结构的变化，以及地形衰减率对解结构的影响。研究发现高度非平凡的包含螺旋和闭合环的分岔结构，其特点是"同宿脱落"现象——振幅变化时成对的孤立波向上游和下游发射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究发现无粘无旋流体流过局部地形凹陷时，在临界流（弗劳德数为1）条件下存在无穷多组稳态孤立波解分支，但存在两个未解决问题：i) 当流速延伸到超临界区域时解结构如何变化？ii) 地形衰减率如何影响解结构？本文旨在回答这些问题。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：1) fKdV (forced Korteweg-de Vries) 模型的精确稳态解分析；2) 全非线性Euler系统的数值解。研究三种地形：i) 沟槽（trench）、ii) 高斯（Gaussian）和 iii) 广义Agnesi地形。

Result: 发现了一个高度非平凡的分岔结构，由螺旋和闭合环组成，其特征是"同宿脱落"（homoclinic shedding）现象——当振幅变化时，成对的孤立波从上游和下游方向发射出来。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了超临界流区域中地形凹陷诱发的孤立波解的丰富分岔结构，证明了地形形状（特别是衰减率）对解结构有显著影响，并发现了"同宿脱落"这一新型动力学现象。

Abstract: Recently, an infinite set of steady solitary-wave solution branches was discovered for inviscid and irrotational flow over a localised topographic depression. These solutions were restricted to critical flow, when the Froude number is unity. Several questions were raised, including i) what happens to the solution structure as the flow speed is continued into the supercritical regime? and ii) how does the decay rate of the topography affect the solution structure? In this article, we answer these questions by examining exact steady solutions of the fKdV model and numerical solutions of the fully nonlinear Euler system over i) trench, ii) Gaussian and iii) generalised Agnesi topographies. We uncover a highly non-trivial bifurcation structure consisting of spirals and closed loops, characterised by the phenomenon of `homoclinic shedding', where pairs of solitary waves are emitted up- and downstream as the amplitude is varied.

</details>


### [118] [Unveiling wing turbulence dynamics through explainable deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05015)
*Samuel Molina-Casino,Andrés Cremades,Sergio Hoyas,José I. Cardesa,François Chedevergne,Ricardo Vinuesa*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用可解释人工智能（XAI）方法研究飞机机翼湍流边界层中相干结构的三维组织。作者训练深度神经网络预测流动短期演化，并通过Shapley值归因方法识别高相关流区，发现在流动减速至尾缘过程中，预测重要性从近壁低速结构转移至高低速流体组成的三维结构对，这些结构对不匹配任何经典相干结构家族，且具有几何不变性和体积扩张特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机翼上方湍流中的相干结构三维组织至今难以捉摸，限制了我们减少燃油消耗的能力。传统方法依赖预定义的经典运动学判据来识别结构，需要一种基于预测动力学的新方法来揭示传统范式遗漏的动态相关结构。

Method: 采用可解释人工智能方法：首先训练深度神经网络预测流动的短期演化，然后使用Shapley值归因方法识别对预测贡献最大的高相关性流动区域，以此描述相干结构——不是基于经典预定义的运动学判据，而是基于预测流动演化的能力。

Result: 研究发现：流动向尾缘减速时，预测重要性从近壁低速结构转向高低速流体区域组成的三维配对结构；这些配对不匹配单一经典相干结构家族，且随流动趋近分离逐渐主导动态相关流动；多数配对以展向并排方式排列，包围近垂直动量界面并具有最强速度跳跃；其几何在粘性单位下保持不变，而接近分离时体积扩张。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了传统范式忽视的预测性组织规律，为湍流流动控制开辟了新途径，有助于最终实现减少燃料消耗的目标。

Abstract: The three-dimensional organization of coherent structures in the turbulent flow over aircraft wings remains elusive, limiting our ability to reduce fuel consumption. Here, we use explainable artificial intelligence to characterize these structures by predicted flow evolution rather than classical predefined kinematic criteria. After training a deep neural network to predict the short-term evolution of the flow, we identify the highest-relevance flow regions using Shapley-value attribution methods. We have found that as the flow decelerates towards the trailing edge, predictive importance shifts from near-wall low-speed structures to three-dimensional pairs of high- and low-speed fluid regions. Matching no single classical structure family, these pairs progressively dominate the dynamically relevant flow. Most stand spanwise side-by-side, enclosing a near-vertical momentum interface with the strongest velocity jump. Their geometry remains invariant in viscous units while their volume expands when approaching separation. Our results reveal a predictive organization that traditional paradigms overlook, opening new ways to control turbulent flows.

</details>


### [119] [Fisher Information Dynamics: A Kinematic Framework for Phase Space Ordering with Applications to Shock Layers and Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05021)
*Yingchuan Wu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文建立了基于Fisher信息产生率的相空间有序化运动学框架。从连续性方程推导出各向同性收缩、无迹剪切、散度梯度和边界通量的四项分解；推广至Fokker-Planck方程时严格证明了扩散耗散项的非正性；证明了纯旋度速度场不改变有序度量的涡量无关定理；对Burgers激波层精确解发现了压缩项与散度梯度项逐点恒等、构成三项平衡的内在关系；二维湍流模拟验证了剪切-耗散平衡；关联Wasserstein梯度流的变分稳态与分解平衡。该框架为非平衡系统的有序化动力学提供了诊断与预测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在建立一个通用的运动学/几何框架来量化和预测非平衡系统中有序结构的生成与破坏。现有的有序化分析缺乏统一的动力学诊断工具，特别是缺乏将Fisher信息（作为序的度量）的时间演化分解为具有明确几何含义的独立物理机制的严格数学框架。

Method: 基于连续性方程推导Fisher信息产生率的四项分解（各向同性收缩、无迹剪切、散度梯度、边界通量）；将框架推广至Fokker-Planck方程并证明扩散耗散项非正性；通过涡量无关定理建立旋度场不变量；利用Burgers激波层精确解进行解析验证；通过二维湍流数值模拟定量验证剪切-耗散平衡；并与Wasserstein梯度流的变分理论建立联系。

Result: (1) 严格的四项运动学分解；(2) 扩散项的严格非正性证明；(3) 涡量无关定理的证明；(4) Burgers激波解中压缩项与散度梯度项的逐点恒等关系及三项平衡；(5) 二维湍流中剪切-耗散平衡的数值验证；(6) Wasserstein梯度流变分稳态与分解平衡的等价性。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一个以Fisher信息产生率为核心、具有解析可验证性和数值可检验性的有序化诊断框架，可作为非平衡系统（如流体湍流、激波等）中有序结构演化的预测性分析工具。分解的各项均具有明确的几何解释，为后续应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We establish a kinematic framework for phase space ordering based on the Fisher information production rate. From the continuity equation we derive a four-term decomposition into isotropic contraction, traceless shear, divergence gradient, and boundary flux. Extending to the Fokker-Planck equation, we prove the strict non-positivity of the diffusive dissipation term. We prove a vorticity-independence theorem: purely rotational velocity fields do not alter the order measure. We derive explicit geometric sign criteria for each deformation term, enabling prediction of order generation or destruction. Through exact solutions of the Burgers shock layer, we uncover a pointwise identity between the compression and divergence-gradient contributions, underlying the three-term balance. Two-dimensional turbulence simulations verify the shear-dissipation balance. We further show that variational steady states of the associated Wasserstein gradient flow imply decomposition balance. This framework provides a diagnostic and predictive tool for analyzing ordering dynamics in non-equilibrium systems.

</details>

### [120] [The Erdős similarity conjecture and Rajchman measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04456)
*A. Iosevich,N. Kulkarni,N. Mora Cuéllar,I. Rojas Aravena,A. Yavicoli*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了：若集合 A⊆ℝ 支撑一个 Fourier--Stieltjes 变换在无穷远处趋于零的概率测度（即 Rajchman 测度），则对任意 ε∈(0,1)，存在闭的、1-周期的、无处稠密集合 E⊆ℝ，使得每个单位区间 I 满足 m(E∩I)≥1−ε，且 E 不含 A 的任何仿射拷贝。这以统一的大集合形式验证了 Erdős 相似性猜想中任何支撑 Rajchman 测度的集合的情形。同时作者还改进了 Ivašev-Musatov 的经典定理：对任意 Hausdorff 度量函数 h，存在 h-零紧致 Rajchman 支撑集 K，其对数上盒维数与对数填充分维均为 1，且 1 对两维都是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős 相似性猜想是调和分析与分形几何交叉领域的经典开放问题，它问的是：任何具有正 Lebesgue 测度的集合是否必定包含某个给定非收缩序列（如 {1/n}）的仿射拷贝？本文关注更强的"大集合形式"——集合应在每个单位区间上占据几乎全部测度。作者试图证明所有支撑 Rajchman 测度的集合（一类非常广泛的零测度集，涵盖 Cantor 集等）都满足这一性质，从而极大推广已有的部分结果。

Method: 证明的核心思想是将问题分解为两层：(1) 利用所给 Rajchman 测度大伸缩下的模 1 等分布性质（equidistribution modulo one），确保"无处可隐藏"的条件；(2) 构造一个多尺度（multiscale）的低密度周期阻塞块（periodic blockers）家族，将这些 блокer 叠加到目标集合上，使 A 的任何仿射拷贝在每个尺度上都被拦截。关键是该方法完全不需要任何 Fourier 衰减的定量速率，仅依赖 Rajchman 条件本身。对于 Ivašev-Musatov 定理的改进，作者构造了 h-零的紧致 Rajchman 支撑集，并通过精细的维度分析证明其对数上盒维数与对数填充分维达到 1。

Result: 主要结果为：每个支撑 Rajchman 测度的集合 A 都满足 Erdős 相似性猜想的统一大集合形式，即存在在每个单位区间上占满几乎全部测度（≥1−ε）的闭、周期、无处稠密集合 E，不包含 A 的仿射拷贝。次要结果为：改进了 Ivašev-Musatov 定理，证明了存在 h-零的紧致 Rajchman 支撑集，其对数的上盒维数和填充分维同时达到 1，且该值在两种维度意义下均为最优/尖锐的。

Conclusion: 本文在 Erdős 相似性猜想的"大集合形式"方向上取得了本质进展，将所有支撑 Rajchman 测度的集合都纳入满足该猜想的范畴，且结果不依赖任何 Fourier 衰减速率这一脆弱条件，体现了方法的稳健性。同时，对经典 Ivašev-Musatov 定理的改进揭示了 Rajchman 支撑集在对数维度结构上可达到的最大可能的"丰满度"。

Abstract: Let $A\subseteq\mathbb{R}$ support a probability measure whose Fourier--Stieltjes transform tends to zero at infinity. We prove that, for every $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$, there is a closed, $1$-periodic, nowhere dense set $E\subseteq\mathbb{R}$ such that \[ m(E\cap I)\ge1-\varepsilon \] for every interval $I$ of length $1$, while $E$ contains no affine copy of $A$. Thus every set supporting a Rajchman measure satisfies the Erdős similarity conjecture in a uniform large-set form. The proof combines equidistribution modulo one for large dilates of the measure with a multiscale family of low-density periodic blockers; no quantitative rate of Fourier decay is used. We also refine a classical theorem of Ivašev-Musatov, showing that for every Hausdorff gauge $h$ there is an $h$-null compact Rajchman support $K$ satisfying \[ \overline{\dim}_{\mathrm B}^{\log} K=\dim_{\mathrm P}^{\log} K=1. \] The value $1$ is sharp for both dimensions.

</details>


### [121] [Linear Growth and Nonlinear Stability of Two-Dimensional MHD Couette Flow with Vertical Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04584)
*Tao Liang,Jiahong Wu,Xiaoping Zhai*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维不可压缩磁流体动力学（MHD）系统在环面×实线域上、各向异性耗散（仅垂直方向有粘性与磁扩散）条件下，围绕Couette平衡态的稳定性问题。在线性层面证明了涡量与电流密度的线性增长、垂直分量的无粘阻尼；在非线性层面通过引入剪切自适应的傅里叶乘子，在水平背景磁场与耗散参数兼容性条件下建立了全局非线性稳定性，并得到增强耗散与无粘阻尼的定量衰减估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有剪切流动与磁场共同作用的MHD系统中，各向异性耗散如何影响流体的稳定性与长期动力学行为。Couette流动与横向磁场是研究剪切流不稳定性与增强耗散效应的基准模型，但各向异性情形下的线性增长机制、共振回波效应及其与非线性的相互作用尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 对线性化问题，利用放大函数的谱分析证明涡量与电流密度在|β|>1/2时具有线性时间增长；对非线性问题，引入剪切自适应的傅里叶乘子，同时刻画增强耗散、临界时间效应与回波型共振相互作用；在水平背景磁场和耗散参数间的定量兼容性条件下，结合能量方法建立全局非线性稳定性。

Result: 线性层面：当|β|>1/2时涡量与电流密度以线性时间速率增长（尖锐）；水平速度与磁场扰动量一致有界；垂直分量以⟨t⟩⁻¹速率无粘阻尼。非线性层面：在初始扰动满足H^N范数界ε₀·min{μ,ν}^{1/2}（N≥4）时，系统全局稳定；非零水平Fourier模态以增强耗散速率e^{-c·min{μ,ν}^{1/3}t}衰减，垂直速度与磁场分量额外获得⟨t⟩⁻¹的无粘阻尼因子。

Conclusion: 在各向异性（仅垂直耗散）情形下，水平背景磁场是稳定剪切流的关键物理机制：当磁场足够强（|β|>1/2），线性系统呈现涡量与电流的线性增长但整体仍保持稳定；非线性系统在兼容性条件下达到全局渐近稳定，展现出增强耗散与无粘阻尼的协同效应，且稳定性阈值允许随耗散参数趋于零而松弛至ε₀量级。

Abstract: We study the two-dimensional incompressible magnetohydrodynamic system near the Couette equilibrium $\bigl((y,0)^{\rm T},(β,0)^{\rm T}\bigr)$ on $\mathbb T\times\mathbb R$, in the anisotropic regime where both viscosity and magnetic diffusivity act only in the vertical direction. For the inviscid linearized problem with $|β|>1/2$, we prove sharp linear-in-time growth of the vorticity and current density at the level of the time rate. In contrast, the horizontal components of the velocity and magnetic perturbations remain uniformly bounded, while the vertical components exhibit quantitative inviscid damping at the rate $\langle t\rangle^{-1}$. For the nonlinear problem, we introduce shear-adapted Fourier multipliers that simultaneously capture enhanced dissipation, critical-time effects, and echo-type resonant interactions. Under a suitable horizontal background magnetic field and a quantitative compatibility condition between the magnetic field strength, viscosity, and magnetic diffusivity, we establish global nonlinear stability for divergence-free perturbations satisfying $ \left\| \bigl( \mathbf v_{\rm in}-(y,0)^{\rm T}, \mathbf H_{\rm in}-(β,0)^{\rm T} \bigr) \right\|_{H^N} \leq \varepsilon_0\min\{μ,ν\}^{1/2}, N\geq4$. Moreover, the nonzero horizontal Fourier modes decay in a shear-adapted $H^N$ norm at the enhanced-dissipation rate $e^{-c\min\{μ,ν\}^{1/3}\,t},$ and the vertical velocity and magnetic components gain an additional inviscid-damping factor $\langle t\rangle^{-1}$.

</details>


### [122] [Three asymptotic regimes for a Keller--Segel system with volume-filling effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04599)
*Mingyue Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有多孔介质扩散和体积填充敏感函数 u(1-u) 的抛物-椭圆 Keller-Segel 系统的三种渐近机制，分别证明其收敛到标量非线性扩散方程、双曲-椭圆 Keller-Segel 极限以及固定时间区间上的时间无关初始斑块（在 BV 意义下）。由于敏感函数关于密度是非线性的，弱收敛不足以识别趋化通量，三种情形中均需要密度的强紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Keller-Segel 系统的渐近极限对于理解趋化现象在不同尺度下的行为至关重要。由于敏感函数对密度是非线性的，通量识别不能依赖弱收敛，须发展强紧性工具分别处理三种退化情形。

Method: 针对三种参数缩放分别采用不同的紧性论证：(1) 当 D=ε²→0 时，利用能量-熵估计建立密度的强收敛到非线性扩散方程；(2) 当 δ=ε→0 时，通过动力学约化（kinetic reduction）论证获得双曲-椭圆极限；(3) 当 χ=ε⁻¹, D=ε² 时，借助 BV 紧性证明密度收敛到时间无关的初始斑块。三种情形均需超越弱收敛以获得强紧性，从而正确识别非线性趋化通量。

Result: 在三个不同的渐近机制下均获得了密度的强收敛结果，且第一种情形中给出了显式的依赖于 m 的抛物性阈值。第三种情形在特征初值的有限周长条件下，于固定时间区间内证明了 BV(Ω;{0,1}) 收敛到时间无关的初始斑块。

Conclusion: 文章表明，尽管 Keller-Segel 系统的多孔介质扩散版本在不同缩放下呈现出从扩散主导向双曲主导再到斑块驻留的丰富渐近行为，但所有极限过程的通量识别都依赖于密度强紧性的建立，能量-熵、动力学约化与 BV 方法分别为此提供了统一的论证框架。

Abstract: We study three asymptotic regimes for a parabolic--elliptic Keller--Segel system with porous-medium diffusion and the volume-filling sensitivity function $u(1-u)$. First, when $D=ε^2\to0$, $δ=1$, $1\le m<2$, and the chemotactic sensitivity coefficient is below an explicit $m$-dependent parabolicity threshold, we prove strong convergence to a scalar nonlinear diffusion equation. Second, as $δ=ε\to0$ with $D=1$, we obtain a hyperbolic--elliptic Keller--Segel limit by means of a kinetic reduction argument. Third, under the scaling $χ=ε^{-1}$, $D=ε^2$, $δ=1$, and for characteristic initial data of finite perimeter, we prove convergence on fixed time intervals to the time-independent initial patch in $BV(Ω;\{0,1\})$. Because the sensitivity function is nonlinear in the density, weak convergence alone is insufficient to identify the chemotactic flux. Strong compactness of the density is therefore required in all three regimes and is obtained, respectively, through energy--entropy estimates, kinetic reduction, and $BV$ compactness.

</details>


### [123] [On Liouville problems for $(p,q)$-Laplacian inequalities on Finsler measure spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04780)
*Tiancheng Wang,Changwei Xiong,Dongjie Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究非紧致可逆向有限的前向测地完备Finsler测度空间上(p,q)-Laplacian椭圆与抛物微分不等式的Liouville性质。在正位势函数满足若干积分增长条件下，证明非负弱解几乎处处为零，证明基于非线性容量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Finsler测度空间上非标准增长条件的(p,q)-Laplacian型椭圆及抛物微分不等式的Liouville性质，即非负弱解在何种位势增长条件下会平凡化为零解，这是非线性分析中重要的唯一性与灭绝性课题

Method: 采用非线性容量方法(nonlinear capacity method)，结合前向测地完备非紧Finsler测度空间的几何结构、有限可逆向条件，以及环状区域上的若干组位势函数积分增长条件来建立解的整体消失结果。

Result: 在多种关于正位势函数V在环状区域上的积分增长条件集合下，证明(p,q)-Laplacian椭圆与抛物微分不等式的任何非负弱解几乎处处为零。

Conclusion: 该工作将Liouville性质的研究从Riemannian流形扩展到Finsler测度空间，推广了(p,q)-Laplacian型算子在非欧几何框架下的平凡解唯一性理论，并验证了非线性容量方法是处理此类问题的有效工具。

Abstract: We study the Liouville property for nonnegative weak solutions of the $(p,q)$-Laplacian elliptic differential inequality $$Δ^{m}_{p}u(x)+Δ^{m}_{q}u(x)+V(x)u^{s}(x)\leq 0$$ and the associated parabolic differential inequality $$\partial_t u(x,t) \geq Δ^{m}_{p}u(x,t)+Δ^{m}_{q}u(x,t)+V(x,t)u^{s}(x,t)$$ on a forward geodesically complete noncompact Finsler measure space $(M,F,m)$ with finite reversibility. Under several sets of integral growth conditions on the positive potential function over certain annular domains, we prove that any nonnegative weak solution vanishes almost everywhere. The proofs of our results are essentially based on the nonlinear capacity method.

</details>


### [124] [On smooth inviscid vortices with fat tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04786)
*Pierre Raphaël,Ayman R. Saïd*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文推导出一族具有非平凡涡旋和厚尾部的退化涡旋解，并利用它们从三维轴对称不可压缩欧拉方程中分岔出稳态解和自相似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维轴对称不可压缩欧拉方程中涡旋解的存在性与结构，特别是具有退化特性（如厚尾、非平凡涡旋）的解，以及利用这些退化涡旋作为基础来构造新的稳态和自相似解。

Method: 推导一族具有非平凡涡旋（swirls）和厚尾部（fat tails）的退化涡旋解，并基于这些退化涡旋解通过分岔（bifurcation）技术构造三维轴对称不可压缩欧拉方程的稳态解和自相似解。

Result: 成功构建了一族退化涡旋解，并证明了可以从这些解分岔出三维轴对称不可压缩欧拉方程的稳态解和自相似解。

Conclusion: 该退化涡旋族为三维轴对称欧拉方程的解结构提供了新的路径，能够用于生成更丰富的稳态和自相似涡旋解，对理解三维涡旋动力学具有重要意义。

Abstract: We derive a family of degenerate vortices with non trivial swirls and fat tails which can be used to bifurcate both stationary and self similar solutions to the three dimensional axi-symmetric incompressible Euler equations.

</details>


### [125] [Failure of analyticity-radius growth in energy-canceling fluid models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05023)
*Ke Chen,Haina Li,Quoc-Hung Nguyen,Ping Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过两个显式构造证明：精确的二次能量守恒条件本身并不决定空间解析半径的增长。在三维环面T^3上构造了保持散度自由类且满足能量恒等式的双线性算子Q，使得其对应的方程在指定时刻T解析半径回到初始值1；同时构造了投影耗散准地转方程的稀疏频率集与三角多项式初值，其解初具无限解析半径但在时刻T变为有限。两者共同说明能量恒等式不能决定频率几何从而控制解析半径增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体动力学方程（如Navier-Stokes型方程）中，能量守恒/耗散恒等式常被认为是控制解解析半径增长的核心机制。作者希望检验这一直觉是否充分，即精确的能量二次恒等式是否足以保证解析半径的单调增长或最优增长速率。通过构造反例来证明其不足，从而揭示频率几何（频谱空间结构）在解析半径演化中的真实作用。

Method: 方法分两部分：(1)在T^3上构造一个对称、平移不变、一阶的双线性算子Q，它保持无散度向量场类并精确满足能量恒等式。将该动力学限制在一个不变循环-剪切（cyclic-shear）类上，将其约化为粘性Burgers方程，并利用Cole-Hopf变换调谐热剖面，使最近复零点到实数环面的距离在时刻T回到与初始时刻相同的值。(2)对投影耗散SGE方程，构造对称稀疏频率集及其Fourier投影，选取三角多项式初值。利用加性分离的Fourier级联得到逐系数指数下界，配合一致性比较估计控制反馈相互作用，证明解在T时刻解析半径为有限（不超过1）。

Result: (1)对任意给定充分小的T>0，构造的方程∂tu−Δu=Q(u,u)存在全局光滑无散度实值均值零解u，满足rad(u(0))=rad(u(T))=1。(2)对任意1<α<2及给定充分小T>0，构造的投影耗散SGE方程存在唯一全局光滑解θ，初值解析半径为无穷，但rad(θ(T))∈(0,1]。(3)两例子联合证明：仅有精确能量恒等式不足以确定解析半径增长的频率几何规律；整函数解析性即使在三角多项式初值下也不必持续。

Conclusion: 精确的二次能量恒等式（或相消条件）本身并不强制解析半径的增长，也不足以维持整解析性。能量恒等式缺失的部分——即频率空间几何结构（稀疏性、频率耦合方式等）——才是决定解析半径演化行为（停滞、回退或增长）的真正关键因素。该结果深化了对流体方程正则性与时空结构（analyticity radius）之间关系的理解。

Abstract: We prove that exact quadratic energy cancellation alone does not force growth of the spatial analyticity radius. On $\mathbb{T}^3$, we construct an explicit symmetric, translation-invariant, first-order bilinear operator $Q$ that preserves the divergence-free class and, for every smooth real-valued divergence-free vector field $v$, satisfies $ \int_{\mathbb{T}^3} Q(v,v)\cdot v\,dx=0.$ For every prescribed sufficiently small time $T>0$, the equation $\partial_t u-Δu=Q(u,u)$ admits a global smooth, real-valued, mean-zero, divergence-free solution $u$ such that $\operatorname{rad}(u(0))=\operatorname{rad}(u(T))=1$. The construction reduces the dynamics on an invariant cyclic-shear class to viscous Burgers and tunes a Cole-Hopf heat profile so that its nearest complex zero returns to its initial distance from the real torus.
  For every $1<α<2$ and every prescribed sufficiently small $T>0$, we also construct a symmetric sparse frequency set, its associated Fourier projection, and trigonometric-polynomial initial data for the projected dissipative surface quasi-geostrophic equation. The resulting unique global smooth solution $θ$ has infinite analyticity radius initially but satisfies $0<\operatorname{rad}(θ(T))\leq1$. An additively separated Fourier cascade yields coefficientwise exponential lower bounds, while uniform comparison estimates control the feedback interactions. Thus entire analyticity need not persist even from trigonometric-polynomial data. Together, the two constructions show that an exact energy identity alone does not determine the frequency geometry governing analyticity-radius growth.

</details>


### [126] [A dispersive extension result for a class of nonlocal nonlinearities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05071)
*Bastian Hilder,Christian Kuehn*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类定义于周期Sobolev空间上的非局部非线性项可以表示为某个局部色散问题的温和解的迹，该局部问题由一组受迫薛定谔方程构成。证明基于将非局部非线性项重写为自由薛定谔算子生成的酉群作用下实解析函数的共振轨道平均。作者通过一个非局部振幅方程的等价局部重整化示例验证了该结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部非线性项出现在许多抛物型系统接近守恒Hopf失稳的振幅方程中，但其数学处理往往因非局部性而困难。若能将其等价地表示为局部色散问题的分量，则可以利用局部薛定谔系统的成熟理论与数值方法进行分析，从而弥合非局部模型与局部可解框架之间的鸿沟。

Method: 作者在周期Sobolev空间上考察一类非局部非线性项，利用自由薛定谔算子 i∂_x^2 生成的酉群，将非局部非线性重整为相应实解析函数的共振轨道平均。在此基础上，构造一个由受迫薛定谔方程构成的局部色散系统，并利用温和解的存在唯一性证明该系统的迹恰好还原原始非局部非线性项。

Result: 成功证明了一类非局部非线性可表示为受迫薛定谔方程局部色散系统的温和解的迹，并将该抽象结果应用于一个近期获得的、描述接近守恒Hopf失稳的抛物型系统动力学的非局部振幅方程，给出了其等价的局部重整化形式。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一条从非局部非线性项到局部色散系统的严格等价桥梁，表明复杂非局部结构可由有限维（或可解）局部薛定谔系统编码，为后续的数学分析与数值求解提供了新的工具与视角。

Abstract: We prove that a class of nonlocal nonlinearities on periodic Sobolev spaces can be expressed as a trace of the unique mild solution to a local dispersive problem consisting of a system of forced Schrödinger equations. The proof is based on a reformulation of the nonlocal nonlinearities as the resonant orbit average of a real-analytic function under the unitary group generated by the free Schrödinger operator $\mathrm{i} \partial_x^2$. We illustrate our result by providing an equivalent local reformulation for a recently obtained nonlocal amplitude equation that formally captures the dynamics of parabolic systems close to a conserved Hopf instability.

</details>


### [127] [Monotonicity formulas in positively curved settings with applications to two-phase free boundary problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05107)
*Johannes Hosle*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文受Alt-Caffarelli-Friedman单调性公式启发，在正曲率二维环境中提出ACF泛函的自然变体——对Green函数子水平集而非圆盘进行积分。作者在凸平面域（极点位于边界或内部）以及具有欧氏体积增长和非负高斯曲率的二维完备流形上证明了尖锐的几乎单调性公式。作为推论，利用拟共形估计，作者在流形情形给出了两相自由边界问题极小元的新的Lipschitz界，其常数仅依赖总积分曲率，不同于Teixeira和Zhang的工作中依赖黎曼曲率张量及其导数的逐点界。该方法还恢复了Gemmer-Moon-Raynor在凸平面域中到Neumann边界的Lipschitz界。

Abstract: Inspired by the monotonicity formula of Alt, Caffarelli, and Friedman, we consider a natural variant of the ACF functional in positively curved 2-dimensional settings where we integrate over sublevel sets of Green's function rather than disks. We prove sharp almost-monotonicity formulas for our new functional in the case of convex planar domains (both with the pole on the boundary and in the interior) and in the setting of 2-dimensional complete manifolds with Euclidean volume growth and nonnegative Gaussian curvature. The tools include the Schwarz-Christoffel formula and Riesz decomposition using conformal coordinates. As a consequence, using quasiconformal estimates, we give a new Lipschitz bound in the manifold setting for minimizers of the two-phase free boundary problem of Alt, Caffarelli, and Friedman, with the constant depending on only the total integral curvature, in contrast to work of Teixeira and Zhang, which gives constants depending on pointwise bounds for the Riemann curvature tensor and its derivatives. Result: (1) Fisher-KPP反应下紧支数据传播速率为a/(d+2s)；logistic情形各水平集位于exp(at/(d+2s))的常数加倍区间内，且对临界代数尾数据仍成立；(2) 一维非降front-like数据传播速率为a/(2s)，由此在所有维度排除有限速度的平面行波（加速传播）；(3) s↑1奇异极限分析表明at<τ_s时紧支解遵循Wulff形状，经过τ_s的常数倍后转为指数传播，半空间数据展示方向性局部速度到指数a/(2s)的相应转变。

Abstract: Let $P$ denote normalized nearest-neighbor averaging on $\mathbb{Z}^d$ and set $A=I-P$. We consider $\partial_tu+A^su=f(u),t>0, x\in\mathbb{Z}^d, 0<s<1.$ For a Fisher-KPP reaction with $a=f'(0)>0$, compactly supported data have logarithmic propagation rate $a/(d+2s)$. In the logistic case, each fixed level set lies between two constant multiples of $\exp(at/(d+2s))$; this localization remains valid for data with the critical algebraic tail. Nondecreasing front-like data in one dimension instead propagate at rate $a/(2s)$, and the resulting acceleration rules out planar traveling fronts of finite speed in every dimension. We also analyze the singular limit $s\uparrow1$. If $τ_s=-\log(1-s)$, then throughout $at<τ_s$ compactly supported solutions follow the Wulff shape of the local nearest-neighbor equation. After a constant multiple of $τ_s$, their propagation is exponential. Half-space data exhibit the corresponding transition from the directional local speed to the exponent $a/(2s)$. The analysis rests on sharp bounds for the subordinated kernel, algebraic barriers, a compactness--Liouville argument, and a decomposition of the fractional walk into its nearest-neighbor and rare long-jump parts.

### [129] [A weighted semigroup approach to exponential stability in linear parabolic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05173)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过构造与加权测度μ=ρdx相关的Dirichlet形式，将偏微分方程的解与次马尔可夫C₀-压缩半群建立联系，从而利用泛函分析中半群理论和Dirichlet形式的工具来分析解的指数L²-稳定性。

Result: 建立了唯一弱解的指数L²-稳定性，该结果在零阶系数为零的退化情形下依然成立，并且在漂移系数H属于L^p(U,R^d)（p∈(d,∞)）这一广泛的函数类中均稳健有效。

Conclusion: 该工作为带一般漂移项的线性抛物型方程的长时间行为提供了统一的稳定性框架，填补了零阶项消失情形下级稳定性研究的空白，推广了已有结果到更一般的系数条件。

Abstract: This paper establishes the exponential $L^2$-stability of the unique solutions to initial-boundary value problems for linear parabolic partial differential equations with general drift and zero-order coefficients in bounded domains. The key idea lies in constructing a suitable Dirichlet form with respect to a weighted measure $μ= ρ\,dx$ and identifying the corresponding sub-Markovian $C_0$-semigroup of contractions on $L^2(U, μ)$ with the unique weak solution. Remarkably, the exponential $L^2$-stability remains valid even when the zero-order term vanishes, and it holds robustly for all drift coefficients $\mathbf{H} \in L^p(U, \mathbb{R}^d)$ with $p \in (d, \infty)$.

</details>

Method: 利用螺旋对称流的特殊结构——三维流动在螺旋对称性下可约化为某种二维/有效结构——结合时间加权的Kato型函数空间来处理初始涡度仅可积而非有限能量所带来的困难，从而在超临界尺度下建立全局适定性。

Result: 证明了在$\mathbb R^2\times\mathbb T$上，初始涡度属于螺旋对称且可积函数类的Navier-Stokes方程具有全局适定性，该结果超越了经典有限能量适定性理论的适用范围。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了Navier-Stokes方程对更奇异初始数据的适定性理论边界，展示了螺旋对称性作为结构条件在克服超临界困难中的重要作用，为处理非有限能量物理流提供了一个新的理论框架。

Abstract: We show that the Navier-Stokes equations on $\mathbb R^2\times\mathbb T$ are globally well posed for initial vorticities that are both helically symmetric and integrable. This class of data typically generates velocity fields of infinite kinetic energy, so this result is not covered by the classical finite-energy well-posedness theory. This result is supercritical from the perspective of the three-dimensional Navier-Stokes scaling, and it is made possible by the special structure of helical flows using time-weighted Kato-type spaces.

</details>

Abstract: We compute the limit of the eigenvalue of the $k$-Hessian operator on the unit ball in $\mathbb{R}^n$ when $k\rightarrow \infty$, assuming that the ratio $\frac{n}{k}$ remains fixed. </details>


### [132] [Asymptotic expansion of the voltage potential under small Robin perturbations of its boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05283)

TL;DR: 本文针对反问题和形状优化中提出的疑问，推导了在边界条件发生小扰动时，模型椭圆二阶偏微分方程电压势解的渐近展开式。具体而言，将固定参考构型中的齐次Dirichlet或齐次Neumann边界条件替换为在一小部分区域ω_ε上的Robin边界条件（导纳为k_ε>0），且当ε→0时该区域消失。在两种情形下均建立了电压势的一般渐近公式，只需对消失子集ω_ε的形状和参数k_ε作极小的假设。这些展开式的缩放由新定义的数量（"Robin-Dirichlet"容量与"Robin-Neumann"容量）刻画，它们依赖于ω_ε的几何形状和k_ε的取值。作者分析了根据k_ε随ε→0的行为，这些数量与更经典的小性度量（如ω_ε的容量或Neumann容量）的比较关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源于反问题和形状优化的实际需求：需要理解边界条件在小区域内被微小扰动（从齐次Dirichlet/Neumann变为Robin型）时解的行为。这为后续反问题的唯一性识别和最优形状设计提供理论基础，且现有文献中对非齐次Robin扰动的渐近分析尚不完善。

Method: 采用渐近分析的方法，在小参数ε→0的极限下，对带有Robin边界扰动的椭圆二阶PDE解进行渐近展开。文中引入两种新的容量概念——"Robin-Dirichlet容量"和"Robin-Neumann容量"来量化扰动的尺度，并与经典容量和Neumann容量进行对比，依据k_ε的极限行为进行分类分析。

Result: 分别在Dirichlet和Neumann两种基准边界条件下建立了电压势的一般渐近展开公式，该公式仅需对ω_ε的形状和k_ε作出最小假设。给出了新的容量度量与经典容量度量在不同k_ε渐近行为下的比较结果，揭示了扰动强度随参数k_ε与区域ω_ε几何共同变化的规律。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一套统一的渐近框架来描述边界上小区域Robin扰动的效应，引入了新的容量概念作为刻画扰动强度的自然度量，并阐明了它们与经典小性度量在不同缩放情形下的联系与差异。这为反问题中边界扰动的灵敏度分析奠定了数学基础。

Abstract: Inspired by questions related to inverse problems and shape optimization, we derive an asymptotic expansion of the voltage potential, solution to a model elliptic second-order partial differential equation, under small perturbations of its boundary conditions. More precisely, the homogeneous Dirichlet or homogeneous Neumann boundary condition in a fixed, reference configuration of the problem is replaced by a Robin boundary condition with admittance $k_\varepsilon > 0$ on a ``small'' subset $ω_\varepsilon$ of the boundary of the ambient domain, vanishing at the limit $\varepsilon \to 0$. In each of these two situations, a general asymptotic formula is established for the voltage potential, which rests on minimal assumptions about the shape of the vanishing subset $ω_\varepsilon$ and the parameter $k_\varepsilon$. The scalings of these expansions, capturing the intensity of the perturbation, are measured by new quantities called ``Robin-Dirichlet'' capacity or a ``Robin-Neumann'' capacity, which depend on the geometry of $ω_\varepsilon$ and on the value of the parameter $k_\varepsilon$. We analyze how these quantities compare to more classical measures of the ``smallness'' of $ω_\varepsilon$, such as the capacity or the Neumann capacity of $ω_\varepsilon$, according to the behavior of $k_\varepsilon$ as $\varepsilon \to 0$.

</details>


### [133] [Solutions to fourth order degenerate parabolic equations obtained via weighted energy dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05293)
